`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# AGENTS.md

Guide for AI coding agents working in this repository. Humans: see README.md and CONTRIBUTING.md.

## What this is

PlayGoblin is a QA bot that plays a game and reports bugs. An engine side (Unity package, Unreal plugin, or anything that writes the run-folder format in `docs/PROTOCOL.md`) walks the game and writes `events.jsonl`. A Python CLI turns run folders into findings, a report and GitHub issues. An MCP server exposes the CLI to agents.

## Map

| Path | What |
|---|---|
| `cli/playgoblin/` | Python package (no hard dependencies). `checks/` = one pure function per check `(run, config) -> findings`; `dedupe.py`, `evidence.py`, `report.py`, `pipeline.py`, `service.py`, `issues.py`, `llm.py`, `mcp_server.py`, `writer.py` |
| `cli/tests/` | pytest; `conftest.py` has `make_run` to build fake run folders |
| `unity/` | Unity package `com.playgoblin.unity` (Runtime, Editor, Tests). Namespaces/classes start with `PlayGoblin` |
| `unreal/PlayGoblin/` | Unreal plugin (C++, experimental, not yet compiled) |
| `samples/sandbox` | Unity project that hosts the package tests |
| `samples/broken-game` | Unity level with planted bugs + clean twin. Scenes are generated by `Assets/Editor/BrokenGameBuilder.cs`; never hand-edit the `.unity` files |
| `samples/unreal-sandbox` | Unreal sample project (experimental) |
| `scripts/verify_samples.py`, `verify_unreal.py` | Walk the samples with a real engine and compare reports with planted bugs |
| `skills/playgoblin/SKILL.md`, `.claude-plugin/`, `.mcp.json` | Claude Code skill / plugin |

## Run the tests

```bash
cd cli && pip install -e ".[dev]" && python -m pytest -q        # Python, no engine needed
```

Unity (use the installed editor path; edit mode is quick, play mode takes minutes):

```bash
Unity -batchmode -nographics -projectPath samples/sandbox -runTests -testPlatform EditMode -testResults out/em.xml -logFile out/em.log
Unity -batchmode -nographics -projectPath samples/sandbox -runTests -testPlatform PlayMode -testResults out/pm.xml -logFile out/pm.log
python scripts/verify_samples.py --unity <Unity.exe> --seeds 1 2 3     # opens a window: frames need rendering
```

Unreal: needs Unreal Editor closed (Live Coding blocks the build) and a short project path (Windows 260-character limit). See `unreal/README.md`.

## Conventions and rules

- Checks are deterministic code. Do not put an LLM in a check. `llm.py` only writes an optional summary.
- Never report something without evidence (an event, frame or log line). A stuck spot seen once is "unconfirmed", not dropped and not asserted.
- `file-issues` and `playgoblin_file_issues` are dry runs unless explicitly confirmed. Keep it that way.
- Game text (error messages, button labels) goes into HTML reports: always escape it (`report.py` does).
- Event `data` values are strings. Positions are metres, Y up (`docs/PROTOCOL.md`).
- Keep the CLI free of hard dependencies; numpy/Pillow are optional extras and checks must skip cleanly without them.
- Windows consoles: stdout is reconfigured to UTF-8 in `cli._safe_console`; do not print raw emoji elsewhere.
- Unity: never hand-edit `.unity`, `.prefab`, `.asset` or move `.meta` files separately. Avoid APIs deprecated in Unity 6000.4 (see `UiText` wrappers in `MenuCrawler.cs`); the build must stay at zero warnings.
- Write tests with the real format an engine emits. A past bug: Unity writes error kind `exception` (lowercase) while fixtures used `Exception`, so real exceptions were ranked wrong for a while.

```

`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing to PlayGoblin

The most useful thing you can do right now: **run it on a game and tell us what it got wrong.** A missed bug or a false report, with the run folder (`events.jsonl`, `run.json`) attached, is worth more than a new feature.

## Set up

```bash
git clone https://github.com/grloper/playgoblin && cd playgoblin/cli
pip install -e ".[dev]"
python -m pytest -q
```

The Python side needs no game engine. Engine-side changes (Unity, Unreal) are described in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md), including how to run their tests.

## Ground rules

- Checks are plain deterministic code, one module per check in `cli/playgoblin/checks/`. A check takes a run and returns findings, so a test is a fake run folder in, findings out (`cli/tests/conftest.py`).
- Every finding needs evidence: an event, frame or log line. A signal seen once should be shown as unconfirmed, not dropped and not asserted.
- Test with the format a real engine writes (see `docs/PROTOCOL.md`), not only hand-made fixtures.
- New engine support = a writer for the run-folder format. Open an issue first so we agree on positions, time and event names.
- Keep the CLI free of hard dependencies. Keep Unity builds at zero warnings. Do not hand-edit Unity scenes; regenerate them with the sample builder.

## Pull requests

Describe what you changed and how you verified it (test output, a report, a screenshot). Small PRs get reviewed faster.

```

`README.md`:

```md
<h1 align="center">PlayGoblin</h1>

<p align="center"><b>A goblin plays your game overnight and files the bugs it breaks.</b><br>
Open-source QA bot for Unity, Unreal (experimental) and any engine that can write a log file.</p>

<p align="center"><i>Status: pre-release (0.1.0-dev). Verified on Unity 6000.4 and on two sample games; not yet tried on a real shipped game. The Unreal plugin is written but has not been compiled yet. See "Status" below.</i></p>

---

PlayGoblin walks your game like a very persistent player: it picks destinations, runs into walls, falls into holes, clicks every menu button,
and writes down everything that goes wrong. Then it turns that into a report with frames and clips, and optionally into GitHub issues.

- **Open and free.** Apache-2.0, runs on your machine, no account.
- **Deterministic first, AI second.** The checks are plain code and give the same answer every run. An LLM is optional and only writes the summary.
- **Built for AI agents.** An MCP server, a Claude Code skill, and `--json` output on every command. See [Use it from an AI agent](#use-it-from-an-ai-agent).
- **Any engine.** Unity and Unreal plugins ship here. Anything else can write the [run-folder format](docs/PROTOCOL.md) (one JSON line per event) and get the same checks and reports.
- **Repeatable.** Seeded runs, and a stuck spot only counts as confirmed once it repeats, so one unlucky run does not file a fake bug.

## What it finds

| Check | How | Evidence | Limits today |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Crashes and exceptions** | Captures every error/exception log, grouped by message | Message, count, where the player was | |
| **Dead buttons** | Clicks each active uGUI button twice on menu screens; reports ones that change nothing | Button path and scene | Unity uGUI only; runs before a player exists; never clicks Quit/Exit/Delete/Reset/Buy |
| **Stuck spots** | Walks to random reachable points; flags where the player stops. Unconfirmed until it repeats | Position, what blocked it, frames and clip | |
| **Falling through the world** | Player goes below the level or off the NavMesh | Position, frames and clip | |
| **Blank frames** | Tells a dark scene apart from nothing being drawn | The frames | Needs a rendering device (not headless) |
| **Debug leftovers** | Magenta missing-material areas; TODO/placeholder text in uGUI and TextMeshPro | Frames, UI object path | **Editor gizmos are not detected** |
| **FPS drops** | Frame time above a budget for a full second | Position, average frame time | Off unless a budget is set |

Every finding comes from an event, a frame or a log line. If PlayGoblin cannot show it, it does not report it.
It does **not** judge fun, balance, visuals or audio, and it walks randomly rather than playing to win, so "no findings" never means "bug free".

## Quickstart (Unity)

Unity 6000.4 is the version tested. Python 3.11+; `numpy` and `Pillow` for frame checks; `ffmpeg` for clips.

```bash
pip install "./cli[frames]"        # the playgoblin command
```

1. In Unity: Package Manager > Add package from disk > `unity/package.json`.
2. Add the **PlayGoblin Player** component to your player object (CharacterController, NavMeshAgent, Rigidbody or plain transform; or implement `IPlayGoblinMover` for a custom controller).
3. Press Play, then:

```bash
playgoblin run --minutes 10 --runs 3         # walks, then writes playgoblin-report/report.html, report.md, findings.json
playgoblin report path/to/PlayGoblinRuns/<run>   # rebuild a report from run folders, offline
playgoblin file-issues --repo you/your-game      # dry run: shows what would be filed
playgoblin file-issues --repo you/your-game --yes
```

If your game starts from a keyboard-only menu, or spawns the player from code, add a few lines of glue (start the game, add the component, call `PlayGoblinRun.StartRun`). The [broken-game sample test](samples/broken-game/Assets/Tests/PlayMode/SampleRunTests.cs) shows the shape.

## Unreal Engine (experimental)

[`unreal/PlayGoblin`](unreal) is a C++ plugin that writes the same run folders and serves the same local API: it finds the player pawn,
walks the NavMesh (or a sampled floor grid when there is none), detects stuck spots, falls and error logs, and captures frames.
**It has not been compiled or run yet** (the first build attempt was blocked by a running editor). Read [unreal/README.md](unreal/README.md) before relying on it.

## Any other engine or game

Write `PlayGoblinRuns/<id>/events.jsonl` and `run.json` from your engine (Godot, a custom engine, a web game, a bot on top of a QA harness),
then run `playgoblin report <folder>`. The format is small and documented in [docs/PROTOCOL.md](docs/PROTOCOL.md);
[`playgoblin/writer.py`](cli/playgoblin/writer.py) is a short reference writer you can port.

## Use it from an AI agent

```bash
playgoblin mcp          # MCP server on stdio
```

```json
{ "mcpServers": { "playgoblin": { "command": "playgoblin", "args": ["mcp"] } } }
```

| MCP tool | What it does |
|---|---|
| `playgoblin_status` | Is a game with PlayGoblin reachable, and what is it doing |
| `playgoblin_run` | Walk for N minutes (several seeds), then return the findings |
| `playgoblin_report` | Build a report from existing run folders, offline |
| `playgoblin_findings` | Read a report's findings |
| `playgoblin_file_issues` | File GitHub issues; **dry run unless `confirm: true`** |

- **Claude Code:** this repo is also a plugin (`.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `.mcp.json`, `skills/playgoblin/SKILL.md`). The skill tells the agent when to use PlayGoblin, how to read confidence, and that issues need your yes first.
- **Any agent or script:** every command takes `--json` and prints one JSON document on stdout (progress goes to stderr).
- [`llms.txt`](llms.txt) and [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md) are the index and the developer guide for coding agents.

## Does it work? (the evidence so far)

`samples/broken-game` is a small Unity level with seven planted bugs and a clean twin scene. `python scripts/verify_samples.py --unity <Unity.exe> --seeds 1 2 3 4 5`
walks both and compares the report with what was planted. Last full run: all planted bugs found in 5 of 5 seeds, no findings on the clean scene.
The sample was written by the people who wrote the checks, so treat it as a regression test, **not** as accuracy on real games. Real-game numbers are the next thing to collect.

## Status

| Part | State |
|---|---|
| Unity package | Built and tested on Unity 6000.4: 80 edit-mode tests, 22 play-mode tests |
| Python CLI, report, MCP server | 48 tests |
| Unreal plugin | Written, **not yet compiled or tested** |
| Other Unity versions (2022.3, 6000.0-6000.3), player builds, UI Toolkit | Not tested / not supported yet |
| Real games | Not yet run on one |

## Roadmap

- Compile and verify the Unreal plugin on UE 5.x, add menu crawling and a sample level.
- Run on real projects and publish precision numbers.
- Editor-gizmo check, UI Toolkit buttons, Unity 2022.3 and player builds, a GitHub Action.
- A hook so games with custom start flows need no glue code.
- Comparing runs between builds ("new stuck spot since last night").

## Contributing

Issues and PRs welcome. The most useful contribution early on is a game where PlayGoblin misses a bug or reports one that is not there.
See [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).

## License

Apache-2.0

The command line tool, report builder and MCP server for PlayGoblin, an open-source QA bot that plays your game and reports bugs.
See https://github.com/grloper/playgoblin for the Unity package, the Unreal plugin and the full documentation.

`cli/playgoblin/checks/__init__.py`:

```py
"""One module per check. Each is a pure function: (run, config) -> iterable of Finding."""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Config
from ..model import Finding, Run
from . import blank_frames, dead_buttons, exceptions, falls, fps, inputs, placeholder, prototype_look, setup, stuck, trapped

ALL = (setup, exceptions, stuck, falls, trapped, dead_buttons, placeholder, fps, blank_frames, inputs, prototype_look)


def run_checks(run: Run, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    for check in ALL:
        for f in check.check(run, config):
            f.run_id = run.run_id
            findings.append(f)
    return findings

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/blank_frames.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from .. import frames
from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """Blank or all-magenta frames. Frames close to a scene change are skipped (loading screens)."""
    if run.frames_dir is None or not run.frames_dir.is_dir() or not frames.available():
        return
    scene_changes = [e.t for e in run.events if e.type == "scene_change"]
    flagged: list[tuple[float, str, str]] = []
    for path in frames.list_frames(run.frames_dir):
        t = frames.frame_time(path)
        if t is None:
            continue
        if any(abs(t - s) <= config.blank_frame_skip_seconds for s in scene_changes):
            continue
        stats = frames.analyze(path)
        rel = path.relative_to(run.directory).as_posix()
        if stats.blank:
            flagged.append((t, "blank", rel))
        elif stats.pink_percent >= config.pink_area_percent:
            flagged.append((t, "pink", rel))

    # Consecutive flagged frames (2 s apart or less) of the same kind become one finding.
    flagged.sort()
    groups: list[list[tuple[float, str, str]]] = []
    for item in flagged:
        if groups and groups[-1][-1][1] == item[1] and item[0] - groups[-1][-1][0] <= 2.0:
            groups[-1].append(item)
        else:
            groups.append([item])
    for g in groups:
        paths = [p for _, _, p in g]
        if g[0][1] == "blank":
            yield Finding(type="blank_frame", severity="high", title="Screen rendered blank", t=g[0][0],
                          detail=f"{len(g)} frame(s) with almost no color variation", key="blank",
                          evidence=paths, data={"frames": len(g)})
        else:
            yield Finding(type="debug_leftover", severity="medium", title="Missing-material pink on screen",
                          t=g[0][0], detail=f"Magenta covers {config.pink_area_percent}%+ of {len(g)} frame(s)",
                          key="pink", evidence=paths, data={"frames": len(g)})

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/dead_buttons.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """Buttons the MenuCrawler clicked twice without seeing any change."""
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "dead_button":
            continue
        path = e.data.get("path", e.detail)
        label = e.data.get("label") or e.detail
        yield Finding(type="dead_button", severity="medium", title=f"Button does nothing: \"{label}\"", t=e.t,
                      detail=f"{path} (scene {e.data.get('scene', '?')}). Clicked twice; no UI change, scene change or log output.",
                      key="button:" + path, data=dict(e.data))

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/exceptions.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """One finding per distinct error (the Unity side already groups by message + stack hash)."""
    unreal = any(e.type == "run_start" and e.data.get("engine") == "unreal" for e in run.events) \
        or run.summary.get("engine") == "unreal"
    errors = [e for e in run.events if e.type == "error_summary" and not config.ignored(e.detail)]

    def startup(e):
        first = e.data.get("first_t")
        return e.detail.startswith("LogPython:") and float(first if first is not None else e.t) <= 1.0

    noise = []
    if unreal:
        cand = [e for e in errors if startup(e)]
        if any("/Engine/Plugins/" in e.detail for e in cand):
            noise = cand
    if noise:
        yield Finding(
            type="engine_noise", severity="low",
            title=f"{len(noise)} engine plugin startup errors (Epic Python toolsets), not game bugs",
            t=min(e.t for e in noise), detail="\n".join(e.detail for e in noise[:5]),
            key="engine_noise", occurrences=len(noise), data={"count": str(len(noise))},
        )
    skip = {id(e) for e in noise}
    for e in errors:
        if id(e) in skip:
            continue
        kind = str(e.data.get("kind", "Error")).capitalize()  # engines write "exception" / "error"
        count = int(e.data.get("count", 1))
        first = e.data.get("first_t")
        first_line = e.detail.splitlines()[0][:120] if e.detail else ""
        yield Finding(
            type="exception",
            severity="critical" if kind == "Exception" else "high",
            title=f"{kind}: {first_line}" if first_line else kind,
            t=float(first) if first is not None else e.t,
            detail=e.detail,
            position=e.pos,
            key=e.data.get("hash", e.detail),
            occurrences=count,
            data={"kind": kind, "count": count, "last_t": e.data.get("last_t")},
        )

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/falls.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding

TITLES = {
    "below_world": "Player fell out of the world",
    "invalid_position": "Player position became invalid (NaN/Infinity)",
    "off_navmesh": "Player left the NavMesh",
}


def check(run, config):
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "out_of_world":
            continue
        reason = e.data.get("reason", e.detail)
        yield Finding(type="out_of_world", severity="critical", title=TITLES.get(reason, "Player left the level"),
                      t=e.t, detail=f"{reason}; ended up at {e.data.get('fell_to', '?')}", position=e.pos,
                      data=dict(e.data))

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/fps.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """Sustained frame-time spikes reported by the Unity package (it only emits them when fpsBudgetMs is set)."""
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "fps_drop":
            continue
        ms = float(e.data.get("ms", 0) or 0)
        if config.fps_budget_ms is not None and ms < config.fps_budget_ms:
            continue
        secs = e.data.get("seconds", "?")
        yield Finding(type="fps_drop", severity="medium", title="Frame rate dropped", t=e.t,
                      detail=f"Average {ms:.0f} ms/frame for {secs} s (budget {e.data.get('budget_ms', '?')} ms)",
                      position=e.pos, key="fps", data=dict(e.data))

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/inputs.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """Dead input (the engine side found a control that does nothing) and a player that never moved."""
    seen = set()
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "dead_input" or e.detail in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(e.detail)
        kind = str(e.data.get("input", ""))
        yield Finding(
            type="dead_input",
            severity="critical" if kind == "move" else "high",
            title=e.detail or "An input does nothing",
            t=e.t, detail=e.detail, position=e.pos, key="dead_input:" + e.detail,
            data={"input": kind},
        )

    if not any(e.type == "player_found" for e in run.events):
        return
    length = max((e.t for e in run.events), default=0.0)
    pts = [e.pos for e in run.events if e.type == "pos" and e.pos and len(e.pos) >= 3]
    if length <= 20 or not pts:
        return
    xs = [p[0] for p in pts]
    zs = [p[2] for p in pts]
    spread = max(max(xs) - min(xs), max(zs) - min(zs))
    if spread < 1.0:
        yield Finding(
            type="no_movement", severity="critical", title="The player never moved",
            t=0.0, detail=f"{len(pts)} position samples over {length:.0f} s moved at most {spread:.2f} m",
            position=pts[0], key="no_movement", data={"samples": str(len(pts)), "spread_m": f"{spread:.2f}"},
        )

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/placeholder.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    """Placeholder or TODO text visible in the UI (reported by the Unity package as placeholder_text events)."""
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "placeholder_text":
            continue
        where = e.data.get("path", "")
        yield Finding(type="debug_leftover", severity="medium", title=f"Placeholder text on screen: \"{e.detail[:60]}\"",
                      t=e.t, detail=f"UI object {where}" if where else "", key="text:" + e.detail.strip().lower(),
                      data=dict(e.data))

```

`cli/playgoblin/checks/prototype_look.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding

REASONS = {
    "basic_shape": "an engine basic shape mesh (cube, sphere, ...)",
    "default_material": "the engine default or grid material",
}


def check(run, config):
    """Visible actors that still look like a prototype (reported by the Unreal plugin as prototype_look events).

    One finding per actor. The engine side caps events at 50 per run; `data.total` says how many it found,
    so the finding detail says when more exist than were reported.
    """
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "prototype_look":
            continue
        reason = e.data.get("reason", "")
        parts = [f"{k}: {e.data[k]}" for k in ("mesh", "material", "component") if e.data.get(k)]
        total = e.data.get("total", "")
        if total.isdigit() and int(total) > 50:
            parts.append(f"{total} actors look like a prototype; only the first 50 are reported")
        yield Finding(type="prototype_look", severity="medium",
                      title=f"Prototype look: {e.detail} uses {REASONS.get(reason, reason or 'placeholder art')}",
                      t=e.t, detail="; ".join(parts), position=e.pos, key="prototype:" + e.detail, data=dict(e.data))

```

def check(run, config):
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type == "setup_error":
            yield Finding(type="setup_error", severity="critical", title="PlayGoblin could not start walking",
                          t=e.t, detail=e.detail, key="setup:" + e.detail)

`cli/playgoblin/checks/stuck.py`:

from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "stuck":
            continue
        detail = e.detail
        if e.data.get("blocked_by"):
            detail += f". Blocked by: {e.data['blocked_by']}"
        if e.data.get("controller"):
            detail += f". Controller: {e.data['controller']}"
        yield Finding(type="stuck", severity="medium", title="Player got stuck", t=e.t, detail=detail,
                      position=e.pos, data=dict(e.data))

```

from __future__ import annotations

from ..model import Finding


def check(run, config):
    for e in run.events:
        if e.type != "trapped":
            continue
        d = e.data
        yield Finding(
            type="trapped", severity="high", title="Player spawns in a small enclosed area", t=e.t,
            detail=f"Can reach {d.get('reachable_cells', '?')} of {d.get('walkable_cells', '?')} walkable cells "
                   f"(cells are {d.get('cell_m', '?')} m)",
            position=e.pos, data=dict(d))

`cli/playgoblin/cli.py`:

```py
"""playgoblin run | report | file-issues | mcp"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__, issues, llm
from .client import Client, PlayGoblinError
from .config import load_config
from .model import SEVERITY_ICON
from .pipeline import analyze
from .service import run_walks


def _say(args):
    """Progress goes to stderr with --json, so stdout stays a single JSON document."""
    return (lambda s: print(s, file=sys.stderr)) if getattr(args, "json", False) else print


def _emit(findings, paths, args, extra: dict | None = None) -> None:
    if getattr(args, "json", False):
        data = json.loads(Path(paths["json"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        data["report"] = {k: str(v) for k, v in paths.items()}
        data.update(extra or {})
        print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
        return
    confirmed = [f for f in findings if f.confirmed]
    print(f"\n{len(confirmed)} finding(s), {len(findings) - len(confirmed)} unconfirmed")
    for f in findings:
        mark = "" if f.confirmed else " (unconfirmed)"
        print(f"  {SEVERITY_ICON.get(f.severity, '')} {f.data['id']} {f.title}{mark}")
    print(f"\nReport: {paths['html']}")
    print(f"        {paths['md']}")
    print(f"        {paths['json']}")


def _summarizer(args):
    return llm.summarize if getattr(args, "summary", False) else None


def _config_path(args) -> Path | None:
    if args.config:
        return Path(args.config)
    default = Path("playgoblin.toml")
    return default if default.exists() else None


def cmd_report(args) -> int:
    findings, paths = analyze([Path(d) for d in args.run_dirs], Path(args.out), load_config(_config_path(args)), _summarizer(args))
    _emit(findings, paths, args)
    return 0


def cmd_file_issues(args) -> int:
    lines: list[str] = []
    echo = lines.append if args.json else print
    code = issues.file_issues(Path(args.report_dir), args.repo, args.yes, args.label, args.include_unconfirmed, echo=echo)
    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps({"posted": args.yes, "log": lines}, indent=2))
    return code


def cmd_run(args) -> int:
    client = Client(args.host, args.port)
    say = _say(args)

    def tick(s):
        if not args.json and sys.stdout.isatty():
            print("\r" + f"  {s.get('state')} t={s.get('t', 0):.0f}s events={s.get('events', 0)}   ", end="", flush=True)

    try:
        findings, paths, _ = run_walks(client, args.minutes, args.seed, args.runs, Path(args.out), load_config(_config_path(args)),
                                       _summarizer(args), say=say, on_tick=tick)
    except PlayGoblinError as e:
        print(f"error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nstopping the run...", file=sys.stderr)
        try:
            client.stop()
        except PlayGoblinError:
            pass
        return 130
    _emit(findings, paths, args)
    return 0


def cmd_mcp(args) -> int:
    from .mcp_server import serve
    return serve()


def _safe_console() -> None:
    """Windows consoles default to a legacy codepage that cannot print the severity icons."""
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        try:
            stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
            pass


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    _safe_console()
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="playgoblin", description="A QA bot that plays your game and reports bugs. Works with Unity, Unreal "
                                                              "(experimental) and any engine that writes the run-folder format.")
    p.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=f"playgoblin {__version__}")
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    def common(sp):
        sp.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="print one JSON document on stdout (progress goes to stderr); for scripts and AI agents")

    r = sub.add_parser("run", help="start walks in a running game and write a report")
    r.add_argument("--minutes", type=float, default=5.0)
    r.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1, help="first seed; later runs use seed+1, seed+2, ...")
    r.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=1, help="number of walks; findings seen in several are merged")
    r.add_argument("--out", default="playgoblin-report")
    r.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    r.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None)
    r.add_argument("--config", default=None, help="path to playgoblin.toml")
    r.add_argument("--summary", action="store_true", help="add an LLM-written summary (setup: playgoblin/llm.py)")
    common(r)
    r.set_defaults(func=cmd_run)

    rep = sub.add_parser("report", help="build a report from run folders (works offline)")
    rep.add_argument("run_dirs", nargs="+", help="PlayGoblinRuns/<run id> folders")
    rep.add_argument("--out", default="playgoblin-report")
    rep.add_argument("--config", default=None)
    rep.add_argument("--summary", action="store_true", help="add an LLM-written summary (setup: playgoblin/llm.py)")
    common(rep)
    rep.set_defaults(func=cmd_report)

    fi = sub.add_parser("file-issues", help="file a report's findings as GitHub issues (dry run unless --yes)")
    fi.add_argument("report_dir", nargs="?", default="playgoblin-report")
    fi.add_argument("--repo", required=True, help="owner/name")
    fi.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true", help="actually create the issues")
    fi.add_argument("--label", default=None, help="label to add (must already exist in the repo)")
    fi.add_argument("--include-unconfirmed", action="store_true")
    common(fi)
    fi.set_defaults(func=cmd_file_issues)

    m = sub.add_parser("mcp", help="run the MCP server on stdio so AI agents can drive PlayGoblin")
    m.set_defaults(func=cmd_mcp)

    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    if argv[:2] == ["report", "file-issues"]:  # the spelling used in the README
        argv = argv[1:]
    args = p.parse_args(argv)
    if getattr(args, "runs", 1) < 1 or getattr(args, "minutes", 1) <= 0:
        p.error("--runs must be >= 1 and --minutes > 0")
    try:
        return args.func(args)
    except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError, RuntimeError, OSError) as e:
        print(f"error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

```

`cli/playgoblin/client.py`:

```py
"""Client for the local API the Unity package serves on 127.0.0.1 (see unity/Runtime/PlayGoblinHost.cs)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_PORT = 47321


class PlayGoblinError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class Client:
    def __init__(self, host: str = "127.0.0.1", port: int | None = None, timeout: float = 10.0):
        self.base = f"http://{host}:{port or int(os.environ.get('PLAYGOBLIN_PORT', DEFAULT_PORT))}"
        self.timeout = timeout

    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, body: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else (b"" if method == "POST" else None)
        req = urllib.request.Request(self.base + path, data=data, method=method)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:
                return json.loads(resp.read().decode() or "{}")
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            detail = e.read().decode(errors="replace")
            raise PlayGoblinError(f"{method} {path} failed with HTTP {e.code}: {detail}") from e
        except (urllib.error.URLError, ConnectionError, TimeoutError) as e:
            raise PlayGoblinError(
                f"Cannot reach PlayGoblin at {self.base}. Start the game with the PlayGoblin package or plugin (Unity: press Play; Unreal: launch with -playgoblin) "
                f"and make sure the package is installed.") from e

    def status(self) -> dict:
        return self._request("GET", "/status")

    def start(self, seed: int, minutes: float) -> dict:
        return self._request("POST", "/run/start", {"seed": seed, "minutes": minutes})

    def stop(self) -> dict:
        return self._request("POST", "/run/stop")

    def events(self, since: int = 0, max_events: int = 1000) -> dict:
        return self._request("GET", f"/events?since={since}&max={max_events}")

    def latest_run(self) -> dict:
        return self._request("GET", "/runs/latest")

    def wait_for_end(self, poll_seconds: float = 1.0, timeout: float | None = None, on_tick=None) -> dict:
        started = time.monotonic()
        while True:
            s = self.status()
            if on_tick:
                on_tick(s)
            if s.get("state") == "ended":
                return s
            if timeout is not None and time.monotonic() - started > timeout:
                raise PlayGoblinError("timed out waiting for the run to finish")
            time.sleep(poll_seconds)

    def download_run(self, status: dict, dest: Path) -> Path:
        """Return a local run directory for the run that just ended.

        If Unity wrote it somewhere this machine can read, use that. Otherwise rebuild run.json and events.jsonl
        from the API (frames are only available locally)."""
        run_dir = status.get("run_dir")
        if run_dir and (Path(run_dir) / "run.json").exists():
            return Path(run_dir)
        dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (dest / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(self.latest_run()), encoding="utf-8")
        lines, since = [], 0
        while True:
            page = self.events(since, 5000)
            if not page["events"]:
                break
            lines += [json.dumps(e) for e in page["events"]]
            since = page["next"]
        (dest / "events.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return dest

```

`cli/playgoblin/config.py`:

```py
"""playgoblin.toml: the ignore list and check settings. Every key is optional."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass
class Config:
    ignore_messages: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)   # regexes matched against error messages
    stuck_radius_m: float = 2.0
    stuck_min_occurrences: int = 2        # a stuck spot seen fewer times is shown as unconfirmed
    merge_radius_m: float = 2.0
    blank_frame_skip_seconds: float = 2.0  # ignore frames this close to a scene change
    pink_area_percent: float = 0.5
    fps_budget_ms: float | None = None

    def ignored(self, message: str) -> bool:
        return any(re.search(p, message) for p in self.ignore_messages)


def load_config(path: Path | None) -> Config:
    if path is None or not Path(path).exists():
        return Config()
    raw = tomllib.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    cfg = Config()
    checks = raw.get("checks", {})
    cfg.ignore_messages = list(raw.get("ignore", {}).get("messages", []))
    for name in ("stuck_radius_m", "stuck_min_occurrences", "merge_radius_m", "blank_frame_skip_seconds",
                 "pink_area_percent", "fps_budget_ms"):
        if name in checks:
            setattr(cfg, name, checks[name])
    for pattern in cfg.ignore_messages:
        re.compile(pattern)  # fail early on a bad regex
    return cfg

```

`cli/playgoblin/dedupe.py`:

```py
"""Merge findings across runs: same type and same key (stack hash) or same place (within a radius)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from .config import Config
from .model import SEVERITY_ORDER, Finding, distance_xz

POSITIONAL = {"stuck", "out_of_world", "trapped"}


def _same(a: Finding, b: Finding, radius: float) -> bool:
    if a.type != b.type:
        return False
    if a.type in POSITIONAL and a.position and b.position:
        return distance_xz(a.position, b.position) <= radius
    return a.key == b.key and (a.key != "" or a.title == b.title)


def merge(findings: list[Finding], runs_total: int, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    merged: list[Finding] = []
    seen_runs: list[set[str]] = []
    for f in sorted(findings, key=lambda x: (x.run_id, x.t)):
        for i, m in enumerate(merged):
            if _same(m, f, config.merge_radius_m):
                m.occurrences += f.occurrences
                seen_runs[i].add(f.run_id)
                m.evidence.extend(e for e in f.evidence if e not in m.evidence)
                break
        else:
            merged.append(f)
            seen_runs.append({f.run_id})

    for f, runs in zip(merged, seen_runs):
        f.runs_seen = len(runs)
        f.runs_total = runs_total
        f.confidence, f.confirmed = _confidence(f, config)
    merged.sort(key=lambda f: (not f.confirmed, SEVERITY_ORDER.get(f.severity, 9), -f.runs_seen, f.t))
    return merged


def _confidence(f: Finding, config: Config) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    if f.type == "stuck":
        # Only trust a stuck spot that repeats in the same place (2+ times, or in 2+ runs).
        if f.occurrences >= config.stuck_min_occurrences or f.runs_seen >= 2:
            return ("high" if f.runs_seen >= 2 else "medium"), True
        return "low", False
    if f.type in ("exception", "setup_error"):
        return "high", True
    if f.runs_total > 1 and f.runs_seen == f.runs_total:
        return "high", True
    return "medium", True

```

`cli/playgoblin/evidence.py`:

```py
"""Attach evidence to findings: copy the frames around the moment it happened and, when ffmpeg is installed,
turn them into a short MP4 clip. Everything is copied under the report directory so the report is self-contained."""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from . import frames
from .model import Finding, Run

WINDOW_SECONDS = 3.0
MAX_FRAMES = 12


def ffmpeg_path() -> str | None:
    return shutil.which("ffmpeg")


def attach(findings: list[Finding], runs: list[Run], out_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Fills finding.evidence with paths relative to out_dir. Findings are expected to have ids in finding.data['id']."""
    by_id = {r.run_id: r for r in runs}
    for f in findings:
        run = by_id.get(f.run_id)
        if run is None or run.directory is None:
            f.evidence = []
            continue
        fid = f.data["id"]
        dest = out_dir / "evidence" / fid
        picked = _frames_for(f, run)
        if not picked:
            f.evidence = []
            continue
        dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        copied: list[Path] = []
        for p in picked:
            target = dest / p.name
            shutil.copyfile(p, target)
            copied.append(target)
        evidence = [c.relative_to(out_dir).as_posix() for c in copied]
        clip = _make_clip(copied, dest / "clip.mp4")
        if clip is not None:
            evidence.insert(0, clip.relative_to(out_dir).as_posix())
        f.evidence = evidence


def _frames_for(f: Finding, run: Run) -> list[Path]:
    if run.frames_dir is None or not run.frames_dir.is_dir():
        return []
    if f.evidence:  # frame checks already know which frames proved the finding (paths relative to the run dir)
        return [run.directory / e for e in f.evidence[:MAX_FRAMES] if (run.directory / e).is_file()]
    timed = [(frames.frame_time(p), p) for p in frames.list_frames(run.frames_dir)]
    near = [(t, p) for t, p in timed if t is not None and abs(t - f.t) <= WINDOW_SECONDS]
    near.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    if len(near) > MAX_FRAMES:
        step = len(near) / MAX_FRAMES
        near = [near[int(i * step)] for i in range(MAX_FRAMES)]
    return [p for _, p in near]


def _make_clip(frame_files: list[Path], out: Path) -> Path | None:
    ffmpeg = ffmpeg_path()
    if ffmpeg is None or len(frame_files) < 3:
        return None
    listing = out.parent / "frames.txt"
    lines = []
    for p in frame_files:
        lines.append(f"file '{p.name}'")
        lines.append("duration 0.5")
    lines.append(f"file '{frame_files[-1].name}'")
    listing.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [ffmpeg, "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", listing.name,
             "-vf", "scale=trunc(iw/2)*2:trunc(ih/2)*2,format=yuv420p", "-r", "10", "-movflags", "+faststart", out.name],
            cwd=out.parent, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    finally:
        listing.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return out if result.returncode == 0 and out.exists() else None

```

`cli/playgoblin/frames.py`:

```py
"""Frame analysis: blank-frame gate and missing-material pink detection.

Frames are written by the Unity Recorder as frames/f<index>_t<seconds>.jpg or .png. Needs numpy and Pillow
(pip install playgoblin[frames]); without them the frame checks are skipped and everything else still works.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

try:
    import numpy as np
    from PIL import Image
except ImportError:  # optional dependency
    np = None
    Image = None

_NAME = re.compile(r"_t(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png)$", re.IGNORECASE)


@dataclass
class FrameStats:
    blank: bool
    pink_percent: float
    color_std: float
    distinct_colors: int


def available() -> bool:
    return np is not None


def list_frames(directory: Path) -> list[Path]:
    return sorted(p for p in Path(directory).iterdir() if _NAME.search(p.name))


def frame_time(path: Path) -> float | None:
    m = _NAME.search(path.name)
    return float(m.group(1)) if m else None


def analyze(path: Path) -> FrameStats:
    """A frame is blank when it has almost no variation: one flat color, a black or white screen, or a clear color
    with nothing drawn. A dark but textured scene stays above the threshold because of its grain and gradients."""
    with Image.open(path) as im:
        im = im.convert("RGB")
        im.thumbnail((256, 256))
        a = np.asarray(im, dtype=np.int16)
    std = float(a.std(axis=(0, 1)).mean())
    # Quantize to 4 bits per channel so JPEG noise doesn't count as variety.
    q = (a >> 4).reshape(-1, 3)
    distinct = int(len(np.unique(q[:, 0] * 256 + q[:, 1] * 16 + q[:, 2])))
    magenta = (a[..., 0] > 200) & (a[..., 1] < 70) & (a[..., 2] > 200)
    return FrameStats(blank=std < 3.0 and distinct <= 6, pink_percent=float(magenta.mean() * 100),
                      color_std=std, distinct_colors=distinct)

```

`cli/playgoblin/issues.py`:

```py
"""File findings as GitHub issues through the `gh` CLI. Dry run unless told otherwise."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

MARKER = "playgoblin-fingerprint"
POSITIONAL = ("stuck", "out_of_world", "trapped")


def fingerprint(f: dict) -> str:
    pos = f.get("position")
    where = f"{round(pos[0] / 4)},{round(pos[2] / 4)}" if f["type"] in POSITIONAL and pos else f.get("key", "")
    return hashlib.sha1(f"{f['type']}|{where}".encode()).hexdigest()[:12]


def issue_body(f: dict, report_dir: str) -> str:
    lines = [f"**{f['title']}**", ""]
    meta = [f"Severity: {f['severity']}", f"Seen: {f['runs_seen']}/{f['runs_total']} runs, {f['occurrences']}x",
            f"First at {f['t']:.1f}s", f"Confidence: {f['confidence']}"]
    if f.get("position"):
        meta.append("Where: ({:.1f}, {:.1f}, {:.1f})".format(*f["position"]))
    lines += [" · ".join(meta), ""]
    if f.get("detail"):
        lines += ["```", f["detail"].strip(), "```", ""]
    if f.get("evidence"):
        lines += [f"Evidence is in the local report ({len(f['evidence'])} file(s) under "
                  f"`{report_dir}/evidence/{f['data'].get('id', '')}`). "
                  "The GitHub CLI cannot upload attachments, so drag the clip into this issue by hand.", ""]
    lines += ["_Filed by PlayGoblin from an automated play-through._", "", f"<!-- {MARKER}:{fingerprint(f)} -->"]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _gh(args: list[str], runner=None) -> str:
    r = (runner or subprocess.run)(["gh", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8")
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"gh {' '.join(args[:2])} failed: {(r.stderr or r.stdout).strip()}")
    return r.stdout


def plan(report_dir: Path, repo: str, include_unconfirmed: bool, runner=None):
    """Returns (to_create, skipped); skipped holds (finding, reason). Existing issues are matched by fingerprint."""
    data = json.loads((report_dir / "findings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    existing = _gh(["issue", "list", "--repo", repo, "--state", "all", "--limit", "500",
                    "--search", MARKER, "--json", "number,body"], runner)
    create, skipped = [], []
    for f in data["findings"]:
        if not f["confirmed"] and not include_unconfirmed:
            skipped.append((f, "unconfirmed"))
        elif f"{MARKER}:{fingerprint(f)}" in existing:
            skipped.append((f, "already filed"))
        else:
            create.append(f)
    return create, skipped


def file_issues(report_dir: Path, repo: str, yes: bool, label: str | None = None, include_unconfirmed: bool = False,
                echo=print, runner=None) -> int:
    if not (report_dir / "findings.json").is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{report_dir} has no findings.json. Run `playgoblin report` first.")
    if runner is None and shutil.which("gh") is None:
        raise RuntimeError("The GitHub CLI (gh) is not installed or not on PATH. See https://cli.github.com")
    create, skipped = plan(report_dir, repo, include_unconfirmed, runner)
    for f, why in skipped:
        echo(f"skip   {f['data']['id']} {f['title']} ({why})")
    for f in create:
        title = f"[PlayGoblin] {f['title']}"
        if not yes:
            echo(f"would file in {repo}: {title}")
            continue
        args = ["issue", "create", "--repo", repo, "--title", title, "--body", issue_body(f, str(report_dir))]
        if label:
            args += ["--label", label]
        echo(f"filed  {_gh(args, runner).strip()}  {title}")
    if not yes and create:
        echo(f"\nDry run: {len(create)} issue(s) would be filed. Add --yes to post them.")
    return 0

```

`cli/playgoblin/llm.py`:

```py
"""Optional report summary from an LLM. Off unless asked for. Only finding titles and details are sent: no frames, no code.

Configure with environment variables:
  PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER   openai (any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, including local ones) or anthropic
  PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_API_KEY    key (not needed for most local servers)
  PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL      model name
  PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_BASE_URL   default https://api.openai.com/v1 or https://api.anthropic.com
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import urllib.request

PROMPT = ("You are a QA lead. Below are findings from an automated play-through of a Unity game. Write a summary of at "
          "most 5 sentences for the developer: what is most urgent and why. Mention only what is in the findings; do not "
          "invent causes. Findings:\n\n")


def summarize(findings: list[dict], env=os.environ, timeout: float = 60.0) -> str:
    provider = env.get("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER", "").lower()
    model = env.get("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL", "")
    if provider not in ("openai", "anthropic") or not model:
        raise RuntimeError("Set PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER (openai|anthropic) and PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL to use --summary.")
    if not findings:
        return "No findings."
    text = PROMPT + "\n".join(f"- [{f['severity']}] {f['title']} ({f['runs_seen']}/{f['runs_total']} runs): {f.get('detail', '')[:300]}"
                              for f in findings[:30])
    key = env.get("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_API_KEY", "")
    if provider == "openai":
        url = env.get("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions"
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {key}"} if key else {}
        body = {"model": model, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": text}]}
    else:
        url = env.get("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.anthropic.com").rstrip("/") + "/v1/messages"
        headers = {"x-api-key": key, "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"}
        body = {"model": model, "max_tokens": 400, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": text}]}
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), method="POST",
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", **headers})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
        data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
    if provider == "openai":
        return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
    return "".join(b.get("text", "") for b in data["content"]).strip()

```

`cli/playgoblin/mcp_server.py`:

```py
"""A Model Context Protocol server (stdio, no dependencies) so AI agents can run PlayGoblin and read its findings.

Run it with `playgoblin mcp`. Example client config:

    { "mcpServers": { "playgoblin": { "command": "playgoblin", "args": ["mcp"] } } }

Tools: playgoblin_status, playgoblin_run, playgoblin_report, playgoblin_findings, playgoblin_file_issues.
Posting GitHub issues needs confirm=true and should only be done after the person has agreed to it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__, issues
from .client import Client, PlayGoblinError
from .config import load_config
from .service import build_report, run_walks

PROTOCOL = "2024-11-05"

TOOLS = [
    {
        "name": "playgoblin_status",
        "description": "Check whether a game with the PlayGoblin package/plugin is running and reachable on the local API, and what state its current run is in.",
        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"port": {"type": "integer", "description": "Local API port, default 47321"}}},
    },
    {
        "name": "playgoblin_run",
        "description": ("Walk the open game for a number of minutes (blocks until done), then build a report and return the findings. "
                        "The game must be in Play Mode / running with the plugin. Keep minutes small (1-5) for a first check; use several runs "
                        "(different seeds) to confirm stuck spots."),
        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {
            "minutes": {"type": "number", "default": 2, "description": "Game minutes per run"},
            "runs": {"type": "integer", "default": 1}, "seed": {"type": "integer", "default": 1},
            "out": {"type": "string", "default": "playgoblin-report"}, "port": {"type": "integer"},
            "config": {"type": "string", "description": "Path to playgoblin.toml"}}},
    },
    {
        "name": "playgoblin_report",
        "description": "Build a report from existing run folders (PlayGoblinRuns/<id>). Works offline: no game, network or API key.",
        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "required": ["run_dirs"], "properties": {
            "run_dirs": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "out": {"type": "string", "default": "playgoblin-report"},
            "config": {"type": "string"}}},
    },
    {
        "name": "playgoblin_findings",
        "description": "Read the findings of an existing report folder (findings.json). Every finding has an id, type, severity, title, time, position, how often it was seen and whether it is confirmed.",
        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"report_dir": {"type": "string", "default": "playgoblin-report"},
                                                         "include_unconfirmed": {"type": "boolean", "default": True}}},
    },
    {
        "name": "playgoblin_file_issues",
        "description": ("File a report's confirmed findings as GitHub issues with the gh CLI. DRY RUN unless confirm=true; "
                        "ask the person before setting confirm=true. Skips findings that are already filed."),
        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "required": ["repo"], "properties": {
            "repo": {"type": "string", "description": "owner/name"}, "report_dir": {"type": "string", "default": "playgoblin-report"},
            "confirm": {"type": "boolean", "default": False}, "label": {"type": "string"},
            "include_unconfirmed": {"type": "boolean", "default": False}}},
    },
]


def _brief(f: dict) -> dict:
    keys = ("type", "severity", "title", "t", "position", "detail", "occurrences", "runs_seen", "runs_total", "confidence", "confirmed")
    out = {k: f.get(k) for k in keys}
    out["id"] = f.get("data", {}).get("id")
    out["evidence_files"] = len(f.get("evidence", []))
    return out


def _payload(findings, paths) -> dict:
    items = [_brief(f.to_dict()) for f in findings]
    return {"findings": items, "confirmed": sum(1 for f in findings if f.confirmed),
            "unconfirmed": sum(1 for f in findings if not f.confirmed),
            "report": {k: str(v) for k, v in paths.items()}}


def call_tool(name: str, args: dict) -> dict:
    cfg_path = Path(args["config"]) if args.get("config") else (Path("playgoblin.toml") if Path("playgoblin.toml").exists() else None)
    if name == "playgoblin_status":
        return Client(port=args.get("port")).status()
    if name == "playgoblin_run":
        findings, paths, runs = run_walks(Client(port=args.get("port")), float(args.get("minutes", 2)), int(args.get("seed", 1)),
                                          int(args.get("runs", 1)), Path(args.get("out", "playgoblin-report")), load_config(cfg_path))
        return {**_payload(findings, paths), "run_dirs": [str(r) for r in runs]}
    if name == "playgoblin_report":
        findings, paths = build_report([Path(d) for d in args["run_dirs"]], Path(args.get("out", "playgoblin-report")), load_config(cfg_path))
        return _payload(findings, paths)
    if name == "playgoblin_findings":
        data = json.loads((Path(args.get("report_dir", "playgoblin-report")) / "findings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        items = [f for f in data["findings"] if args.get("include_unconfirmed", True) or f["confirmed"]]
        return {"findings": [_brief(f) for f in items], "summary": data.get("summary"), "runs": data.get("runs")}
    if name == "playgoblin_file_issues":
        lines: list[str] = []
        issues.file_issues(Path(args.get("report_dir", "playgoblin-report")), args["repo"], bool(args.get("confirm", False)),
                           args.get("label"), bool(args.get("include_unconfirmed", False)), echo=lines.append)
        return {"posted": bool(args.get("confirm", False)), "log": lines}
    raise ValueError(f"unknown tool {name}")


def handle(msg: dict) -> dict | None:
    """One JSON-RPC message in, one response out (None for notifications)."""
    method, mid = msg.get("method"), msg.get("id")
    if mid is None:
        return None  # notifications such as notifications/initialized
    try:
        if method == "initialize":
            result = {"protocolVersion": msg.get("params", {}).get("protocolVersion", PROTOCOL),
                      "capabilities": {"tools": {}}, "serverInfo": {"name": "playgoblin", "version": __version__},
                      "instructions": "PlayGoblin walks a game and reports crashes, stuck spots, falls, dead buttons and more. "
                                      "Call playgoblin_status first, then playgoblin_run. Never post issues without the person's OK."}
        elif method == "tools/list":
            result = {"tools": TOOLS}
        elif method == "tools/call":
            p = msg.get("params", {})
            try:
                out = call_tool(p["name"], p.get("arguments") or {})
                result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": json.dumps(out, indent=2, default=str)}], "isError": False}
            except (PlayGoblinError, FileNotFoundError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, OSError) as e:
                result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"}], "isError": True}
        elif method == "ping":
            result = {}
        else:
            return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "error": {"code": -32601, "message": f"method not found: {method}"}}
    except Exception as e:  # never let one bad request kill the server
        return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "error": {"code": -32603, "message": str(e)}}
    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "result": result}


def serve(stdin=None, stdout=None) -> int:
    stdin, stdout = stdin or sys.stdin, stdout or sys.stdout
    for line in stdin:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            msg = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            stdout.write(json.dumps({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": None, "error": {"code": -32700, "message": "parse error"}}) + "\n")
            stdout.flush()
            continue
        resp = handle(msg)
        if resp is not None:
            stdout.write(json.dumps(resp) + "\n")
            stdout.flush()
    return 0

```

`cli/playgoblin/model.py`:

```py
"""Data shared by the checks, dedupe and report. Plain dataclasses so findings.json is easy to produce."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

SEVERITY_ORDER = {"critical": 0, "high": 1, "medium": 2, "low": 3}
SEVERITY_ICON = {"critical": "🔴", "high": "🟠", "medium": "🟡", "low": "⚪"}


@dataclass
class Finding:
    type: str
    severity: str
    title: str
    t: float
    detail: str = ""
    position: list[float] | None = None
    key: str = ""                       # identity for exact merges (e.g. a stack hash)
    run_id: str = ""
    evidence: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)   # paths relative to the report directory
    data: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    # Filled in by dedupe:
    occurrences: int = 1
    runs_seen: int = 1
    runs_total: int = 1
    confidence: str = "medium"          # "high" | "medium" | "low"
    confirmed: bool = True              # False: shown under "Unconfirmed", never dropped silently

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


@dataclass
class Event:
    seq: int
    t: float
    type: str
    detail: str = ""
    pos: list[float] | None = None
    data: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    @staticmethod
    def from_json(d: dict) -> "Event":
        return Event(
            seq=int(d.get("seq", 0)),
            t=float(d.get("t", 0.0)),
            type=str(d.get("type", "")),
            detail=str(d.get("detail", "") or ""),
            pos=d.get("pos"),
            data=d.get("data") or {},
        )


@dataclass
class Run:
    """One finished PlayGoblin run: its summary (run.json), its events, and where its frames live."""
    summary: dict
    events: list[Event]
    directory: Path | None = None

    @property
    def run_id(self) -> str:
        return str(self.summary.get("run_id", ""))

    @property
    def frames_dir(self) -> Path | None:
        return self.directory / "frames" if self.directory else None


def load_run(directory: Path) -> Run:
    """Load a run directory written by the Unity package (run.json + events.jsonl [+ frames/])."""
    directory = Path(directory)
    summary_path = directory / "run.json"
    events_path = directory / "events.jsonl"
    if not summary_path.exists() or not events_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{directory} is not a PlayGoblin run (needs run.json and events.jsonl)")
    summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    events: list[Event] = []
    for line in events_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            events.append(Event.from_json(json.loads(line)))
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
            continue  # a truncated last line from a crashed run must not kill the report
    return Run(summary=summary, events=events, directory=directory)


def distance_xz(a: list[float] | None, b: list[float] | None) -> float:
    if not a or not b:
        return math.inf
    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[2] - b[2])

```

`cli/playgoblin/pipeline.py`:

```py
"""Run directories in, report out. Pure offline: no Unity, no network, no API key."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from . import dedupe, evidence, report
from .checks import run_checks
from .config import Config
from .model import Finding, Run, load_run


def analyze(run_dirs: list[Path], out_dir: Path, config: Config, summarize=None) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, Path]]:
    runs: list[Run] = [load_run(d) for d in run_dirs]
    if not runs:
        raise ValueError("no runs to analyze")
    raw: list[Finding] = []
    for run in runs:
        raw += run_checks(run, config)
    findings = dedupe.merge(raw, runs_total=len(runs), config=config)
    for i, f in enumerate(findings, 1):
        f.data["id"] = f"F{i}"
    evidence.attach(findings, runs, out_dir)
    summary = summarize([f.to_dict() for f in findings]) if summarize else None
    paths = report.write_reports(findings, runs, out_dir, summary)
    return findings, paths

```

`cli/playgoblin/report.py`:

```py
"""Writes report.md, report.html and findings.json. Generated by code only: nothing here is hand-edited."""
from __future__ import annotations

import html
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__
from .model import SEVERITY_ICON, Finding, Run


def summarize_runs(runs: list[Run]) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    for r in runs:
        s = r.summary
        rows.append({
            "run_id": r.run_id, "seed": s.get("seed"), "scene": s.get("scene"), "unity": s.get("unity") or s.get("engine_version"),
            "end_reason": s.get("end_reason"), "game_seconds": s.get("game_seconds"),
            "distance_m": s.get("distance_m"), "coverage": s.get("coverage"), "mover": s.get("mover"),
            "navmesh": s.get("navmesh"), "waypoints": s.get("waypoints"),
        })
    return rows


def where(f: Finding) -> str:
    if not f.position:
        return ""
    return "({:.1f}, {:.1f}, {:.1f})".format(*f.position)


def seen(f: Finding) -> str:
    return f"{f.runs_seen}/{f.runs_total} runs" + (f", {f.occurrences}x" if f.occurrences > 1 else "")


def write_reports(findings: list[Finding], runs: list[Run], out_dir: Path, summary: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Path]:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = summarize_runs(runs)
    confirmed = [f for f in findings if f.confirmed]
    unconfirmed = [f for f in findings if not f.confirmed]

    payload = {
        "playgoblin": __version__,
        "generated_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "summary": summary,
        "runs": rows,
        "findings": [f.to_dict() for f in findings],
    }
    paths = {
        "json": out_dir / "findings.json",
        "md": out_dir / "report.md",
        "html": out_dir / "report.html",
    }
    paths["json"].write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    paths["md"].write_text(_markdown(confirmed, unconfirmed, rows, summary), encoding="utf-8")
    paths["html"].write_text(_html(confirmed, unconfirmed, rows, summary), encoding="utf-8")
    return paths


def _fmt_pct(x) -> str:
    return "?" if x is None else f"{float(x) * 100:.0f}%"


def _run_line(r: dict) -> str:
    mins = (r["game_seconds"] or 0) / 60
    return (f"seed {r['seed']}: {mins:.1f} min, {r['distance_m'] or 0:.0f} m walked, "
            f"{_fmt_pct(r['coverage'])} covered, ended: {r['end_reason']}")


def _markdown(confirmed: list[Finding], unconfirmed: list[Finding], rows: list[dict], summary: str | None = None) -> str:
    out = ["# PlayGoblin report", ""]
    out.append(f"{len(rows)} run(s), {len(confirmed)} finding(s)"
               + (f", {len(unconfirmed)} unconfirmed" if unconfirmed else "") + ".")
    out.append("")
    if summary:
        out += ["> **Summary (written by an LLM from the findings below; the findings themselves are plain code output)**",
                ">", *[f"> {l}" for l in summary.splitlines()], ""]
    for r in rows:
        out.append(f"- {_run_line(r)}")
    out.append("")
    if not confirmed and not unconfirmed:
        out.append("No findings. This is not a guarantee the game is bug free: PlayGoblin only walks and clicks "
                   "what it can reach.")
        out.append("")
    for i, f in enumerate(confirmed):
        out += _md_finding(f)
    if unconfirmed:
        out += ["## Unconfirmed", "",
                "Seen once and not repeated, so they may be noise. Rerun with another seed to confirm.", ""]
        for f in unconfirmed:
            out += _md_finding(f)
    out.append(f"_Generated by PlayGoblin {__version__}. No findings were written by hand._")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def _md_finding(f: Finding) -> list[str]:
    lines = [f"### {SEVERITY_ICON.get(f.severity, '')} {f.data['id']} · {f.title}", ""]
    meta = [f"**Seen:** {seen(f)}", f"**First at:** {f.t:.1f}s", f"**Confidence:** {f.confidence}"]
    if f.position:
        meta.append(f"**Where:** {where(f)}")
    lines += [" · ".join(meta), ""]
    if f.detail:
        lines += ["```", f.detail.strip(), "```", ""]
    for e in f.evidence:
        if e.endswith(".mp4"):
            lines.append(f"- [clip]({e})")
        else:
            lines.append(f"- ![frame]({e})")
    if f.evidence:
        lines.append("")
    return lines


CSS = """
:root{--bg:#fff;--fg:#1c1c1e;--muted:#6b6b70;--card:#f5f5f7;--line:#e2e2e6}
@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root{--bg:#16161a;--fg:#ececf0;--muted:#9a9aa3;--card:#202026;--line:#33333b}}
body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);font:16px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif}
main{max-width:860px;margin:0 auto;padding:24px 16px 64px}
h1{font-size:28px;margin:0 0 4px}.muted{color:var(--muted)}
.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;margin:14px 0}
.card h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:18px}.meta{color:var(--muted);font-size:14px}
pre{white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--line);
border-radius:6px;padding:8px;font-size:13px;overflow-x:auto}
.ev{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-top:8px}.ev img,.ev video{max-width:100%;width:260px;border-radius:6px}
"""


def _html(confirmed: list[Finding], unconfirmed: list[Finding], rows: list[dict], summary: str | None = None) -> str:
    e = html.escape
    parts = ["<!doctype html><html lang=en><meta charset=utf-8>",
             "<meta name=viewport content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'>",
             f"<title>PlayGoblin report</title><style>{CSS}</style><main>",
             "<h1>PlayGoblin report</h1>",
             f"<p class=muted>{len(rows)} run(s), {len(confirmed)} finding(s)"
             + (f", {len(unconfirmed)} unconfirmed" if unconfirmed else "") + "</p><ul>"]
    if summary:
        parts.insert(-1, f"<p><b>Summary</b> <span class=muted>(LLM-written from the findings)</span><br>{e(summary)}</p>")
    parts += [f"<li>{e(_run_line(r))}</li>" for r in rows]
    parts.append("</ul>")
    if not confirmed and not unconfirmed:
        parts.append("<p>No findings. This is not a guarantee the game is bug free.</p>")
    parts += [_html_finding(f) for f in confirmed]
    if unconfirmed:
        parts.append("<h2>Unconfirmed</h2><p class=muted>Seen once and not repeated, so they may be noise.</p>")
        parts += [_html_finding(f) for f in unconfirmed]
    parts.append(f"<p class=muted>Generated by PlayGoblin {e(__version__)}.</p></main></html>")
    return "".join(parts)


def _html_finding(f: Finding) -> str:
    e = html.escape
    meta = f"Seen {e(seen(f))} · first at {f.t:.1f}s · confidence {e(f.confidence)}"
    if f.position:
        meta += f" · at {e(where(f))}"
    body = [f"<div class=card><h3>{SEVERITY_ICON.get(f.severity, '')} {e(f.data['id'])} · {e(f.title)}</h3>",
            f"<div class=meta>{meta}</div>"]
    if f.detail:
        body.append(f"<pre>{e(f.detail.strip())}</pre>")
    if f.evidence:
        body.append("<div class=ev>")
        for ev in f.evidence:
            if ev.endswith(".mp4"):
                body.append(f"<video src='{e(ev)}' controls muted loop></video>")
            else:
                body.append(f"<a href='{e(ev)}'><img loading=lazy src='{e(ev)}' alt='frame'></a>")
        body.append("</div>")
    body.append("</div>")
    return "".join(body)

```

`cli/playgoblin/service.py`:

```py
"""The two things the CLI and the MCP server both do: walk a game, and turn run folders into a report."""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

from .client import Client
from .config import Config
from .pipeline import analyze


def run_walks(client: Client, minutes: float, seed: int, runs: int, out: Path, config: Config,
              summarize=None, say: Callable[[str], None] = lambda s: None, on_tick=None):
    """Start `runs` walks one after another in the game the client is connected to, then build the report.

    Returns (findings, report_paths, run_dirs). Raises PlayGoblinError if the game cannot be reached."""
    client.status()  # fail fast with a clear message when the game is not running
    run_dirs: list[Path] = []
    for i in range(runs):
        s = seed + i
        say(f"Run {i + 1}/{runs}: seed {s}, {minutes:g} min")
        client.start(s, minutes)
        status = client.wait_for_end(on_tick=on_tick, timeout=minutes * 60 * 4 + 120)
        say(f"  ended: {status.get('end_reason')}")
        local = client.download_run(status, out / "runs" / (status.get("run_id") or f"seed{s}"))
        kept = out / "runs" / local.name
        if local.resolve() != kept.resolve():
            shutil.copytree(local, kept, dirs_exist_ok=True)
        run_dirs.append(kept)
    findings, paths = analyze(run_dirs, out, config, summarize)
    return findings, paths, run_dirs


def build_report(run_dirs: list[Path], out: Path, config: Config, summarize=None):
    """Offline: no game, network or API key needed (unless `summarize` is given)."""
    return analyze(run_dirs, out, config, summarize)

```

`cli/playgoblin/writer.py`:

```py
"""Write a PlayGoblin run folder from any engine or tool (see docs/PROTOCOL.md).

    w = RunWriter("PlayGoblinRuns", seed=1, engine="godot", scene="res://level1.tscn")
    w.event("player_found", pos=(0, 0, 0))
    w.event("stuck", "no progress for 5s", pos=(12.0, 0.0, 3.4), t=61.5, blocked_by="Crate")
    w.end("completed", game_seconds=120, distance_m=410)

Positions are metres, Y up: [x, height, z]. Engines with Z up (Unreal, Blender) must map (x, y, z) to [x, z, y].
`playgoblin report <folder>` then turns the folder into findings, with no engine involved.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence


class RunWriter:
    def __init__(self, out_root: str | Path, seed: int = 1, engine: str = "custom", scene: str = "",
                 engine_version: str = "", run_id: str | None = None):
        started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        self.run_id = run_id or f"{started:%Y%m%d-%H%M%S}-s{seed}"
        self.directory = Path(out_root) / self.run_id
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._seed, self._engine, self._scene, self._version = seed, engine, scene, engine_version
        self._started = started
        self._t0 = time.monotonic()
        self._seq = 0
        self._counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._fh = open(self.directory / "events.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8")
        self.event("run_start", run_id=self.run_id, seed=seed, engine=engine, scene=scene)

    def event(self, type: str, detail: str | None = None, pos: Sequence[float] | None = None,
              t: float | None = None, **data) -> None:
        """Append one event. `t` is seconds since the run started (defaults to wall time since the writer was created)."""
        self._seq += 1
        e: dict = {"seq": self._seq, "t": round(time.monotonic() - self._t0 if t is None else t, 3), "type": type}
        if detail:
            e["detail"] = detail
        if pos is not None:
            e["pos"] = [round(float(x), 3) for x in pos]
        if data:
            e["data"] = {k: str(v) for k, v in data.items()}
        self._counts[type] = self._counts.get(type, 0) + 1
        self._fh.write(json.dumps(e, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        self._fh.flush()

    def error(self, message: str, count: int = 1, kind: str = "error", first_t: float = 0.0, last_t: float | None = None,
              pos: Sequence[float] | None = None, stack_hash: str | None = None) -> None:
        """Report a grouped error or exception (the exception check reads these `error_summary` events)."""
        import hashlib
        h = stack_hash or hashlib.sha1(message.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
        self.event("error_summary", message, pos=pos, hash=h, kind=kind, count=count, first_t=first_t,
                   last_t=first_t if last_t is None else last_t)

    def end(self, reason: str = "completed", **summary) -> Path:
        """Write run_end and run.json, close the file and return the run folder."""
        self.event("run_end", reason)
        self._fh.close()
        s = {"run_id": self.run_id, "seed": self._seed, "engine": self._engine, "engine_version": self._version,
             "scene": self._scene, "started_utc": self._started.isoformat(), "end_reason": reason,
             "game_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - self._t0, 3), "event_counts": self._counts}
        s.update(summary)
        (self.directory / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(s, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return self.directory

```

`cli/tests/conftest.py`:

```py
"""Helpers that build fake run directories in the same format the Unity package writes."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image


def make_run(root: Path, run_id: str, events: list[dict], frames: list[tuple[float, Image.Image]] | None = None,
             seed: int = 1) -> Path:
    d = root / run_id
    d.mkdir(parents=True)
    summary = {"run_id": run_id, "seed": seed, "scene": "Assets/Level.unity", "unity": "6000.4.0f1",
               "end_reason": "completed", "game_seconds": 300, "distance_m": 900, "coverage": 0.7,
               "mover": "CharacterController", "navmesh": False, "waypoints": 40}
    (d / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary), encoding="utf-8")
    lines = [json.dumps({"seq": i + 1, **e}) for i, e in enumerate(events)]
    (d / "events.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    if frames:
        fd = d / "frames"
        fd.mkdir()
        for i, (t, im) in enumerate(frames):
            im.save(fd / f"f{i:05d}_t{t:.2f}.png")
    return d


def textured(seed: int = 0, size=(160, 90)) -> Image.Image:
    """A game-like frame: smooth gradients plus mild noise (pure random noise would contain magenta pixels)."""
    rnd = random.Random(seed)
    w, h = size
    im = Image.new("RGB", size)
    im.putdata([(min(255, x * 255 // w + rnd.randrange(24)), min(255, y * 200 // h + rnd.randrange(24)),
                 40 + rnd.randrange(60)) for y in range(h) for x in range(w)])
    return im


def flat(color=(0, 0, 0), size=(160, 90)) -> Image.Image:
    return Image.new("RGB", size, color)


def stuck_event(t, pos, **data):
    return {"t": t, "type": "stuck", "detail": "no progress for 5s", "pos": pos,
            "data": {"blocked_by": "Wall", "controller": "flags=Sides", **data}}


def error_event(t, message, h="abc123", kind="Exception", count=3):
    return {"t": t, "type": "error_summary", "detail": message, "pos": [1, 0, 1],
            "data": {"hash": h, "kind": kind, "count": str(count), "first_t": str(t), "last_t": str(t + 5)}}


@pytest.fixture
def root(tmp_path):
    return tmp_path

```
