import io
import json

from playgoblin.config import Config
from playgoblin.mcp_server import TOOLS, handle, serve
from playgoblin.pipeline import analyze
from playgoblin.writer import RunWriter
from conftest import error_event, make_run, stuck_event
from test_pipeline import FakeUnity, fake_unity  # noqa: F401  (fixture)
from playgoblin.cli import main


# ---- --json ----

def test_report_json_is_one_parseable_document(root, capsys):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    assert main(["report", str(d), "--out", str(root / "rep"), "--json"]) == 0
    data = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert data["findings"][0]["type"] == "exception" and data["report"]["html"].endswith("report.html")


def test_run_json_keeps_progress_off_stdout(root, fake_unity, monkeypatch, capsys):
    import playgoblin.client as c
    monkeypatch.setattr(c.time, "sleep", lambda s: None)
    assert main(["run", "--minutes", "1", "--port", str(fake_unity), "--out", str(root / "rep"), "--json"]) == 0
    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert json.loads(captured.out)["findings"]
    assert "Run 1/1" in captured.err


def test_file_issues_json_dry_run(root, capsys, monkeypatch):
    from test_week3 import FakeGh, make_report
    import playgoblin.issues as issues
    rep = make_report(root)
    gh = FakeGh()
    monkeypatch.setattr(issues.subprocess, "run", gh)
    monkeypatch.setattr(issues.shutil, "which", lambda n: "gh")  # gh is faked, never called for real
    assert main(["file-issues", str(rep), "--repo", "o/r", "--json"]) == 0
    out = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert out["posted"] is False and any("would file" in l for l in out["log"])


# ---- writer: any engine can produce a run folder ----

def test_a_run_written_by_the_writer_produces_findings(root):
    w = RunWriter(root / "runs", seed=3, engine="godot", scene="res://level1.tscn", engine_version="Godot 4.3")
    w.event("player_found", pos=(0, 0, 0))
    w.event("stuck", "no progress for 5s", pos=(12.0, 0.0, 3.4), t=61.5, blocked_by="Crate")
    w.event("stuck", "no progress for 5s", pos=(12.5, 0.0, 3.0), t=90.0, blocked_by="Crate")
    w.error("Invalid call. Nonexistent function 'foo' in base 'Node'", count=40, kind="exception", first_t=70.0)
    folder = w.end("completed", game_seconds=120, distance_m=300, coverage=0.5)
    findings, _ = analyze([folder], root / "rep", Config())
    kinds = {f.type: f for f in findings}
    assert kinds["stuck"].confirmed and kinds["exception"].severity == "critical"
    assert (folder / "run.json").is_file()


# ---- MCP server ----

def rpc(method, params=None, id=1):
    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "method": method, **({"params": params} if params is not None else {})}


def test_mcp_initialize_and_list_tools():
    r = handle(rpc("initialize", {"protocolVersion": "2025-03-26"}))
    assert r["result"]["serverInfo"]["name"] == "playgoblin" and r["result"]["protocolVersion"] == "2025-03-26"
    names = {t["name"] for t in handle(rpc("tools/list"))["result"]["tools"]}
    assert names == {t["name"] for t in TOOLS} and "playgoblin_run" in names
    assert handle({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notifications/initialized"}) is None


def test_mcp_report_then_findings(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE"), stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5]), stuck_event(80, [5, 0, 5.5])])
    out = root / "rep"
    r = handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_report", "arguments": {"run_dirs": [str(d)], "out": str(out)}}))
    assert r["result"]["isError"] is False
    payload = json.loads(r["result"]["content"][0]["text"])
    assert payload["confirmed"] == 2 and payload["findings"][0]["id"] == "F1"
    r = handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_findings", "arguments": {"report_dir": str(out), "include_unconfirmed": False}}))
    assert len(json.loads(r["result"]["content"][0]["text"])["findings"]) == 2


def test_mcp_tool_errors_are_results_not_crashes(root):
    r = handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_findings", "arguments": {"report_dir": str(root / "none")}}))
    assert r["result"]["isError"] is True
    r = handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_status", "arguments": {"port": 1}}))
    assert r["result"]["isError"] is True and "Cannot reach" in r["result"]["content"][0]["text"]
    assert handle(rpc("nope"))["error"]["code"] == -32601


def test_mcp_file_issues_is_a_dry_run_unless_confirmed(root, monkeypatch):
    from test_week3 import FakeGh, make_report
    import playgoblin.issues as issues
    rep = make_report(root)
    gh = FakeGh()
    monkeypatch.setattr(issues.subprocess, "run", gh)
    monkeypatch.setattr(issues.shutil, "which", lambda n: "gh")  # gh is faked, never called for real
    handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_file_issues", "arguments": {"repo": "o/r", "report_dir": str(rep)}}))
    assert not any(c[1:3] == ["issue", "create"] for c in gh.calls)
    handle(rpc("tools/call", {"name": "playgoblin_file_issues", "arguments": {"repo": "o/r", "report_dir": str(rep), "confirm": True}}))
    assert any(c[1:3] == ["issue", "create"] for c in gh.calls)


def test_mcp_serves_newline_delimited_json_over_streams():
    stdin = io.StringIO(json.dumps(rpc("tools/list")) + "\nnot json\n" + json.dumps(rpc("ping", id=2)) + "\n")
    stdout = io.StringIO()
    serve(stdin, stdout)
    lines = [json.loads(l) for l in stdout.getvalue().splitlines()]
    assert lines[0]["result"]["tools"] and lines[1]["error"]["code"] == -32700 and lines[2]["result"] == {}

`cli/tests/test_inputs.py`:

```py
from conftest import error_event, make_run
from playgoblin.checks import inputs, exceptions
from playgoblin.config import Config
from playgoblin.model import load_run


def _run(root, events, name="r"):
    return load_run(make_run(root, name, events))


def _pos(t, x=0.0, z=0.0):
    return {"t": t, "type": "pos", "pos": [x, 1, z]}


def test_dead_input(root):
    ev = [{"t": 1, "type": "dead_input", "detail": "No keyboard key moves the player forward", "data": {"input": "move"}},
          {"t": 2, "type": "dead_input", "detail": "No keyboard key moves the player forward", "data": {"input": "move"}},
          {"t": 3, "type": "dead_input", "detail": "Moving the mouse does not turn the camera", "data": {"input": "look"}}]
    fs = list(inputs.check(_run(root, ev), Config()))
    assert [(f.type, f.severity) for f in fs] == [("dead_input", "critical"), ("dead_input", "high")]
    assert fs[0].key == "dead_input:No keyboard key moves the player forward"


def test_no_movement(root):
    ev = [{"t": 0, "type": "player_found", "pos": [0, 1, 0]}] + [_pos(t, 0.1) for t in range(1, 60, 2)]
    fs = list(inputs.check(_run(root, ev), Config()))
    assert len(fs) == 1 and fs[0].type == "no_movement" and fs[0].severity == "critical"


def test_movement_ok(root):
    ev = [{"t": 0, "type": "player_found", "pos": [0, 1, 0]}] + [_pos(t, t * 0.5) for t in range(1, 60, 2)]
    assert list(inputs.check(_run(root, ev), Config())) == []


def test_engine_noise_collapsed(root):
    start = {"t": 0, "type": "run_start", "data": {"engine": "unreal"}}
    noise = [error_event(0, 'LogPython:   File "C:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8/Engine/Plugins/Experimental/Toolsets/x.py"', h=f"h{i}") for i in range(7)]
    real = error_event(30, "NullReference in Foo", h="real")
    fs = list(exceptions.check(_run(root, [start, *noise, real]), Config()))
    assert sorted(f.type for f in fs) == ["engine_noise", "exception"]
    n = next(f for f in fs if f.type == "engine_noise")
    assert n.severity == "low" and n.title.startswith("7 engine plugin") and len(n.detail.splitlines()) == 5


def test_engine_noise_not_collapsed_for_unity(root):
    start = {"t": 0, "type": "run_start", "data": {"engine": "unity"}}
    ev = [start, error_event(0, "LogPython: /Engine/Plugins/x", h="a")]
    assert [f.type for f in exceptions.check(_run(root, ev), Config())] == ["exception"]

import json
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from playgoblin import evidence, frames
from playgoblin.checks import run_checks
from playgoblin.cli import main
from playgoblin.client import PlayGoblinError, Client
from playgoblin.config import Config, load_config
from playgoblin.model import load_run
from playgoblin.pipeline import analyze
from conftest import error_event, flat, make_run, stuck_event, textured


def findings_of(root, *dirs, config=None):
    f, _ = analyze(list(dirs), root / "out", config or Config())
    return f


def test_exception_becomes_a_critical_finding(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(12, "NullReferenceException: boom\n at Foo.Bar()")])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "exception" and f.severity == "critical" and f.occurrences == 3
    assert f.title.startswith("Exception: NullReferenceException")


def test_ignore_list_hides_matching_errors(root, tmp_path):
    cfg = tmp_path / "playgoblin.toml"
    cfg.write_text('[ignore]\nmessages = ["boom"]\n', encoding="utf-8")
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(12, "NullReferenceException: boom")])
    assert findings_of(root, d, config=load_config(cfg)) == []


def test_bad_ignore_regex_fails_early(tmp_path):
    cfg = tmp_path / "playgoblin.toml"
    cfg.write_text('[ignore]\nmessages = ["(unclosed"]\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        load_config(cfg)


def test_same_error_in_two_runs_is_merged_and_counted(root):
    a = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE", h="h1", count=2)])
    b = make_run(root, "r2", [error_event(40, "NRE", h="h1", count=5)])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, a, b)
    assert f.runs_seen == 2 and f.runs_total == 2 and f.occurrences == 7


def test_lone_stuck_is_unconfirmed_but_kept(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5])])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "stuck" and not f.confirmed and f.confidence == "low"


def test_repeated_stuck_in_same_place_is_confirmed(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5]), stuck_event(80, [5.8, 0, 5.5])])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.confirmed and f.occurrences == 2


def test_stuck_in_two_runs_is_confirmed_and_high_confidence(root):
    a = make_run(root, "r1", [stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5])])
    b = make_run(root, "r2", [stuck_event(55, [5.5, 0, 4.5])])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, a, b)
    assert f.confirmed and f.confidence == "high" and f.runs_seen == 2


def test_stuck_spots_far_apart_stay_separate(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5]), stuck_event(80, [40, 0, 40])])
    assert len(findings_of(root, d)) == 2


def test_fall_and_trapped_findings(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [
        {"t": 0, "type": "trapped", "pos": [0, 1, 0],
         "data": {"reachable_cells": "4", "walkable_cells": "900", "cell_m": "1"}},
        {"t": 50, "type": "out_of_world", "detail": "below_world", "pos": [9, 1, 9],
         "data": {"reason": "below_world", "fell_to": "9,-40,9"}},
    ])
    kinds = {f.type: f for f in findings_of(root, d)}
    assert kinds["out_of_world"].severity == "critical"
    assert kinds["trapped"].severity == "high"


def test_setup_error_is_reported(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [{"t": 0, "type": "setup_error", "detail": "No PlayGoblinPlayer found."}])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "setup_error" and f.severity == "critical"


def test_clean_run_has_no_findings_and_says_so(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [{"t": 1, "type": "pos", "pos": [0, 0, 0]}], frames=[(1.0, textured(1))])
    findings, paths = analyze([d], root / "out", Config())
    assert findings == []
    assert "No findings" in paths["md"].read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_truncated_last_event_line_does_not_break_loading(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(5, "x")])
    with open(d / "events.jsonl", "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write('{"seq": 9, "t": 3, "ty')
    assert len(load_run(d).events) == 1


def test_not_a_run_directory_is_a_clear_error(root):
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        load_run(root)


# ---- frames ----

@pytest.mark.skipif(not frames.available(), reason="needs numpy and Pillow")
class TestFrames:
    def test_black_screen_is_blank_but_dark_textured_scene_is_not(self, root):
        dark = textured(3)
        dark = dark.point(lambda v: v // 12)  # very dark but with grain
        d = make_run(root, "r1", [], frames=[(10.0, flat((0, 0, 0))), (11.0, flat((0, 0, 0))), (30.0, dark)])
        (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
        assert f.type == "blank_frame" and f.data["frames"] == 2

    def test_flat_gray_sky_screen_is_blank(self, root):
        d = make_run(root, "r1", [], frames=[(5.0, flat((128, 128, 128)))])
        assert findings_of(root, d)[0].type == "blank_frame"

    def test_blank_frames_next_to_a_scene_change_are_skipped(self, root):
        d = make_run(root, "r1", [{"t": 10, "type": "scene_change", "detail": "Level2"}],
                     frames=[(10.5, flat((0, 0, 0)))])
        assert findings_of(root, d) == []

    def test_magenta_area_is_a_debug_leftover(self, root):
        im = textured(7)
        for x in range(60):
            for y in range(40):
                im.putpixel((x, y), (255, 0, 255))
        d = make_run(root, "r1", [], frames=[(8.0, im)])
        (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
        assert f.type == "debug_leftover"

    def test_blank_frames_far_apart_merge_into_one_counted_finding(self, root):
        d = make_run(root, "r1", [], frames=[(5.0, flat()), (60.0, flat())])
        (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
        assert f.occurrences == 2 and len(f.evidence) >= 2


# ---- evidence + report ----

@pytest.mark.skipif(not frames.available(), reason="needs numpy and Pillow")
def test_findings_get_nearby_frames_and_a_clip(root):
    fr = [(float(t), textured(t)) for t in range(0, 20)]
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")], frames=fr)
    out = root / "out"
    found, paths = analyze([d], out, Config())
    (f,) = found
    pics = [e for e in f.evidence if e.endswith(".png")]
    assert 3 <= len(pics) <= 12
    assert all((out / e).is_file() for e in f.evidence)
    if evidence.ffmpeg_path():
        clip = out / f.evidence[0]
        assert clip.suffix == ".mp4" and clip.stat().st_size > 1000
    html = paths["html"].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "F1" in html and "evidence/F1" in html


def test_report_files_are_self_consistent(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE <b>x</b>"), stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5])])
    findings, paths = analyze([d], root / "out", Config())
    data = json.loads(paths["json"].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert [f["data"]["id"] for f in data["findings"]] == [f.data["id"] for f in findings]
    md = paths["md"].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "Unconfirmed" in md and "F1" in md
    html = paths["html"].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "<b>x</b>" not in html and "&lt;b&gt;x&lt;/b&gt;" in html  # game text must be escaped


def test_findings_are_ordered_by_severity_then_confirmation(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5]), stuck_event(60, [5, 0, 5]), error_event(10, "NRE")])
    findings = findings_of(root, d)
    assert [f.type for f in findings] == ["exception", "stuck"]

    assert (out / "report.html").is_file() and (out / "findings.json").is_file()
    assert "1 finding" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_cli_report_on_a_bad_path_exits_nonzero(root, capsys):
    assert main(["report", str(root / "nope"), "--out", str(root / "o")]) == 2
    assert "not a PlayGoblin run" in capsys.readouterr().err


class FakeUnity(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    run_dir = None
    polls = 0

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _send(self, code, obj):
        body = json.dumps(obj).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path == "/status":
            FakeUnity.polls += 1
            ended = FakeUnity.polls >= 3
            self._send(200, {"state": "ended" if ended else "running", "run_id": "r1", "t": 5, "events": 3,
                             "end_reason": "completed", "run_dir": str(FakeUnity.run_dir)})
        else:
            self._send(404, {"error": "not_found"})

    def do_POST(self):
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        if self.path == "/run/start":
            self._send(202 if body.get("minutes", 0) > 0 else 400, {"state": "running"})
        else:
            self._send(404, {"error": "not_found"})


@pytest.fixture
def fake_unity(root):
    FakeUnity.run_dir = make_run(root / "unity", "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    FakeUnity.polls = 0
    srv = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), FakeUnity)
    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    yield srv.server_address[1]
    srv.shutdown()


def test_cli_run_talks_to_the_api_and_writes_a_report(root, fake_unity, monkeypatch):
    import playgoblin.client as c
    monkeypatch.setattr(c.time, "sleep", lambda s: None)
    out = root / "rep"
    assert main(["run", "--minutes", "1", "--port", str(fake_unity), "--out", str(out)]) == 0
    assert (out / "runs" / "r1" / "events.jsonl").is_file()
    assert json.loads((out / "findings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["findings"]


def test_client_explains_when_unity_is_not_running():
    with pytest.raises(PlayGoblinError, match="Cannot reach PlayGoblin"):
        Client(port=1, timeout=0.5).status()


def test_cli_run_without_unity_exits_nonzero(root, capsys):
    assert main(["run", "--port", "1", "--out", str(root / "o")]) == 2
    assert "Cannot reach PlayGoblin" in capsys.readouterr().err


def test_summary_prints_on_a_legacy_codepage_console(root, monkeypatch):
    import io
    import sys
    raw = io.BytesIO()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "stdout", io.TextIOWrapper(raw, encoding="cp1252", errors="strict"))
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    assert main(["report", str(d), "--out", str(root / "rep")]) == 0
    sys.stdout.flush()
    assert "F1" in raw.getvalue().decode("utf-8")

`cli/tests/test_prototype_look.py`:

```py
from conftest import make_run
from playgoblin.checks import prototype_look, run_checks
from playgoblin.config import Config
from playgoblin.model import load_run


def _ev(name, reason="default_material", total="2", **kw):
    return {"t": 1, "type": "prototype_look", "detail": name, "pos": [1, 0, 2],
            "data": {"reason": reason, "mesh": "/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube", "material": "/Engine/EngineMaterials/WorldGridMaterial.WorldGridMaterial",
                     "component": "StaticMeshComponent0", "total": total, **kw}}


def test_one_finding_per_actor(root):
    run = load_run(make_run(root, "r", [_ev("Floor"), _ev("Wall", reason="basic_shape")]))
    fs = list(prototype_look.check(run, Config()))
    assert [(f.type, f.severity, f.key) for f in fs] == [("prototype_look", "medium", "prototype:Floor"), ("prototype_look", "medium", "prototype:Wall")]
    assert fs[0].title == "Prototype look: Floor uses the engine default or grid material"
    assert "basic shape" in fs[1].title and fs[1].position == [1, 0, 2]
    assert "WorldGridMaterial" in fs[0].detail


def test_unknown_reason_not_dropped(root):
    fs = list(prototype_look.check(load_run(make_run(root, "r", [_ev("X", reason="weird")])), Config()))
    assert len(fs) == 1 and "weird" in fs[0].title


def test_cap_is_reported(root):
    fs = list(prototype_look.check(load_run(make_run(root, "r", [_ev("A", total="120")])), Config()))
    assert "120 actors" in fs[0].detail


def test_registered_and_clean_run(root):
    run = load_run(make_run(root, "r", [_ev("A")]))
    assert [f.type for f in run_checks(run, Config())] == ["prototype_look"]
    assert list(prototype_look.check(load_run(make_run(root, "c", [{"t": 0, "type": "player_found", "pos": [0, 0, 0]}])), Config())) == []

`cli/tests/test_week3.py`:

```py
import json
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from playgoblin import llm
from playgoblin.cli import main
from playgoblin.config import Config
from playgoblin.issues import MARKER, fingerprint
from playgoblin.pipeline import analyze
from conftest import error_event, make_run, stuck_event


def fps_event(t, ms=80, seconds=2):
    return {"t": t, "type": "fps_drop", "pos": [3, 0, 3],
            "data": {"ms": str(ms), "seconds": str(seconds), "budget_ms": "33"}}


def findings_of(root, *dirs, config=None):
    return analyze(list(dirs), root / "out", config or Config())[0]


def test_fps_drops_become_one_finding_and_respect_a_stricter_budget(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [fps_event(20), fps_event(40, ms=50)])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "fps_drop" and f.occurrences == 2
    assert findings_of(root, d, config=Config(fps_budget_ms=100)) == []


def test_placeholder_text_is_a_debug_leftover(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [{"t": 5, "type": "placeholder_text", "detail": "TODO: real title",
                               "data": {"path": "Canvas/Menu/Title"}}])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "debug_leftover" and "Canvas/Menu/Title" in f.detail


def test_dead_button_finding(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [{"t": 9, "type": "dead_button", "detail": "Options",
                               "data": {"path": "Canvas/Options", "label": "Options", "scene": "Menu"}}])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.type == "dead_button" and f.confirmed and "Options" in f.title


# ---- file-issues ----

class FakeGh:
    def __init__(self, existing=""):
        self.calls, self.existing = [], existing

    def __call__(self, cmd, **kw):
        self.calls.append(cmd)
        out = self.existing if cmd[1:3] == ["issue", "list"] else "https://github.com/o/r/issues/7\n"
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout=out, stderr="")


def make_report(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE", h="h1"), stuck_event(30, [5, 0, 5])])
    analyze([d], root / "rep", Config())
    return root / "rep"


def test_file_issues_is_a_dry_run_by_default(root):
    from playgoblin.issues import file_issues
    gh, out = FakeGh(), []
    file_issues(make_report(root), "o/r", yes=False, echo=out.append, runner=gh)
    assert not any(c[1:3] == ["issue", "create"] for c in gh.calls)
    assert any("would file" in l for l in out) and any("Dry run" in l for l in out)
    assert any("unconfirmed" in l for l in out)  # the lone stuck spot is not filed


def test_file_issues_with_yes_creates_issues_with_a_fingerprint(root):
    from playgoblin.issues import file_issues
    gh = FakeGh()
    file_issues(make_report(root), "o/r", yes=True, label="playgoblin", echo=lambda s: None, runner=gh)
    (create,) = [c for c in gh.calls if c[1:3] == ["issue", "create"]]
    body = create[create.index("--body") + 1]
    assert MARKER in body and "--label" in create and create[create.index("--title") + 1].startswith("[PlayGoblin]")


def test_file_issues_skips_findings_that_are_already_filed(root):
    from playgoblin.issues import file_issues
    rep = make_report(root)
    f = json.loads((rep / "findings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["findings"][0]
    gh, out = FakeGh(existing=f"[{{'body': '{MARKER}:{fingerprint(f)}'}}]"), []
    file_issues(rep, "o/r", yes=True, echo=out.append, runner=gh)
    assert not any(c[1:3] == ["issue", "create"] for c in gh.calls)
    assert any("already filed" in l for l in out)


def test_file_issues_without_a_report_is_a_clear_error(root, capsys):
    assert main(["report", "file-issues", str(root / "nope"), "--repo", "o/r"]) == 2
    assert "findings.json" in capsys.readouterr().err


# ---- LLM summary ----

class FakeLlm(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    seen = []

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
        FakeLlm.seen.append((self.path, dict(self.headers), body))
        payload = ({"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "claude says <b>fix NRE</b>"}]} if "messages" in self.path and "v1/messages" in self.path
                   else {"choices": [{"message": {"content": "openai says fix NRE"}}]})
        raw = json.dumps(payload).encode()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(raw)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(raw)


@pytest.fixture
def llm_server():
    FakeLlm.seen = []
    srv = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), FakeLlm)
    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{srv.server_address[1]}"
    srv.shutdown()


FINDINGS = [{"severity": "critical", "title": "Exception: NRE", "runs_seen": 1, "runs_total": 1, "detail": "boom"}]


def test_summary_openai_compatible(llm_server):
    env = {"PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER": "openai", "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL": "m", "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_BASE_URL": llm_server}
    assert llm.summarize(FINDINGS, env) == "openai says fix NRE"
    path, headers, body = FakeLlm.seen[0]
    assert path == "/chat/completions" and "Exception: NRE" in body["messages"][0]["content"]
    assert "Authorization" not in headers


def test_summary_anthropic_and_report_escapes_it(root, llm_server, monkeypatch):
    env = {"PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER": "anthropic", "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL": "m", "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_API_KEY": "k",
           "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_BASE_URL": llm_server}
    for k, v in env.items():
        monkeypatch.setenv(k, v)
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    assert main(["report", str(d), "--out", str(root / "rep"), "--summary"]) == 0
    assert {k.lower(): v for k, v in FakeLlm.seen[0][1].items()}["x-api-key"] == "k"
    html = (root / "rep" / "report.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "&lt;b&gt;fix NRE&lt;/b&gt;" in html and "<b>fix NRE</b>" not in html
    assert "claude says" in (root / "rep" / "report.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_summary_without_configuration_explains_itself(root, monkeypatch, capsys):
    for k in ("PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER", "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_MODEL"):
        monkeypatch.delenv(k, raising=False)
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    assert main(["report", str(d), "--out", str(root / "rep"), "--summary"]) == 2
    assert "PLAYGOBLIN_LLM_PROVIDER" in capsys.readouterr().err


def test_no_summary_means_no_network(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NRE")])
    assert main(["report", str(d), "--out", str(root / "rep")]) == 0
    assert json.loads((root / "rep" / "findings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["summary"] is None


def test_lowercase_exception_kind_from_the_engines_is_critical(root):
    d = make_run(root, "r1", [error_event(10, "NullReferenceException: boom", kind="exception")])
    (f,) = findings_of(root, d)
    assert f.severity == "critical" and f.title.startswith("Exception: ")

```

`docs/PROTOCOL.md`:

```md
# The PlayGoblin run folder

PlayGoblin does not care what engine a game uses. Anything that writes this folder gets the same checks, report,
deduplication across runs, GitHub issues and AI tooling. The Unity package and the Unreal plugin both write it;
`playgoblin.writer.RunWriter` (Python) is a reference writer you can port to GDScript, C#, Lua or anything else.

```
PlayGoblinRuns/<run_id>/
  events.jsonl      one JSON object per line, in order
  run.json          summary, written when the run ends
  frames/           optional screenshots: f<index>_t<seconds>.png or .jpg
```

Build a report from it with `playgoblin report PlayGoblinRuns/<run_id>`. No engine, network or API key is involved.

## Coordinates and time

- Positions are **metres, Y up**: `[x, height, z]`. Z-up engines (Unreal, Blender) write `[x, z, y]`. Unreal also converts centimetres to metres.
- `t` is **game seconds since the run started**. Frame filenames use the same clock, so `f00012_t6.50.png` belongs to events near `t = 6.5`.

## events.jsonl

Every line: `{"seq": 1, "t": 0.0, "type": "...", "detail": "optional text", "pos": [x, y, z], "data": {"key": "string value"}}`.
`seq` counts up from 1. `detail`, `pos` and `data` are optional. **All `data` values are strings.**

| type | Meaning | Fields the checks read |
|---|---|---|
| `run_start` | First event | `data`: run_id, seed, engine, scene |
| `player_found` | The bot controls a player | `pos`; `data`: mover, navmesh |
| `pos` | Position sample (about 2 per second) | `pos` |
| `waypoint` / `waypoint_timeout` | Reached or gave up on a destination | `pos` |
| `stuck` | The player tried to move and made no progress | `pos`, `detail`; `data`: blocked_by, controller |
| `out_of_world` | The player fell below the level or left the walkable area | `pos`; `data`: reason, fell_to |
| `respawn` | The bot put the player back | `pos`; `data`: from |
| `trapped` | The start area is a closed pocket | `pos`; `data`: reachable_cells, walkable_cells, cell_m |
| `error_summary` | One group of identical errors, written at the end | `detail` = message; `data`: **hash**, **kind** (`exception` or `error`), **count**, first_t, last_t; `pos` |
| `dead_button` | A UI button that did nothing when clicked twice | `detail` = label; `data`: path, label, scene |
| `placeholder_text` | TODO/lorem-style text visible in the UI | `detail` = text; `data`: path |
| `menu_skip` | A button the menu crawler did not click | `detail` = label (or "click limit"); `data`: path, reason (`quit_or_exit`, `max_clicks`). Unreal only for now; not turned into a finding |
| `prototype_look` | A visible actor still looks like a prototype: engine default/grid material, or a mesh from `/Engine/BasicShapes/` (one event per actor, at most 50 per run, written right after `player_found`) | `detail` = actor label; `pos`; `data`: reason (`basic_shape` or `default_material`), mesh, material, component, total (all offending actors, may exceed 50) |
| `input_map` | What the engine side found bound to input (info) | `data`: forward_keys, action_keys, jump_key, mouse_look (`true`/`false`), axes, enhanced_input |
| `dead_input` | A control does nothing (e.g. no key moves the player forward) | `detail`; `data`: input (`move` or `look`) |
| `input_press` | A key the bot pressed (info only) | `detail` = key name |
| `fps_drop` | Frame time above budget for a second or more | `pos`; `data`: ms, seconds, budget_ms |
| `scene_change` | A level or scene loaded (blank frames next to it are ignored) | `detail` = scene |
| `setup_error` | PlayGoblin could not start (no player found, ...) | `detail` |
| `run_end` | Last event | `detail` = reason |

Unknown event types are kept and ignored, so you can add your own (`PlayGoblinEvents.Log`-style game events are fine).
Group identical errors yourself before writing `error_summary`: the same `hash` in several runs becomes one finding.

## run.json

```json
{ "run_id": "20261102-153000-s1", "seed": 1, "engine": "unity", "engine_version": "6000.4.0f1", "scene": "Assets/Level.unity",
  "end_reason": "completed", "game_seconds": 300, "distance_m": 900, "coverage": 0.7, "mover": "CharacterController",
  "navmesh": false, "waypoints": 40, "event_counts": {"stuck": 2} }
```

Only `run_id` is needed; the rest shows up in the report header when present.

## What becomes a finding

| Finding | Source | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Exception / error | `error_summary` | critical when `kind` is `exception`, else high |
| Stuck spot | `stuck` | merged within 2 m; **unconfirmed** until it repeats (2+ times or in 2+ runs) |
| Fell out of the world | `out_of_world` | critical |
| Closed start area | `trapped` | high |
| Dead button | `dead_button` | medium |
| Dead input | `dead_input` | critical for `move`, high for `look` |
| Player never moved | `player_found` + `pos` | critical; run over 20 s and under 1 m of spread |
| Engine startup noise | `error_summary` | low; Unreal `LogPython:` lines at t<=1 s from `/Engine/Plugins/`, collapsed into one finding |
| Placeholder text, magenta (missing material) frames | `placeholder_text`, `frames/` | medium |
| Prototype look | `prototype_look` | medium; one finding per actor (key `prototype:<actor>`); Unreal plugin only for now |
| Frame rate drop | `fps_drop` | medium; `fps_budget_ms` in `playgoblin.toml` can filter |
| Blank screen | `frames/` | high; needs numpy and Pillow |

## The local API (optional)

Engines that want `playgoblin run` to drive them serve this on `127.0.0.1:47321` (`PLAYGOBLIN_PORT`): `GET /status`,
`POST /run/start` (`{"seed": 1, "minutes": 5}`), `POST /run/stop`, `GET /events?since=N&max=M` (returns `{"events": [], "next": N}`),
`GET /runs/latest`. `/status` returns `state` (`idle|starting|running|ended`), `run_id`, `run_dir`, `end_reason`, `t`, `events`.
Without it you can still run your own walk and call `playgoblin report` afterwards.


```

`llms.txt`:

```txt
# PlayGoblin

> An open-source QA bot that plays a game unattended (Unity, Unreal, or any engine that writes its run folder) and reports crashes, stuck spots, falls through the world, dead buttons, placeholder text, missing-material pink, blank screens and FPS drops, with evidence. Apache-2.0. The checks are plain deterministic code; an LLM is optional and only writes a summary.

## Use it from an AI agent
- [SKILL.md](skills/playgoblin/SKILL.md): when and how to use PlayGoblin, step by step
- [mcp_server.py](cli/playgoblin/mcp_server.py): the MCP server (`playgoblin mcp`, stdio, no dependencies); tools: playgoblin_status, playgoblin_run, playgoblin_report, playgoblin_findings, playgoblin_file_issues
- CLI with machine-readable output: `playgoblin run|report|file-issues ... --json`

## Integrate a game or engine
- [PROTOCOL.md](docs/PROTOCOL.md): the run-folder format (events.jsonl, run.json, frames) and the optional local API
- [writer.py](cli/playgoblin/writer.py): reference writer in Python
- [unity/](unity): Unity package (verified on Unity 6000.4)
- [unreal/](unreal): Unreal plugin (experimental, see unreal/README.md for its status)

## Develop
- [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md): repo map, how to run every test, conventions
- [samples/broken-game](samples/broken-game): Unity level with planted bugs and a clean twin; `scripts/verify_samples.py` checks recall and false positives

```

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Editor/BrokenGameBuilder.cs`:

```cs
using System.IO;
using PlayGoblin;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Events;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace BrokenGame.Editor
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Generates the two sample scenes: Broken (seven planted bugs) and Clean (same level, no bugs).
    /// Run: Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath samples/broken-game -executeMethod BrokenGame.Editor.BrokenGameBuilder.BuildAll
    /// </summary>
    public static class BrokenGameBuilder
    {
        const float Half = 30f;
        // The trapdoor tile sits in a hole of the floor, close to where the player starts.
        static readonly Rect Hole = new Rect(-12, -12, 8, 8);

        [MenuItem("PlayGoblin/Build broken-game scenes")]
        public static void BuildAll()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Scenes");
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Materials");
            Build("Broken", broken: true);
            Build("Clean", broken: false);
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = new[]
            {
                new EditorBuildSettingsScene("Assets/Scenes/Broken.unity", true),
                new EditorBuildSettingsScene("Assets/Scenes/Clean.unity", true)
            };
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
        }

        static void Build(string name, bool broken)
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);

            var light = new GameObject("Directional Light").AddComponent<Light>();
            light.type = LightType.Directional;
            light.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(55, 30, 0);

            var camGo = new GameObject("Main Camera") { tag = "MainCamera" };
            camGo.AddComponent<Camera>();
            camGo.transform.position = new Vector3(0, 52, 0);
            camGo.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(90, 0, 0);
            if (broken) camGo.AddComponent<CameraBlackout>();

            var floorMat = Mat("Floor", new Color(0.35f, 0.5f, 0.35f));
            Slab("Floor West", -Half, Hole.xMin, -Half, Half, floorMat);
            Slab("Floor East", Hole.xMax, Half, -Half, Half, floorMat);
            Slab("Floor South", Hole.xMin, Hole.xMax, -Half, Hole.yMin, floorMat);
            Slab("Floor North", Hole.xMin, Hole.xMax, Hole.yMax, Half, floorMat);

            var trap = Slab("Trapdoor", Hole.xMin, Hole.xMax, Hole.yMin, Hole.yMax, Mat("Trapdoor", new Color(0.55f, 0.45f, 0.3f)));
            if (broken) trap.AddComponent<Trapdoor>();

            // A long step across the level. The walk grid allows steps up to 0.6 m but the player controller climbs only 0.3 m, so the 0.5 m step traps it; the 0.2 m kerb in the clean scene is fine.
            var step = broken ? 0.5f : 0.2f;
            var ledge = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            ledge.name = "Ledge";
            ledge.transform.position = new Vector3(12.5f, step / 2f, 0);
            ledge.transform.localScale = new Vector3(35, step, 2);
            ledge.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = Mat("Ledge", new Color(0.6f, 0.6f, 0.65f));

            var decal = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            decal.name = broken ? "Missing Material Decal" : "Floor Decal";
            decal.transform.position = new Vector3(-18, 0.05f, 15);
            decal.transform.localScale = new Vector3(10, 0.1f, 10);
            decal.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = broken ? Missing() : Mat("Decal", new Color(0.3f, 0.35f, 0.7f));

            var player = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            player.name = "Player";
            Object.DestroyImmediate(player.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            var cc = player.AddComponent<CharacterController>();
            cc.stepOffset = 0.3f;
            player.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>().moveSpeed = 5f;
            player.transform.position = new Vector3(-20, 1.1f, -20);
            player.SetActive(false); // the Play button of the main menu turns the player on

            // Menu: Options, Play, Quit. Sorted by path, PlayGoblin meets Options first.
            var canvasGo = new GameObject("Canvas", typeof(Canvas), typeof(CanvasScaler), typeof(GraphicRaycaster));
            canvasGo.GetComponent<Canvas>().renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
            var menu = new GameObject("Menu", typeof(RectTransform));
            menu.transform.SetParent(canvasGo.transform, false);
            var options = new GameObject("OptionsPanel", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
            options.transform.SetParent(canvasGo.transform, false);
            options.SetActive(false);

            var ctl = canvasGo.AddComponent<MenuController>();
            ctl.menu = menu; ctl.options = options; ctl.player = player;

            Label(menu.transform, "Title", broken ? "TODO: game title" : "Garden Walk", new Vector2(0, 160), 36);
            var optionsBtn = Button(menu.transform, "Options", new Vector2(0, 60));
            var playBtn = Button(menu.transform, "Play", new Vector2(0, 0));
            var quitBtn = Button(menu.transform, "Quit", new Vector2(0, -60));
            if (!broken) UnityEventTools.AddPersistentListener(optionsBtn.onClick, ctl.ShowOptions);
            UnityEventTools.AddPersistentListener(playBtn.onClick, ctl.Play);
            UnityEventTools.AddPersistentListener(quitBtn.onClick, ctl.Quit);

            var hud = new GameObject("DeathHud", typeof(RectTransform));
            hud.transform.SetParent(canvasGo.transform, false);
            var hudLabel = Label(hud.transform, "HudLabel", "", new Vector2(0, -200), 20);
            var hudScript = hud.AddComponent<DeathHud>();
            if (!broken) hudScript.label = hudLabel;
            if (broken) new GameObject("Frame Stutter").AddComponent<FrameStutter>();

            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, "Assets/Scenes/" + name + ".unity");
        }

        static GameObject Slab(string name, float x0, float x1, float z0, float z1, Material m)
        {
            var go = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            go.name = name;
            go.transform.position = new Vector3((x0 + x1) / 2f, -0.5f, (z0 + z1) / 2f);
            go.transform.localScale = new Vector3(x1 - x0, 1f, z1 - z0);
            go.GetComponent<Renderer>().sharedMaterial = m;
            return go;
        }

        static Material Mat(string name, Color c)
        {
            var path = "Assets/Materials/" + name + ".mat";
            var m = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Material>(path);
            if (m != null) return m;
            m = new Material(Shader.Find("Standard") ?? Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit")) { color = c };
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(m, path);
            return m;
        }

        /// <summary>A material whose shader failed to compile renders as flat magenta. Reproduce that look.</summary>
        static Material Missing()
        {
            const string path = "Assets/Materials/Missing.mat";
            var m = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Material>(path);
            if (m != null) return m;
            m = new Material(Shader.Find("Hidden/InternalErrorShader"));
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(m, path);
            return m;
        }

        static Text Label(Transform parent, string name, string text, Vector2 at, int size)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Text));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            ((RectTransform)go.transform).anchoredPosition = at;
            ((RectTransform)go.transform).sizeDelta = new Vector2(400, 50);
            var t = go.GetComponent<Text>();
            t.text = text;
            t.fontSize = size;
            t.alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
            t.font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
            return t;
        }

        static Button Button(Transform parent, string label, Vector2 at)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(label, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image), typeof(Button));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            ((RectTransform)go.transform).anchoredPosition = at;
            ((RectTransform)go.transform).sizeDelta = new Vector2(200, 40);
            var t = Label(go.transform, "Label", label, Vector2.zero, 20);
            t.color = Color.black;
            return go.GetComponent<Button>();
        }
    }
}

  m_FogColor: {r: 0.5, g: 0.5, b: 0.5, a: 1}
  m_FogMode: 3
  m_FogDensity: 0.01
  m_LinearFogStart: 0
  m_LinearFogEnd: 300
  m_AmbientSkyColor: {r: 0.212, g: 0.227, b: 0.259, a: 1}
  m_AmbientEquatorColor: {r: 0.114, g: 0.125, b: 0.133, a: 1}
  m_AmbientGroundColor: {r: 0.047, g: 0.043, b: 0.035, a: 1}
  m_AmbientIntensity: 1
  m_AmbientMode: 0
  m_SubtractiveShadowColor: {r: 0.42, g: 0.478, b: 0.627, a: 1}

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Scripts/CameraBlackout.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;

namespace BrokenGame
{
    /// <summary>PLANTED BUG: the game camera renders nothing for a few seconds (black screen).</summary>
    [RequireComponent(typeof(Camera))]
    public class CameraBlackout : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public float startAt = 25f, seconds = 8f;
        int _mask;

        void Start() => _mask = GetComponent<Camera>().cullingMask;

        void Update()
        {
            var cam = GetComponent<Camera>();
            var on = Time.timeSinceLevelLoad > startAt && Time.timeSinceLevelLoad < startAt + seconds;
            cam.clearFlags = on ? CameraClearFlags.SolidColor : CameraClearFlags.Skybox;
            cam.backgroundColor = Color.black;
            cam.cullingMask = on ? 0 : _mask;
        }
    }
}

```

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Scripts/DeathHud.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace BrokenGame
{
    /// <summary>PLANTED BUG: after the player has fallen once, the HUD label is read every frame but was never assigned.</summary>
    public class DeathHud : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public Text label;
        bool _hasDied;

        void Update()
        {
            var cc = FindAnyObjectByType<CharacterController>();
            if (cc != null && cc.transform.position.y < -2f) _hasDied = true;
            if (_hasDied) label.text = "Respawned"; // NullReferenceException every frame when label is unassigned
        }
    }
}

```

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Scripts/FrameStutter.cs`:

```cs
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;

namespace BrokenGame
{
    /// <summary>PLANTED BUG: a section of the game runs at about 15 frames per second.</summary>
    public class FrameStutter : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public float startAt = 45f, seconds = 40f;
        public int sleepMs = 60;

        void Update()
        {
            var t = Time.timeSinceLevelLoad;
            if (t > startAt && t < startAt + seconds) Thread.Sleep(sleepMs);
        }
    }
}

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Scripts/Trapdoor.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;

namespace BrokenGame
{
    /// <summary>PLANTED BUG: a floor tile that gives way under the player, dropping them out of the level.</summary>
    public class Trapdoor : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public float standSeconds = 0.4f;
        float _timer;
        bool _open;

        void Update()
        {
            if (_open) return;
            var cc = FindAnyObjectByType<CharacterController>();
            if (cc == null) return;
            var b = GetComponent<Collider>().bounds;
            var p = cc.transform.position;
            var inside = p.x > b.min.x && p.x < b.max.x && p.z > b.min.z && p.z < b.max.z && p.y < b.max.y + 2f;
            _timer = inside ? _timer + Time.deltaTime : 0f;
            if (_timer < standSeconds) return;
            _open = true;
            GetComponent<Collider>().enabled = false;
            GetComponent<Renderer>().enabled = false;
        }
    }
}

```

`samples/broken-game/Assets/Tests/PlayMode/SampleRunTests.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections;
using PlayGoblin;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace BrokenGame.Tests
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Walks a sample scene and keeps the run folder. The pass/fail judgement is made by scripts/verify_samples.py,
    /// which turns the run into a report and compares it with the planted bugs.
    /// Environment: PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SCENE (Broken|Clean), PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_OUT (folder), PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SECONDS (game seconds), PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SEED.
    /// </summary>
    public class SampleRunTests
    {
        [UnityTest, Timeout(600000)]
        public IEnumerator WalkTheSampleScene()
        {
            var scene = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SCENE");
            var outDir = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_OUT");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(scene) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(outDir)) Assert.Ignore("set PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SCENE and PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_OUT");
            var seconds = float.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SECONDS"), out var s) ? s : 120f;
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true; // the planted bugs log errors on purpose

            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(scene);
            Time.timeScale = 4f;
            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions
            {
                seed = int.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SEED"), out var seed) ? seed : 7, minutes = seconds / 60f, outputRoot = outDir, fpsBudgetMs = 33f, waitForPlayerSeconds = 30f
            });
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended) yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            Assert.AreNotEqual("no_player", run.EndReason);
        }
    }
}

```

`samples/broken-game/README.md`:

```md
# broken-game

A tiny Unity level used to check that PlayGoblin finds what it should and stays quiet when there is nothing to find.
It has two scenes built from the same layout by `Assets/Editor/BrokenGameBuilder.cs`:

- **Broken**: seven planted bugs.
- **Clean**: the same level with every bug switched off. PlayGoblin must report nothing here.

| Planted bug | Where | What PlayGoblin reports |
|---|---|---|
| Floor tile gives way under the player | `Trapdoor.cs`, tile near the start | Player fell out of the world |
| `NullReferenceException` every frame after the first fall | `DeathHud.cs` (unassigned label) | Exception with the stack hash and count |
| 0.5 m step across the level. The walk grid allows 0.6 m, the controller climbs 0.3 m | `Ledge` object | Stuck spot, confirmed when it repeats |
| Menu button with no listener | `Options` button | Dead button |
| Placeholder text on the main menu | `TODO: game title` | Placeholder text on screen |
| Missing-material (magenta) decal on the floor | `Missing Material Decal` | Missing-material pink on screen |
| Black screen for 8 s | `CameraBlackout.cs` | Screen rendered blank |
| About 15 fps for 40 s | `FrameStutter.cs` | Frame rate dropped (needs `fpsBudgetMs`) |

Not planted: editor gizmos left on, because PlayGoblin cannot detect them yet.

## Run it

```bash
# build the scenes (only needed after editing the builder)
Unity -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath samples/broken-game -executeMethod BrokenGame.Editor.BrokenGameBuilder.BuildAll

# walk both scenes for several seeds and compare the reports with the table above
python scripts/verify_samples.py --unity "<path to Unity.exe>" --seeds 1 2 3 4 5
```

The script opens a Unity window: frames cannot be captured under `-nographics`. Each walk is 120 game seconds at 4x speed.

## What this does and does not show

All planted bugs were found in 5 of 5 seeds, and the Clean scene had no findings in 5 of 5 runs. The planted bugs were chosen by the people who wrote the checks, so this is a regression test, not a measure of accuracy on real games. Real-game numbers still need to be collected.

```

}

```

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox.Target.cs`:

```cs
using UnrealBuildTool;

public class PlayGoblinSandboxTarget : TargetRules
{
	public PlayGoblinSandboxTarget(TargetInfo Target) : base(Target)
	{
		Type = TargetType.Game;
		DefaultBuildSettings = BuildSettingsVersion.Latest;
		IncludeOrderVersion = EngineIncludeOrderVersion.Latest;
		ExtraModuleNames.Add("PlayGoblinSandbox");
	}
}

```

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox/PlayGoblinSandbox.Build.cs`:

```cs
using UnrealBuildTool;

public class PlayGoblinSandbox : ModuleRules
{
	public PlayGoblinSandbox(ReadOnlyTargetRules Target) : base(Target)
	{
		PCHUsage = PCHUsageMode.UseExplicitOrSharedPCHs;
		PublicDependencyModuleNames.AddRange(new string[] { "Core", "CoreUObject", "Engine", "InputCore", "Slate", "SlateCore", "UMG" });
	}
}

```

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox/SandboxCharacter.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "SandboxCharacter.h"

#include "Camera/CameraComponent.h"
#include "Components/InputComponent.h"
#include "Components/CapsuleComponent.h"
#include "GameFramework/CharacterMovementComponent.h"
#include "GameFramework/SpringArmComponent.h"

ASandboxCharacter::ASandboxCharacter()
{
	GetCapsuleComponent()->InitCapsuleSize(34.f, 88.f);
	GetCharacterMovement()->MaxWalkSpeed = 500.f;
	GetCharacterMovement()->MaxStepHeight = 45.f;

	USpringArmComponent* Boom = CreateDefaultSubobject<USpringArmComponent>(TEXT("Boom"));
	Boom->SetupAttachment(RootComponent);
	Boom->SetUsingAbsoluteRotation(true);
	Boom->TargetArmLength = 4500.f;
	Boom->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(-80.f, 0.f, 0.f));
	Boom->bDoCollisionTest = false;

	UCameraComponent* Cam = CreateDefaultSubobject<UCameraComponent>(TEXT("Camera"));
	Cam->SetupAttachment(Boom);
}

void ASandboxCharacter::SetupPlayerInputComponent(UInputComponent* In)
{
	Super::SetupPlayerInputComponent(In);
	In->BindAxis(TEXT("MoveForward"), this, &ASandboxCharacter::MoveForward);
	In->BindAxis(TEXT("MoveRight"), this, &ASandboxCharacter::MoveRight);
	In->BindAxis(TEXT("Turn"), this, &ASandboxCharacter::Turn);
	In->BindAction(TEXT("Jump"), IE_Pressed, this, &ACharacter::Jump);
}

void ASandboxCharacter::MoveForward(float V)
{
	if (V != 0.f && Controller) AddMovementInput(FRotationMatrix(FRotator(0, Controller->GetControlRotation().Yaw, 0)).GetUnitAxis(EAxis::X), V);
}

void ASandboxCharacter::MoveRight(float V)
{
	if (V != 0.f && Controller) AddMovementInput(FRotationMatrix(FRotator(0, Controller->GetControlRotation().Yaw, 0)).GetUnitAxis(EAxis::Y), V);
}

void ASandboxCharacter::Turn(float V)
{
	AddControllerYawInput(V);
}

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox/SandboxGameMode.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "SandboxGameMode.h"

#include "Components/DirectionalLightComponent.h"
#include "Components/SkyLightComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/DirectionalLight.h"
#include "Engine/SkyLight.h"
#include "Engine/StaticMeshActor.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerStart.h"
#include "Kismet/GameplayStatics.h"
#include "Misc/CommandLine.h"
#include "SandboxCharacter.h"
#include "SandboxMenu.h"
#include "Blueprint/UserWidget.h"

DEFINE_LOG_CATEGORY_STATIC(LogSandbox, Log, All);

ASandboxGameMode::ASandboxGameMode()
{
	DefaultPawnClass = ASandboxCharacter::StaticClass();
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
}

AStaticMeshActor* ASandboxGameMode::Box(const FVector& Min, const FVector& Max)
{
	UStaticMesh* Cube = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube"));
	AStaticMeshActor* A = GetWorld()->SpawnActor<AStaticMeshActor>(FVector((Min.X + Max.X) / 2, (Min.Y + Max.Y) / 2, (Min.Z + Max.Z) / 2), FRotator::ZeroRotator);
	A->GetStaticMeshComponent()->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	A->GetStaticMeshComponent()->SetStaticMesh(Cube);
	A->SetActorScale3D((Max - Min) / 100.f);
	return A;
}

void ASandboxGameMode::InitGame(const FString& MapName, const FString& Options, FString& ErrorMessage)
{
	Super::InitGame(MapName, Options, ErrorMessage);
	bClean = FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PGClean"));

	// Floor 60 x 60 m with a hole where the trapdoor tile sits. Heights in cm, top of the floor at z = 0.
	const float H = 3000.f, Z0 = -100.f, Z1 = 0.f;
	const float HX0 = -1200.f, HX1 = -400.f, HY0 = -1200.f, HY1 = -400.f;
	Box(FVector(-H, -H, Z0), FVector(HX0, H, Z1));
	Box(FVector(HX1, -H, Z0), FVector(H, H, Z1));
	Box(FVector(HX0, -H, Z0), FVector(HX1, HY0, Z1));
	Box(FVector(HX0, HY1, Z0), FVector(HX1, H, Z1));
	Trapdoor = Box(FVector(HX0, HY0, Z0), FVector(HX1, HY1, Z1));
	TrapBounds = FBox(FVector(HX0, HY0, Z0), FVector(HX1, HY1, Z1));

	// A long step across the level: 70 cm (bug) or a 20 cm kerb (clean).
	const float Step = bClean ? 20.f : 70.f;
	Box(FVector(-500.f, -100.f, 0.f), FVector(3000.f, 100.f, Step));

	GetWorld()->SpawnActor<APlayerStart>(FVector(-2000.f, -2000.f, 120.f), FRotator::ZeroRotator);
	if (ADirectionalLight* Sun = GetWorld()->SpawnActor<ADirectionalLight>(FVector(0, 0, 1000), FRotator(-50, 30, 0)))
	{
		Sun->GetLightComponent()->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Sun->GetLightComponent()->SetIntensity(8.f);
	}
	if (ASkyLight* Sky = GetWorld()->SpawnActor<ASkyLight>(FVector(0, 0, 1000), FRotator::ZeroRotator))
	{
		Sky->GetLightComponent()->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Sky->GetLightComponent()->SetIntensity(1.f);
	}
	UE_LOG(LogSandbox, Display, TEXT("Sandbox level built (%s)."), bClean ? TEXT("clean") : TEXT("with planted bugs"));
}

void ASandboxGameMode::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	// Planted: "Options" does nothing and the title is placeholder text. Clean: only a working "Play".
	if (UUserWidget* Menu = CreateWidget<UUserWidget>(GetWorld(), USandboxMenu::StaticClass()))
		Menu->AddToViewport();
}

void ASandboxGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	APawn* Pawn = UGameplayStatics::GetPlayerPawn(this, 0);
	if (!Pawn) return;
	const FVector P = Pawn->GetActorLocation();

	if (!bClean && !bTrapOpen && Trapdoor)
	{
		const bool bInside = P.X > TrapBounds.Min.X && P.X < TrapBounds.Max.X && P.Y > TrapBounds.Min.Y && P.Y < TrapBounds.Max.Y && P.Z < 300.f;
		StandSeconds = bInside ? StandSeconds + DeltaSeconds : 0.f;
		if (StandSeconds > 0.4f)
		{
			bTrapOpen = true;
			Trapdoor->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
			Trapdoor->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		}
	}
	if (P.Z < -200.f) bHasDied = true;
	// PLANTED BUG: after the first fall the death screen reads a widget that was never created.
	if (!bClean && bHasDied) UE_LOG(LogSandbox, Error, TEXT("DeathScreen: Widget is null, cannot show respawn message"));
}

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox/SandboxGameMode.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GameFramework/GameModeBase.h"
#include "SandboxGameMode.generated.h"

class AStaticMeshActor;

/**
 * Builds a small level at startup. Unless the game is started with -PGClean it contains three planted bugs:
 *  - a trapdoor tile that gives way under the player (fall out of the world),
 *  - a 70 cm step across the level (the plugin allows 150 cm of climb, the character climbs 45 cm: stuck spot),
 *  - an error logged every frame once the player has fallen once.
 */
UCLASS()
class ASandboxGameMode : public AGameModeBase
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	ASandboxGameMode();
	virtual void InitGame(const FString& MapName, const FString& Options, FString& ErrorMessage) override;
	virtual void Tick(float DeltaSeconds) override;
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;

private:
	AStaticMeshActor* Box(const FVector& Min, const FVector& Max);

	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<AStaticMeshActor> Trapdoor;
	bool bClean = false;
	bool bTrapOpen = false;
	bool bHasDied = false;
	float StandSeconds = 0.f;
	FBox TrapBounds = FBox(ForceInit);
};

`samples/unreal-sandbox/Source/PlayGoblinSandbox/SandboxMenu.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "SandboxMenu.h"

#include "Blueprint/WidgetTree.h"
#include "Components/Button.h"
#include "Components/TextBlock.h"
#include "Components/VerticalBox.h"
#include "Misc/CommandLine.h"

TSharedRef<SWidget> USandboxMenu::RebuildWidget()
{
	if (!WidgetTree) WidgetTree = NewObject<UWidgetTree>(this, TEXT("WidgetTree"), RF_Transient);
	if (!WidgetTree->RootWidget)
	{
		const bool bClean = FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PGClean"));
		UVerticalBox* Root = WidgetTree->ConstructWidget<UVerticalBox>(UVerticalBox::StaticClass(), TEXT("Root"));
		WidgetTree->RootWidget = Root;

		UTextBlock* Title = WidgetTree->ConstructWidget<UTextBlock>(UTextBlock::StaticClass(), TEXT("Title"));
		Title->SetText(FText::FromString(bClean ? TEXT("Sandbox") : TEXT("TODO: title")));
		Root->AddChildToVerticalBox(Title);

		auto AddButton = [&](const TCHAR* Name, const TCHAR* Label) -> UButton*
		{
			UButton* B = WidgetTree->ConstructWidget<UButton>(UButton::StaticClass(), Name);
			UTextBlock* T = WidgetTree->ConstructWidget<UTextBlock>(UTextBlock::StaticClass(), *FString::Printf(TEXT("%sLabel"), Name));
			T->SetText(FText::FromString(Label));
			B->SetContent(T);
			Root->AddChildToVerticalBox(B);
			return B;
		};
		// Options comes first: the crawler clicks top to bottom and "Play" closes the menu.
		if (!bClean) AddButton(TEXT("OptionsButton"), TEXT("Options")); // planted dead button: no handler
		AddButton(TEXT("PlayButton"), TEXT("Play"))->OnClicked.AddDynamic(this, &USandboxMenu::OnPlay);
	}
	return Super::RebuildWidget();
}

void USandboxMenu::OnPlay()
{
	RemoveFromParent();
}

`scripts/verify_samples.py`:

```py
"""Walk the two sample games with a real Unity editor and check PlayGoblin's report against the planted bugs.

  python scripts/verify_samples.py --unity "C:/Program Files/Unity/Hub/Editor/6000.4.0f1/Editor/Unity.exe"

Broken must produce every kind of finding below. Clean (same level, no planted bugs) must produce none at all,
confirmed or not. Needs a Unity install; it opens a window (frames cannot be captured under -nographics).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "cli"))

from playgoblin.config import Config  # noqa: E402
from playgoblin.pipeline import analyze  # noqa: E402

# (finding type, text that must appear in the title) for each planted bug.
EXPECTED = [
    ("exception", "NullReferenceException"),
    ("out_of_world", ""),
    ("stuck", ""),
    ("dead_button", "Options"),
    ("debug_leftover", "Placeholder text"),
    ("debug_leftover", "pink"),
    ("fps_drop", ""),
    ("blank_frame", ""),
]


def walk(unity: str, scene: str, out: Path, seconds: int, seed: int, log: Path) -> Path:
    shutil.rmtree(out, ignore_errors=True)
    env = dict(os.environ, PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SCENE=scene, PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_OUT=str(out), PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SECONDS=str(seconds),
               PLAYGOBLIN_SAMPLE_SEED=str(seed))
    cmd = [unity, "-batchmode", "-projectPath", str(ROOT / "samples" / "broken-game"), "-runTests", "-testPlatform",
           "PlayMode", "-testFilter", "BrokenGame.Tests", "-testResults", str(log.with_suffix(".xml")), "-logFile", str(log)]
    code = subprocess.run(cmd, env=env).returncode
    runs = sorted(p for p in out.glob("*") if (p / "run.json").is_file())
    if code != 0 or not runs:
        raise SystemExit(f"Unity run for {scene} failed (exit {code}); see {log}")
    return runs[-1]


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--unity", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=int, default=120)
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[7])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(ROOT / "out" / "verify"))
    args = ap.parse_args()
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    detected = {i: 0 for i in range(len(EXPECTED))}
    clean_findings: list[str] = []
    broken_extra: list[str] = []
    for seed in args.seeds:
        print(f"\n== seed {seed} ==")
        run = walk(args.unity, "Broken", out / f"broken-s{seed}", args.seconds, seed, out / f"broken-s{seed}.log")
        findings, _ = analyze([run], out / f"broken-report-s{seed}", Config())
        for i, (typ, text) in enumerate(EXPECTED):
            hit = any(f.type == typ and text.lower() in f.title.lower() for f in findings)
            detected[i] += hit
            print(f"  {'found  ' if hit else 'MISSED '} {typ} {text}")
        broken_extra += [f"{f.type}: {f.title}" for f in findings
                         if not any(f.type == t and x.lower() in f.title.lower() for t, x in EXPECTED)]
        run = walk(args.unity, "Clean", out / f"clean-s{seed}", args.seconds, seed, out / f"clean-s{seed}.log")
        clean, _ = analyze([run], out / f"clean-report-s{seed}", Config())
        clean_findings += [f"seed {seed} {f.type}: {f.title}" for f in clean]
        print(f"  clean sample: {len(clean)} finding(s)")

    n = len(args.seeds)
    print(f"\nRecall over {n} seed(s), one 120 s run each")
    for i, (typ, text) in enumerate(EXPECTED):
        print(f"  {detected[i]}/{n}  {typ} {text}")
    print(f"False positives on the clean sample: {len(clean_findings)} across {n} run(s)")
    for line in clean_findings:
        print("   ", line)
    for line in broken_extra:
        print("  note: unexpected finding in broken sample:", line)

    ok = all(v == n for v in detected.values()) and not clean_findings
    (out / "verify.json").write_text(json.dumps({"seeds": args.seeds, "detected": {EXPECTED[i][0] + ":" + EXPECTED[i][1]: v for i, v in detected.items()},
                                                 "clean_findings": clean_findings, "passed": ok}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("\nOK" if ok else "\nFAILED")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

`scripts/verify_unreal.py`:

```py
"""Walk the Unreal sandbox with the PlayGoblin plugin and check the report against the planted bugs.

  python scripts/verify_unreal.py --editor "C:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8/Engine/Binaries/Win64/UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe"

The project must be built first (see unreal/README.md). The sandbox started normally has five planted bugs
(a trapdoor, a step the character cannot climb, an error after the first fall, a dead UMG button, placeholder menu text); started with -PGClean it has none
and PlayGoblin must report nothing.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "cli"))

from playgoblin.config import Config  # noqa: E402
from playgoblin.pipeline import analyze  # noqa: E402

PROJECT = ROOT / "samples" / "unreal-sandbox" / "PlayGoblinSandbox.uproject"
EXPECTED = [("out_of_world", ""), ("stuck", ""), ("exception", "DeathScreen"),
            ("dead_button", "Options"), ("debug_leftover", "TODO")]
# The generated grey sandbox is made of engine cubes and the default material, so it triggers prototype_look on purpose:
# expected and reported, but not counted as a missed bug or a false positive.
EXPECTED_EXTRA = "prototype_look"


def walk(editor: str, clean: bool, seed: int, seconds: int, out: Path, log: Path, render: bool, extra: list[str]) -> Path:
    shutil.rmtree(out, ignore_errors=True)
    cmd = [editor, str(PROJECT), "-game", "-playgoblin", f"-PlayGoblinSeconds={seconds}", f"-PlayGoblinSeed={seed}",
           f"-PlayGoblinOut={out}", "-PlayGoblinTimeScale=3", "-PlayGoblinExit", "-unattended", "-nosplash",
           "-nosound", f"-abslog={log}"]
    if not render:
        cmd.append("-nullrhi")
    if clean:
        cmd.append("-PGClean")
    cmd += extra
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=False)
    runs = sorted(p for p in out.glob("*") if (p / "run.json").is_file())
    if not runs:
        raise SystemExit(f"no run folder produced; see {log}")
    return runs[-1]


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--editor", required=True, help="path to UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe (or UnrealEditor.exe to render frames)")
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=int, default=150)
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[1])
    ap.add_argument("--render", action="store_true", help="render frames (otherwise -nullrhi: no frames)")
    ap.add_argument("--game-arg", action="append", default=[], help="extra argument for the game, e.g. --game-arg=-PlayGoblinKeys")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(ROOT / "out" / "verify-unreal"))
    args = ap.parse_args()
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    detected = {e: 0 for e in EXPECTED}
    clean_findings: list[str] = []
    proto: dict[str, int] = {}
    for seed in args.seeds:
        print(f"\n== seed {seed} ==")
        run = walk(args.editor, False, seed, args.seconds, out / f"broken-s{seed}", out / f"broken-s{seed}.log", args.render, args.game_arg)
        findings, _ = analyze([run], out / f"broken-report-s{seed}", Config())
        proto[f"broken s{seed}"] = sum(f.type == EXPECTED_EXTRA for f in findings)
        for typ, text in EXPECTED:
            hit = any(f.type == typ and text.lower() in (f.title + f.detail).lower() for f in findings)
            detected[(typ, text)] += hit
            print(f"  {'found  ' if hit else 'MISSED '} {typ} {text}")
        run = walk(args.editor, True, seed, args.seconds, out / f"clean-s{seed}", out / f"clean-s{seed}.log", args.render, args.game_arg)
        clean, _ = analyze([run], out / f"clean-report-s{seed}", Config())
        proto[f"clean s{seed}"] = sum(f.type == EXPECTED_EXTRA for f in clean)
        clean = [f for f in clean if f.type != EXPECTED_EXTRA]
        clean_findings += [f"seed {seed} {f.type}: {f.title}" for f in clean]
        print(f"  clean sandbox: {len(clean)} finding(s) besides {EXPECTED_EXTRA}")

    n = len(args.seeds)
    print(f"\nRecall over {n} seed(s)")
    for (typ, text), v in detected.items():
        print(f"  {v}/{n}  {typ} {text}")
    print(f"{EXPECTED_EXTRA} (expected on the generated grey level, reported only): " + ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in proto.items()))
    print(f"False positives on the clean sandbox: {len(clean_findings)}")
    for line in clean_findings:
        print("   ", line)
    ok = all(v == n for v in detected.values()) and not clean_findings
    print("\nOK" if ok else "\nFAILED")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())



```

`skills/playgoblin/SKILL.md`:

```md
---
name: playgoblin
description: Use when the user wants to QA-test, playtest or smoke-test a game (Unity, Unreal, or any engine that writes PlayGoblin run folders), find crashes, stuck spots, falls through the world, dead buttons, missing-material pink, placeholder text or FPS drops, turn an overnight walk into a bug report, or file the findings as GitHub issues.
---

# PlayGoblin: a bot that plays the game and reports what is broken

## Tools

If the `playgoblin` MCP server is connected, use its tools. Otherwise use the CLI with `--json`.

| Goal | MCP tool | CLI |
|---|---|---|
| Is a game reachable? | `playgoblin_status` | `playgoblin run --minutes 0.1 --json` fails with a clear message if not |
| Walk and report | `playgoblin_run` | `playgoblin run --minutes 3 --runs 2 --json` |
| Report from existing runs (offline) | `playgoblin_report` | `playgoblin report <run folders> --json` |
| Read findings | `playgoblin_findings` | read `playgoblin-report/findings.json` |
| File GitHub issues | `playgoblin_file_issues` | `playgoblin file-issues --repo owner/name [--yes]` |

## Workflow

1. **Check the game is ready.** Unity: the PlayGoblin package is installed, a `PlayGoblin Player` component is on the player object, and the editor is in Play Mode. Unreal: the game is started with `-playgoblin` (plugin is experimental). Other engines: write run folders (docs/PROTOCOL.md) and use `playgoblin report`.
2. **Start short.** One 2-3 minute run first. Then run 3+ seeds (`runs: 3`) so stuck spots can be confirmed.
3. **Read findings by confidence.** `confirmed: true` findings are safe to act on. `confirmed: false` (usually a stuck spot seen once) may be noise: rerun with another seed before reporting it as a bug.
4. **Explain, with evidence.** Quote the finding's title, time, position and `runs_seen/runs_total`. The report folder has frames and a clip under `evidence/<id>/`.
5. **Issues need consent.** `playgoblin_file_issues` is a dry run unless `confirm: true`. Show the person what would be filed and get a clear yes first. It skips findings that are already filed.

## What it can and cannot see

Finds: exceptions and error logs (grouped), stuck spots, falls, a closed-in start area, dead uGUI buttons on menus before the player exists, TODO/placeholder text, missing-material magenta, blank screens, frame-rate drops (only with a budget set).
Does not find: gameplay balance, visual polish, audio problems, editor gizmos, logic bugs that need a goal (it walks randomly, it does not play to win). It is not proof the game is bug free.

## Gotchas

- Frames and clips need a rendering device: not `-batchmode -nographics`.
- Results from a walk of 1-2 minutes are thin. Longer or repeated runs find more.
- Ignore known noise with `playgoblin.toml`: `[ignore] messages = ["regex"]`.
- The local API listens on 127.0.0.1:47321; set `PLAYGOBLIN_PORT` or `--port` if it is taken.

```

`unity/Editor/PlayGoblinEditorHost.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin.Editor
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Starts the local API when the editor loads and handles starting runs from outside Play Mode:
    /// the request is saved, Play Mode is entered, and the run starts once the scene is playing.
    /// </summary>
    [InitializeOnLoad]
    public static class PlayGoblinEditorHost
    {
        public const string EnabledPref = "PlayGoblin.HostEnabled";
        const string PendingKey = "PlayGoblin.PendingRun";
        const string StopOnEndKey = "PlayGoblin.StopPlayModeOnEnd";

        static PlayGoblinEditorHost()
        {
            PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = StartRun;
            EditorApplication.update -= PlayGoblinHost.Pump;
            EditorApplication.update += PlayGoblinHost.Pump;
            EditorApplication.playModeStateChanged -= OnPlayModeChanged;
            EditorApplication.playModeStateChanged += OnPlayModeChanged;
            AssemblyReloadEvents.beforeAssemblyReload -= PlayGoblinHost.Stop;
            AssemblyReloadEvents.beforeAssemblyReload += PlayGoblinHost.Stop;
            EditorApplication.quitting -= PlayGoblinHost.Stop;
            EditorApplication.quitting += PlayGoblinHost.Stop;
            PlayGoblinRun.Ended -= OnRunEnded;
            PlayGoblinRun.Ended += OnRunEnded;

            // Batch mode (CI, test runs) doesn't need the API and shouldn't grab the port.
            if (!Application.isBatchMode && EditorPrefs.GetBool(EnabledPref, true))
            {
                PlayGoblinHost.Start(PlayGoblinHost.ResolvePort());
            }
        }

        public static void SetEnabled(bool enabled)
        {
            EditorPrefs.SetBool(EnabledPref, enabled);
            if (enabled) PlayGoblinHost.Start(PlayGoblinHost.ResolvePort());
            else PlayGoblinHost.Stop();
        }

        public static string StartRun(PlayGoblinRunOptions options)
        {
            if (EditorApplication.isPlaying)
            {
                SessionState.SetBool(StopOnEndKey, options.stopPlayModeOnEnd);
                PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(options);
                return "running";
            }
            SessionState.SetString(PendingKey, JsonUtility.ToJson(options));
            // Enter Play Mode on the next editor tick so an HTTP caller gets its response first.
            EditorApplication.delayCall += () => EditorApplication.isPlaying = true;
            return "starting";
        }

        static void OnPlayModeChanged(PlayModeStateChange change)
        {
            if (change != PlayModeStateChange.EnteredPlayMode) return;
            var pending = SessionState.GetString(PendingKey, "");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pending)) return;
            SessionState.EraseString(PendingKey);
            var options = JsonUtility.FromJson<PlayGoblinRunOptions>(pending);
            SessionState.SetBool(StopOnEndKey, options.stopPlayModeOnEnd);
            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(options);
        }

        static void OnRunEnded(PlayGoblinRun run)
        {
            if (!SessionState.GetBool(StopOnEndKey, false)) return;
            SessionState.EraseBool(StopOnEndKey);
            EditorApplication.delayCall += () => EditorApplication.isPlaying = false;
        }
    }
}

`unity/Editor/PlayGoblinWindow.cs`:

```cs
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin.Editor
{
    public sealed class PlayGoblinWindow : EditorWindow
    {
        int _seed = 42;
        float _minutes = 10f;
        bool _stopPlayModeOnEnd = true;

        [MenuItem("Window/PlayGoblin")]
        public static void Open() => GetWindow<PlayGoblinWindow>("PlayGoblin");

        void OnEnable() => EditorApplication.update += Tick;
        void OnDisable() => EditorApplication.update -= Tick;

        double _nextRepaint;
        void Tick()
        {
            if (EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup < _nextRepaint) return;
            _nextRepaint = EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup + 0.5;
            Repaint();
        }

        void OnGUI()
        {
            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var running = snap.State == PlayGoblinRunState.Running || snap.State == PlayGoblinRunState.Starting;

            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Run", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(running))
            {
                _seed = EditorGUILayout.IntField("Seed", _seed);
                _minutes = Mathf.Max(0.1f, EditorGUILayout.FloatField("Minutes", _minutes));
                _stopPlayModeOnEnd = EditorGUILayout.Toggle("Exit Play Mode when done", _stopPlayModeOnEnd);
            }

            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
            {
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(running))
                {
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Start walk", GUILayout.Height(28)))
                    {
                        PlayGoblinEditorHost.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions
                        {
                            seed = _seed, minutes = _minutes, stopPlayModeOnEnd = _stopPlayModeOnEnd
                        });
                    }
                }
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(!running || PlayGoblinRun.Current == null))
                {
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Stop", GUILayout.Height(28))) PlayGoblinRun.Current?.Stop();
                }
            }

            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Status", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("State", snap.State.ToString());
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(snap.RunId))
            {
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Run", snap.RunId);
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Time", $"{snap.T:0}s");
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Stuck spots", snap.Stucks.ToString());
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Falls out of world", snap.Falls.ToString());
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Distinct errors", snap.Errors.ToString());
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Coverage", $"{snap.Coverage * 100f:0}%");
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(snap.EndReason)) EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Ended", snap.EndReason);
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(snap.RunDirectory) && Directory.Exists(snap.RunDirectory)
                    && GUILayout.Button("Open run folder"))
                {
                    EditorUtility.RevealInFinder(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
                }
            }

            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Local API", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            var enabled = EditorPrefs.GetBool(PlayGoblinEditorHost.EnabledPref, true);
            var newEnabled = EditorGUILayout.Toggle("Enabled", enabled);
            if (newEnabled != enabled) PlayGoblinEditorHost.SetEnabled(newEnabled);
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Address", PlayGoblinHost.IsRunning ? $"http://127.0.0.1:{PlayGoblinHost.Port}" : "off");
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PlayGoblinHost.LastError))
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(PlayGoblinHost.LastError, MessageType.Warning);
        }
    }
}
