`unity/Runtime/DestinationPicker.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Picks seeded random walk targets: area-weighted points on the NavMesh when there is one,
    /// otherwise points inside the level bounds that have ground under them.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DestinationPicker
    {
        readonly System.Random _rng;
        readonly Vector3[] _verts;
        readonly int[] _tris;
        readonly float[] _cumArea;
        readonly Bounds _bounds;
        readonly Func<Vector3, Vector3?> _groundProbe;

        public bool HasNavMesh => _cumArea != null && _cumArea.Length > 0;

        DestinationPicker(int seed, Vector3[] verts, int[] tris, Bounds bounds, Func<Vector3, Vector3?> groundProbe)
        {
            _rng = new System.Random(seed);
            _bounds = bounds;
            _groundProbe = groundProbe;
            if (verts == null || tris == null || tris.Length < 3) return;

            _verts = verts;
            _tris = tris;
            _cumArea = new float[tris.Length / 3];
            float total = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < _cumArea.Length; i++)
            {
                var a = verts[tris[i * 3]];
                var b = verts[tris[i * 3 + 1]];
                var c = verts[tris[i * 3 + 2]];
                total += Vector3.Cross(b - a, c - a).magnitude * 0.5f;
                _cumArea[i] = total;
            }
            if (total <= 0f) _cumArea = null;
        }

        public static DestinationPicker FromNavMesh(int seed, Bounds fallbackBounds, Func<Vector3, Vector3?> groundProbe)
        {
            var tri = NavMesh.CalculateTriangulation();
            return new DestinationPicker(seed, tri.vertices, tri.indices, fallbackBounds, groundProbe);
        }

        public static DestinationPicker FromBounds(int seed, Bounds bounds, Func<Vector3, Vector3?> groundProbe)
        {
            return new DestinationPicker(seed, null, null, bounds, groundProbe);
        }

        public Vector3? RandomPoint()
        {
            if (HasNavMesh) return RandomNavMeshPoint();
            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 20; attempt++)
            {
                var p = new Vector3(
                    Lerp(_bounds.min.x, _bounds.max.x, Next()),
                    _bounds.max.y + 1f,
                    Lerp(_bounds.min.z, _bounds.max.z, Next()));
                var ground = _groundProbe?.Invoke(p);
                if (ground.HasValue) return ground;
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>Samples several candidates and returns the one in the least-visited cell (first wins ties).</summary>
        public Vector3? Pick(CoverageGrid coverage, int candidates = 8)
        {
            Vector3? best = null;
            var bestVisits = int.MaxValue;
            for (int i = 0; i < candidates; i++)
            {
                var p = RandomPoint();
                if (!p.HasValue) continue;
                var v = coverage?.Visits(p.Value) ?? 0;
                if (v < bestVisits) { best = p; bestVisits = v; }
            }
            return best;
        }

        Vector3 RandomNavMeshPoint()
        {
            var total = _cumArea[_cumArea.Length - 1];
            var r = Next() * total;
            var i = Array.BinarySearch(_cumArea, r);
            if (i < 0) i = ~i;
            if (i >= _cumArea.Length) i = _cumArea.Length - 1;

            var a = _verts[_tris[i * 3]];
            var b = _verts[_tris[i * 3 + 1]];
            var c = _verts[_tris[i * 3 + 2]];
            var r1 = Next();
            var r2 = Next();
            if (r1 + r2 > 1f) { r1 = 1f - r1; r2 = 1f - r2; }
            return a + r1 * (b - a) + r2 * (c - a);
        }

        float Next() => (float)_rng.NextDouble();
        static float Lerp(float a, float b, float t) => a + (b - a) * t;
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/Detectors.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Fires when the player has been trying to move for <see cref="Window"/> seconds
    /// but everywhere it went in that window fits inside a box with a diagonal under <see cref="MinDistance"/> meters.
    /// Using the whole window (not just start vs. end) means a walk out and back is not mistaken for being stuck.
    /// Pure logic, no Unity calls, so it's unit-testable.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class StuckDetector
    {
        public float Window = 5f;
        public float MinDistance = 0.5f;

        readonly List<(float t, Vector3 p)> _samples = new List<(float, Vector3)>();
        float _tryingSince = -1f;

        public bool Feed(float t, Vector3 p, bool tryingToMove)
        {
            if (!tryingToMove) { Reset(); return false; }
            if (_tryingSince < 0) _tryingSince = t;

            _samples.Add((t, p));
            // Keep the newest sample that is at least Window old as the window start.
            while (_samples.Count >= 2 && _samples[1].t <= t - Window) _samples.RemoveAt(0);

            if (t - _tryingSince < Window) return false;
            if (t - _samples[0].t < Window) return false;

            var min = _samples[0].p;
            var max = min;
            foreach (var s in _samples)
            {
                min = Vector3.Min(min, s.p);
                max = Vector3.Max(max, s.p);
            }

            if (Vector3.Distance(min, max) < MinDistance)
            {
                Reset();
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        public void Reset()
        {
            _samples.Clear();
            _tryingSince = -1f;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Fires when the player drops below the level (<see cref="KillY"/>), has an invalid position,
    /// or has had no NavMesh under it for <see cref="OffNavMeshSeconds"/>.
    /// Returns a reason string, or null.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class OutOfWorldDetector
    {
        public float KillY = -100f;
        public float OffNavMeshSeconds = 3f;

        float _offSince = -1f;

        public string Feed(float t, Vector3 p, bool? onNavMesh)
        {
            if (float.IsNaN(p.x) || float.IsNaN(p.y) || float.IsNaN(p.z) || float.IsInfinity(p.y))
            {
                Reset();
                return "invalid_position";
            }
            if (p.y < KillY)
            {
                Reset();
                return "below_world";
            }
            if (onNavMesh == false)
            {
                if (_offSince < 0) _offSince = t;
                if (t - _offSince >= OffNavMeshSeconds)
                {
                    Reset();
                    return "off_navmesh";
                }
            }
            else
            {
                _offSince = -1f;
            }
            return null;
        }

        public void Reset() { _offSince = -1f; }
    }

    /// <summary>Counts visits per grid cell so the explorer can prefer places it hasn't been.</summary>
    public sealed class CoverageGrid
    {
        readonly float _cell;
        readonly Dictionary<long, int> _visits = new Dictionary<long, int>();
        readonly HashSet<long> _reachable = new HashSet<long>();

        public CoverageGrid(float cellSize = 2f) { _cell = Mathf.Max(0.1f, cellSize); }

        public long Key(Vector3 p)
        {
            var x = Mathf.FloorToInt(p.x / _cell);
            var z = Mathf.FloorToInt(p.z / _cell);
            return ((long)x << 32) ^ (uint)z;
        }

        public void Visit(Vector3 p)
        {
            var k = Key(p);
            _visits.TryGetValue(k, out var n);
            _visits[k] = n + 1;
        }

        public int Visits(Vector3 p) => _visits.TryGetValue(Key(p), out var n) ? n : 0;

        public void MarkReachable(Vector3 p) => _reachable.Add(Key(p));

        public int ReachableCells => _reachable.Count;

        /// <summary>Share of reachable cells visited at least once (0..1). 0 when reachability is unknown.</summary>
        public float Coverage
        {
            get
            {
                if (_reachable.Count == 0) return 0f;
                var seen = 0;
                foreach (var k in _reachable) if (_visits.ContainsKey(k)) seen++;
                return (float)seen / _reachable.Count;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`unity/Runtime/Explorer.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Walks the player to seeded random points, preferring places it hasn't visited, and records
    /// stuck spots and falls out of the world. After either one it respawns the player somewhere
    /// reachable and keeps going, so one bug doesn't end the run.
    /// </summary>
    internal sealed class Explorer
    {
        const float ArriveDistance = 1.2f;
        const float DestinationTimeout = 30f;
        const float MinDwell = 0.25f;
        const float DetectorHz = 10f;
        const float RespawnGrace = 1f;
        const int ReachabilitySamples = 3000;

        readonly PlayGoblinPlayer _player;
        readonly PlayGoblinRunOptions _options;
        readonly StuckDetector _stuck;
        readonly OutOfWorldDetector _outOfWorld = new OutOfWorldDetector();
        CoverageGrid _coverage;
        DestinationPicker _picker;
        Bounds _world;

        WalkGrid _grid;
        List<Vector3> _path;
        int _pathIdx;
        float _bodyRadius = 0.5f;
        readonly System.Random _rng;
        Vector3? _dest;
        float _levelY;
        Vector3 _lastStuck;
        float _lastStuckT = -1000f;
        float _destSince;
        Vector3 _lastGood;
        bool _hasLastGood;
        bool? _onNavMesh;
        float _nextDetect;
        float _nextSample;
        float _graceUntil;

        public int Stucks { get; private set; }
        public int Falls { get; private set; }
        public int Respawns { get; private set; }
        public int Waypoints { get; private set; }
        public float DistanceWalked { get; private set; }
        public CoverageGrid Coverage => _coverage;
        public bool UsesNavMesh => _picker != null && _picker.HasNavMesh;
        public bool UsesGrid => _grid != null;
        public int WalkableCells => _grid?.WalkableCount ?? 0;
        public int ReachableCells => _grid?.ReachableCount ?? 0;
        public Bounds World => _world;

        Vector3 _prevPos;
        bool _hasPrev;

        public Explorer(PlayGoblinPlayer player, PlayGoblinRunOptions options)
        {
            _player = player;
            _options = options;
            _rng = new System.Random(options.seed);
            _stuck = new StuckDetector { Window = options.stuckSeconds, MinDistance = options.stuckDistance };
            Rebuild();
        }

        /// <summary>Recompute level bounds and walk targets, e.g. after a scene change.</summary>
        public void Rebuild()
        {
            _world = WorldBounds.Compute(_player.transform);
            _levelY = _player.FeetY;
            _outOfWorld.KillY = _world.min.y - 10f;

            _bodyRadius = _player.BodyRadius;

            var tri = NavMesh.CalculateTriangulation();
            var hasNavMesh = tri.indices != null && tri.indices.Length >= 3;
            _picker = hasNavMesh
                ? DestinationPicker.FromNavMesh(_options.seed, _world, PickerProbe)
                : DestinationPicker.FromBounds(_options.seed, _world, PickerProbe);

            _grid = null;
            if (!hasNavMesh)
            {
                var grid = WalkGrid.Build(_world, Mathf.Max(0.5f, _bodyRadius * 2f), 0.6f, GroundProbe, HasBodyClearance);
                if (grid.FloodFrom(_player.Position)) _grid = grid;
            }

            _coverage = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            if (_grid != null)
            {
                foreach (var c in _grid.ReachableCells) _coverage.MarkReachable(c);
                if (_grid.WalkableCount >= 50 && _grid.ReachableCount < _grid.WalkableCount * 0.2f)
                {
                    PlayGoblinEvents.Add("trapped", "player can reach only a small part of the level", _player.Position,
                        new Dictionary<string, string>
                        {
                            { "reachable_cells", _grid.ReachableCount.ToString() },
                            { "walkable_cells", _grid.WalkableCount.ToString() },
                            { "cell_m", _grid.Cell.ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) }
                        });
                }
            }
            else
            {
                var reach = hasNavMesh
                    ? DestinationPicker.FromNavMesh(_options.seed + 1, _world, PickerProbe)
                    : DestinationPicker.FromBounds(_options.seed + 1, _world, PickerProbe);
                for (int i = 0; i < ReachabilitySamples; i++)
                {
                    var p = reach.RandomPoint();
                    if (!p.HasValue) continue;
                    if (hasNavMesh && !CanPathTo(_player.Position, p.Value)) continue;
                    _coverage.MarkReachable(p.Value);
                }
            }

            _dest = null;
            _path = null;
            _stuck.Reset();
            _outOfWorld.Reset();
        }

        public void Tick(float t)
        {
            var pos = _player.Position;
            PlayGoblinEvents.LastPlayerPosition = pos;
            PlayGoblinEvents.HasPlayerPosition = true;

            if (_hasPrev && t >= _graceUntil) DistanceWalked += Vector3.Distance(_prevPos, pos);
            _prevPos = pos;
            _hasPrev = true;

            var detectNow = t >= _nextDetect;
            if (detectNow)
            {
                _nextDetect = t + 1f / DetectorHz;
                if (UsesNavMesh) _onNavMesh = NavMesh.SamplePosition(pos, out _, 2.5f, NavMesh.AllAreas);
            }

            if (detectNow && t >= _graceUntil)
            {
                var reason = _outOfWorld.Feed(t, pos, UsesNavMesh ? _onNavMesh : null);
                if (reason != null)
                {
                    Falls++;
                    var where = _hasLastGood ? _lastGood : pos;
                    PlayGoblinEvents.Add("out_of_world", reason, where, new Dictionary<string, string>
                    {
                        { "reason", reason },
                        { "fell_to", Fmt(pos) }
                    });
                    Respawn(t, "out_of_world", pos);
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (pos.y >= _outOfWorld.KillY && _onNavMesh != false)
            {
                _lastGood = pos;
                _hasLastGood = true;
            }

            if ((!_dest.HasValue || HorizontalDistance(pos, _dest.Value) < ArriveDistance) && t - _destSince >= MinDwell)
            {
                NewDestination(t);
            }
            else if (t - _destSince > DestinationTimeout)
            {
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("waypoint_timeout", $"didn't reach waypoint in {DestinationTimeout:0}s", pos,
                    new Dictionary<string, string> { { "dest", Fmt(_dest.Value) } });
                NewDestination(t);
            }

            if (_dest.HasValue)
            {
                var target = _dest.Value;
                if (_path != null)
                {
                    var reach = Mathf.Max(0.6f, _grid.Cell * 0.6f);
                    while (_pathIdx < _path.Count - 1 && HorizontalDistance(pos, _path[_pathIdx]) < reach) _pathIdx++;
                    target = _path[_pathIdx];
                }
                _player.Mover.MoveTowards(target);
            }

            if (detectNow && t >= _graceUntil && _stuck.Feed(t, pos, _dest.HasValue))
            {
                Stucks++;
                var repeat = t - _lastStuckT < 60f && HorizontalDistance(pos, _lastStuck) < 2f;
                _lastStuck = pos;
                _lastStuckT = t;
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("stuck", $"no progress for {_options.stuckSeconds:0.#}s", pos,
                    new Dictionary<string, string>
                    {
                        { "dest", _dest.HasValue ? Fmt(_dest.Value) : "" },
                        { "next_node", _path != null ? Fmt(_path[_pathIdx]) : "" },
                        { "node", _path != null ? $"{_pathIdx + 1}/{_path.Count}" : "" },
                        { "blocked_by", DescribeBlocker(pos, _path != null ? _path[_pathIdx] : _dest) },
                        { "controller", _player.Diagnostics },
                        { "repeat", repeat ? "true" : "false" }
                    });
                if (repeat || UsesNavMesh)
                {
                    Respawn(t, "unstick", pos);
                }
                else
                {
                    // Straight-line walking can stall on walls; give it another target before teleporting.
                    _stuck.Reset();
                    NewDestination(t);
                }
                return;
            }

            if (detectNow) _coverage.Visit(pos);

            if (t >= _nextSample)
            {
                _nextSample = t + 1f / Mathf.Max(0.1f, _options.positionHz);
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("pos", null, pos, null);
            }
        }

        void NewDestination(float t)
        {
            _path = null;
            _pathIdx = 0;
            _dest = null;
            if (_grid != null)
            {
                for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 6 && _path == null; attempt++)
                {
                    var d = PickReachable();
                    if (!d.HasValue) break;
                    var path = _grid.FindPath(_player.Position, d.Value);
                    if (path == null || path.Count == 0) continue;
                    _path = path;
                    _dest = path[path.Count - 1];
                }
            }
            if (!_dest.HasValue)
            {
                // On a NavMesh, skip targets the player can't path to (islands such as wall tops).
                for (int attempt = 0; attempt < (UsesNavMesh ? 10 : 1); attempt++)
                {
                    var d = _picker.Pick(_coverage);
                    if (!d.HasValue) break;
                    if (UsesNavMesh && !CanPathTo(_player.Position, d.Value)) continue;
                    _dest = d;
                    break;
                }
                if (!_dest.HasValue && UsesNavMesh) _dest = _picker.Pick(_coverage);
            }

            _destSince = t;
            if (_dest.HasValue)
            {
                Waypoints++;
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("waypoint", null, _dest.Value, null);
            }
        }

        readonly NavMeshPath _navPath = new NavMeshPath();

        bool CanPathTo(Vector3 from, Vector3 to)
        {
            if (!NavMesh.SamplePosition(from, out var a, 2.5f, NavMesh.AllAreas)) return false;
            if (!NavMesh.SamplePosition(to, out var b, 2.5f, NavMesh.AllAreas)) return false;
            return NavMesh.CalculatePath(a.position, b.position, NavMesh.AllAreas, _navPath)
                   && _navPath.status == NavMeshPathStatus.PathComplete;
        }

        /// <summary>A reachable cell, preferring ones the player has visited least and that are not right under it.</summary>
        Vector3? PickReachable()
        {
            var cells = _grid.ReachableCells;
            if (cells.Count == 0) return null;
            Vector3? best = null;
            var bestVisits = int.MaxValue;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
            {
                var c = cells[_rng.Next(cells.Count)];
                if (cells.Count > 20 && HorizontalDistance(c, _player.Position) < 3f) continue;
                var v = _coverage.Visits(c);
                if (v < bestVisits) { best = c; bestVisits = v; }
            }
            return best ?? cells[_rng.Next(cells.Count)];
        }

        void Respawn(float t, string reason, Vector3 from)
        {
            var target = (_grid != null ? PickReachable() : _picker.Pick(_coverage)) ?? (_hasLastGood ? _lastGood : from);
            _player.StopMoving();
            _player.Teleport(target);
            Respawns++;
            PlayGoblinEvents.Add("respawn", reason, target, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "from", Fmt(from) } });
            _stuck.Reset();
            _outOfWorld.Reset();
            _graceUntil = t + RespawnGrace;
            _hasPrev = false;
            NewDestination(t);
        }

        /// <summary>Name and distance of the first solid thing between the player and where it is heading ("" if clear).</summary>
        string DescribeBlocker(Vector3 pos, Vector3? heading)
        {
            if (!heading.HasValue) return "";
            var dir = heading.Value - pos;
            dir.y = 0;
            if (dir.sqrMagnitude < 0.0001f) return "";
            var r = _bodyRadius * 0.9f;
            var origin = new Vector3(pos.x, _player.FeetY + _bodyRadius + 0.3f, pos.z);
            foreach (var h in Physics.SphereCastAll(origin, r, dir.normalized, 2f, ~0, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore))
            {
                if (h.collider.transform.IsChildOf(_player.transform)) continue;
                return $"{h.collider.name} @{h.distance.ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}m";
            }
            return "";
        }

        /// <summary>First walkable-looking surface below <paramref name="from"/> on the player's level.</summary>
        Vector3? GroundProbe(Vector3 from)
        {
            var hits = Physics.RaycastAll(from, Vector3.down, _world.size.y + 20f, ~0, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore);
            System.Array.Sort(hits, (a, b) => a.distance.CompareTo(b.distance));
            foreach (var h in hits)
            {
                if (h.collider.transform.IsChildOf(_player.transform)) continue;
                if (Mathf.Abs(h.point.y - _levelY) > _options.maxClimb) continue;
                if (h.normal.y < 0.7f) continue;
                return h.point;
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>Ground probe that also rejects spots inside solid objects (used for random targets).</summary>
        Vector3? PickerProbe(Vector3 from)
        {
            var g = GroundProbe(from);
            return g.HasValue && HasBodyClearance(g.Value) ? g : null;
        }

        bool HasBodyClearance(Vector3 ground)
        {
            // Keep a margin around walls: gliding along a corner at exactly body width can stall the controller.
            var r = _bodyRadius + 0.12f;
            foreach (var c in Physics.OverlapSphere(ground + Vector3.up * (r + 0.3f), r, ~0, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore))
            {
                if (!c.transform.IsChildOf(_player.transform)) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        static float HorizontalDistance(Vector3 a, Vector3 b)
        {
            a.y = 0; b.y = 0;
            return Vector3.Distance(a, b);
        }

        internal static string Fmt(Vector3 v) =>
            string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0:0.##},{1:0.##},{2:0.##}", v.x, v.y, v.z);
    }

    internal static class WorldBounds
    {
        static T[] Find<T>() where T : Object
        {
#if UNITY_6000_4_OR_NEWER
            return Object.FindObjectsByType<T>();
#else
            return Object.FindObjectsByType<T>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
#endif
        }

        /// <summary>Bounds of all solid colliders not under <paramref name="exclude"/>; falls back to renderers.</summary>
        public static Bounds Compute(Transform exclude)
        {
            // Collider bounds are stale for objects moved or scaled since the last physics step.
            Physics.SyncTransforms();
            var has = false;
            var b = new Bounds();
            foreach (var c in Find<Collider>())
            {
                if (c.isTrigger || !c.enabled || (exclude != null && c.transform.IsChildOf(exclude))) continue;
                if (!has) { b = c.bounds; has = true; } else b.Encapsulate(c.bounds);
            }
            if (has) return b;
            foreach (var r in Find<Renderer>())
            {
                if (exclude != null && r.transform.IsChildOf(exclude)) continue;
                if (!has) { b = r.bounds; has = true; } else b.Encapsulate(r.bounds);
            }
            if (has) return b;
            return new Bounds(exclude != null ? exclude.position : Vector3.zero, Vector3.one * 100f);
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/FpsWatch.cs`:

```cs
namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>Reports a stretch of slow frames: average frame time above a budget for a minimum duration.</summary>
    public sealed class FpsWatch
    {
        public float BudgetMs;
        public float MinSeconds = 1f;
        /// <summary>Quiet period after an event so one long stutter is reported once.</summary>
        public float CooldownSeconds = 5f;

        float _slowSeconds;
        float _slowFrames;
        float _slowMsSum;
        float _cooldownUntil;

        public struct Drop
        {
            public float AverageMs;
            public float Seconds;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Feed one frame. <paramref name="ignore"/> marks frames that are skipped (right after load, or the frame
        /// PlayGoblin itself captured a screenshot). Returns true when a drop should be reported.
        /// </summary>
        public bool Feed(float unscaledDeltaSeconds, float gameTime, bool ignore, out Drop drop)
        {
            drop = default;
            if (BudgetMs <= 0f) return false;
            var ms = unscaledDeltaSeconds * 1000f;
            if (ignore) return false; // skipped: neither counts as slow nor breaks a run of slow frames
            if (ms <= BudgetMs)
            {
                _slowSeconds = _slowFrames = _slowMsSum = 0f;
                return false;
            }
            _slowSeconds += unscaledDeltaSeconds;
            _slowFrames++;
            _slowMsSum += ms;
            if (_slowSeconds < MinSeconds || gameTime < _cooldownUntil) return false;
            drop = new Drop { AverageMs = _slowMsSum / _slowFrames, Seconds = _slowSeconds };
            _cooldownUntil = gameTime + CooldownSeconds;
            _slowSeconds = _slowFrames = _slowMsSum = 0f;
            return true;
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/Json.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>Minimal JSON writer. Output is always invariant-culture and valid JSON.</summary>
    public sealed class JsonWriter
    {
        readonly StringBuilder _sb = new StringBuilder(256);
        bool _needComma;

        public JsonWriter BeginObject() { Sep(); _sb.Append('{'); _needComma = false; return this; }
        public JsonWriter EndObject() { _sb.Append('}'); _needComma = true; return this; }
        public JsonWriter BeginArray(string key) { Key(key); _sb.Append('['); _needComma = false; return this; }
        public JsonWriter EndArray() { _sb.Append(']'); _needComma = true; return this; }

        public JsonWriter Prop(string key, string value)
        {
            Key(key);
            if (value == null) _sb.Append("null"); else Escape(_sb, value);
            _needComma = true;
            return this;
        }

        public JsonWriter Prop(string key, double value) { Key(key); Number(value); _needComma = true; return this; }
        public JsonWriter Prop(string key, long value) { Key(key); _sb.Append(value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); _needComma = true; return this; }
        public JsonWriter Prop(string key, bool value) { Key(key); _sb.Append(value ? "true" : "false"); _needComma = true; return this; }

        public JsonWriter Prop(string key, Vector3 v)
        {
            Key(key);
            _sb.Append('[');
            Number(v.x); _sb.Append(','); Number(v.y); _sb.Append(','); Number(v.z);
            _sb.Append(']');
            _needComma = true;
            return this;
        }

        public JsonWriter Raw(string key, string rawJson) { Key(key); _sb.Append(rawJson); _needComma = true; return this; }

        public JsonWriter Item(string value) { Sep(); Escape(_sb, value); _needComma = true; return this; }

        public JsonWriter Props(IDictionary<string, string> data)
        {
            if (data == null) return this;
            foreach (var kv in data) Prop(kv.Key, kv.Value);
            return this;
        }

        public override string ToString() => _sb.ToString();

        void Key(string key)
        {
            Sep();
            if (key != null) { Escape(_sb, key); _sb.Append(':'); }
        }

        void Sep()
        {
            if (_needComma) _sb.Append(',');
            _needComma = false;
        }

        void Number(double d)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(d) || double.IsInfinity(d)) { _sb.Append("null"); return; }
            _sb.Append(System.Math.Round(d, 3).ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        }

        public static void Escape(StringBuilder sb, string s)
        {
            sb.Append('"');
            foreach (var c in s)
            {
                switch (c)
                {
                    case '"': sb.Append("\\\""); break;
                    case '\\': sb.Append("\\\\"); break;
                    case '\n': sb.Append("\\n"); break;
                    case '\r': sb.Append("\\r"); break;
                    case '\t': sb.Append("\\t"); break;
                    default:
                        if (c < 0x20) sb.Append("\\u").Append(((int)c).ToString("x4"));
                        else sb.Append(c);
                        break;
                }
            }
            sb.Append('"');
        }

        public static string Quote(string s)
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder(s.Length + 2);
            Escape(sb, s);
            return sb.ToString();
        }
    }

    public static class StableHash
    {
        /// <summary>FNV-1a 32-bit, hex. Stable across runs and machines (unlike string.GetHashCode).</summary>
        public static string Of(string s)
        {
            unchecked
            {
                uint h = 2166136261;
                foreach (var c in s ?? "")
                {
                    h ^= c;
                    h *= 16777619;
                }
                return h.ToString("x8");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/MenuCrawler.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>Reads text from uGUI Text and (through reflection, so TextMeshPro stays optional) TMP_Text.</summary>
    public static class UiText
    {
        static readonly Type Tmp = Type.GetType("TMPro.TMP_Text, Unity.TextMeshPro");
        static readonly PropertyInfo TmpText = Tmp?.GetProperty("text");

        public static T[] Find<T>() where T : Object
        {
#if UNITY_6000_4_OR_NEWER
            return Object.FindObjectsByType<T>();
#else
            return Object.FindObjectsByType<T>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
#endif
        }

        static Object[] FindType(Type t)
        {
#if UNITY_6000_4_OR_NEWER
            return Object.FindObjectsByType(t);
#else
            return Object.FindObjectsByType(t, FindObjectsSortMode.None);
#endif
        }

        public static int IdOf(Object o)
        {
#if UNITY_6000_4_OR_NEWER
            return o.GetEntityId().GetHashCode();
#else
            return o.GetInstanceID();
#endif
        }

        public static string Of(Component c)
        {
            if (c is Text t) return t.text;
            if (Tmp != null && Tmp.IsInstanceOfType(c)) return TmpText.GetValue(c) as string;
            return null;
        }

        public static List<Component> AllActive()
        {
            var list = new List<Component>();
            foreach (var t in UiText.Find<Text>())
                if (t.isActiveAndEnabled) list.Add(t);
            if (Tmp != null)
                foreach (var o in FindType(Tmp))
                    if (o is Behaviour b && b.isActiveAndEnabled) list.Add(b);
            return list;
        }

        public static string LabelOf(Button b)
        {
            var t = b.GetComponentInChildren<Text>();
            if (t != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(t.text)) return t.text.Trim();
            if (Tmp != null)
            {
                var c = b.GetComponentInChildren(Tmp);
                var s = c != null ? TmpText.GetValue(c) as string : null;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)) return s.Trim();
            }
            return b.name;
        }

        public static string PathOf(Transform t)
        {
            var s = t.name;
            for (var p = t.parent; p != null; p = p.parent) s = p.name + "/" + s;
            return s;
        }
    }

    public static class PlaceholderRules
    {
        static readonly Regex Rx = new Regex(@"\b(todo|fixme|lorem ipsum|placeholder|text here|insert text|new text)\b",
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);

        public static bool IsPlaceholder(string s) => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) && Rx.IsMatch(s);
    }

    /// <summary>Reports placeholder text that is visible on screen, once per distinct string.</summary>
    public sealed class PlaceholderScanner
    {
        readonly HashSet<string> _seen = new HashSet<string>();
        float _next;

        public void Tick(float unscaledNow)
        {
            if (unscaledNow < _next) return;
            _next = unscaledNow + 2f;
            foreach (var c in UiText.AllActive())
            {
                var s = UiText.Of(c);
                if (!PlaceholderRules.IsPlaceholder(s) || !_seen.Add(s.Trim())) continue;
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("placeholder_text", s.Trim(), null, new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    { "path", UiText.PathOf(c.transform) },
                    { "scene", SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Clicks every active uGUI <see cref="Button"/> on screen and follows the screens they open. A button that does
    /// nothing visible on two clicks (no UI change, no scene change, no log output) is reported as dead.
    /// Buttons that look like Quit/Exit are never clicked. Works until it runs out of buttons or hits its limits.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MenuCrawler : IDisposable
    {
        const float ClickEvery = 0.5f;
        const float SettleSeconds = 0.6f;

        static readonly Regex Dangerous = new Regex(@"\b(quit|exit|delete|reset|erase|wipe|purchase|buy)\b",
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
        static readonly Regex Back = new Regex(@"\b(back|close|cancel|return|done)\b",
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);

        readonly PlayGoblinRunOptions _options;
        readonly HashSet<string> _done = new HashSet<string>();
        readonly HashSet<string> _backed = new HashSet<string>();
        int _logCount;
        int _clicks;
        float _nextAction;

        Button _pending;
        string _pendingKey, _pendingLabel;
        int _pendingHash, _pendingLogs, _attempt;
        float _pendingDue;

        public int Clicks => _clicks;
        public int DeadButtons { get; private set; }

        public MenuCrawler(PlayGoblinRunOptions options)
        {
            _options = options;
            Application.logMessageReceived += OnLog;
        }

        public void Dispose() => Application.logMessageReceived -= OnLog;

        void OnLog(string message, string stack, LogType type) => _logCount++;

        public static bool IsDangerous(string label) => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(label) && Dangerous.IsMatch(label);

        /// <summary>Call every frame. Returns true while there is still something to click or a click is being judged.</summary>
        public bool Tick(float unscaledNow)
        {
            if (_pending != null || _pendingKey != null)
            {
                if (unscaledNow < _pendingDue) return true;
                Judge(unscaledNow);
                return true;
            }
            if (unscaledNow < _nextAction) return HasWork();
            _nextAction = unscaledNow + ClickEvery;
            if (_clicks >= _options.maxMenuClicks) return false;

            var buttons = ActiveButtons();
            Button next = null;
            foreach (var b in buttons)
            {
                if (_done.Contains(KeyOf(b)) || IsDangerous(UiText.LabelOf(b)) || IsDangerous(b.name)) continue;
                next = b;
                break;
            }
            if (next == null)
            {
                // Everything on this screen is clicked: step back out once, then carry on with what is left above.
                var sig = ScreenKey(buttons);
                if (!_backed.Add(sig)) return false;
                foreach (var b in buttons)
                    if (Back.IsMatch(UiText.LabelOf(b)) && !IsDangerous(UiText.LabelOf(b))) { next = b; break; }
                if (next == null) return false;
            }
            Click(next, attempt: 1, unscaledNow);
            return true;
        }

        bool HasWork()
        {
            if (_clicks >= _options.maxMenuClicks) return false;
            foreach (var b in ActiveButtons())
                if (!_done.Contains(KeyOf(b)) && !IsDangerous(UiText.LabelOf(b)) && !IsDangerous(b.name)) return true;
            return false;
        }

        void Click(Button b, int attempt, float now)
        {
            _pending = b;
            _pendingKey = KeyOf(b);
            _pendingLabel = UiText.LabelOf(b);
            _pendingHash = Signature();
            _pendingLogs = _logCount;
            _attempt = attempt;
            _pendingDue = now + SettleSeconds;
            _clicks++;
            try { b.onClick.Invoke(); }
            catch (Exception ex) { Debug.LogException(ex); }
        }

        void Judge(float now)
        {
            var key = _pendingKey;
            var label = _pendingLabel;
            var changed = Signature() != _pendingHash || _logCount != _pendingLogs;
            var button = _pending;
            _pending = null;
            _pendingKey = null;
            _done.Add(key);

            var data = new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                { "path", key }, { "label", label }, { "scene", SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path },
                { "result", changed ? "changed" : "no_change" }
            };
            if (changed || button == null)
            {
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("ui_click", label, null, data);
                return;
            }
            if (_attempt == 1)
            {
                // One more try: the first click may only have focused or animated something.
                _done.Remove(key);
                Click(button, 2, now);
                return;
            }
            DeadButtons++;
            PlayGoblinEvents.Add("dead_button", label, null, data);
        }

        static string KeyOf(Button b) => SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name + ":" + UiText.PathOf(b.transform);

        static string ScreenKey(List<Button> buttons)
        {
            var keys = new List<string>();
            foreach (var b in buttons) keys.Add(KeyOf(b));
            keys.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            return string.Join("|", keys);
        }

        static List<Button> ActiveButtons()
        {
            var list = new List<Button>();
            foreach (var b in UiText.Find<Button>())
                if (b.isActiveAndEnabled && b.interactable) list.Add(b);
            list.Sort((a, b) => string.CompareOrdinal(UiText.PathOf(a.transform), UiText.PathOf(b.transform)));
            return list;
        }

        /// <summary>Order-independent fingerprint of what is on screen: scene, active graphics, their sprites, colors and text.</summary>
        static int Signature()
        {
            unchecked
            {
                var h = SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path.GetHashCode();
                foreach (var g in UiText.Find<Graphic>())
                {
                    if (!g.isActiveAndEnabled) continue;
                    var e = UiText.IdOf(g) * 31 + g.color.GetHashCode();
                    if (g is Image im && im.sprite != null) e = e * 31 + UiText.IdOf(im.sprite);
                    var text = UiText.Of(g);
                    if (text != null) e = e * 31 + text.GetHashCode();
                    h += e;
                }
                foreach (var s in UiText.Find<Selectable>())
                    if (s.isActiveAndEnabled) h += s.interactable ? 7 : 13;
                return h;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`unity/Runtime/Movers.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Implement this on your player if PlayGoblin's built-in movers don't fit your controller.
    /// MoveTowards is called every frame while PlayGoblin wants the player to walk somewhere.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IPlayGoblinMover
    {
        void MoveTowards(Vector3 destination);
        void Jump();
    }

    /// <summary>Optional: implement to control how PlayGoblin respawns the player after a fall or a stuck spot.</summary>
    public interface IPlayGoblinTeleporter
    {
        void Teleport(Vector3 position);
    }

    /// <summary>Optional: implement so PlayGoblin can tell the player to stand still between moves.</summary>
    public interface IPlayGoblinStoppable
    {
        void StopMoving();
    }

    internal sealed class NavMeshAgentMover : IPlayGoblinMover, IPlayGoblinTeleporter, IPlayGoblinStoppable
    {
        readonly NavMeshAgent _agent;
        Vector3 _lastDest;
        bool _hasDest;

        public NavMeshAgentMover(NavMeshAgent agent) { _agent = agent; }

        public void MoveTowards(Vector3 destination)
        {
            if (!_agent.enabled || !_agent.isOnNavMesh) return;
            if (_agent.isStopped) _agent.isStopped = false;
            if (!_hasDest || (destination - _lastDest).sqrMagnitude > 0.01f)
            {
                _agent.SetDestination(destination);
                _lastDest = destination;
                _hasDest = true;
            }
        }

        public void Jump() { }

        public void StopMoving()
        {
            if (_agent.enabled && _agent.isOnNavMesh) _agent.isStopped = true;
            _hasDest = false;
        }

        public void Teleport(Vector3 position)
        {
            // An agent can only be (re)enabled close to a NavMesh, so move it there first.
            _agent.enabled = false;
            _agent.transform.position = position;
            _agent.enabled = true;
            if (_agent.isOnNavMesh) _agent.Warp(position);
            _hasDest = false;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Optional: a short description of the controller's state, added to stuck reports.</summary>
    public interface IPlayGoblinDiagnostics
    {
        string Describe();
    }

    internal sealed class CharacterControllerMover : IPlayGoblinMover, IPlayGoblinTeleporter, IPlayGoblinDiagnostics
    {
        readonly CharacterController _cc;
        readonly float _speed;
        float _vy;
        bool _jump;
        CollisionFlags _flags;
        Vector3 _lastStep;

        public string Describe() =>
            $"flags={_flags} grounded={_cc.isGrounded} enabled={_cc.enabled} vy={_vy:0.##} step={_lastStep.x:0.###},{_lastStep.z:0.###}";

        public CharacterControllerMover(CharacterController cc, float speed) { _cc = cc; _speed = speed; }

        public void MoveTowards(Vector3 destination)
        {
            var t = _cc.transform;
            var to = destination - t.position;
            to.y = 0;
            var step = to.sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f ? to.normalized * _speed : Vector3.zero;
            if (step != Vector3.zero) t.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(step);

            if (_cc.isGrounded)
            {
                _vy = _jump ? 5f : -1f;
                _jump = false;
            }
            else
            {
                _vy += Physics.gravity.y * Time.deltaTime;
            }
            step.y = _vy;
            _lastStep = step;
            _flags = _cc.Move(step * Time.deltaTime);
        }

        public void Jump() { _jump = true; }

        public void Teleport(Vector3 position)
        {
            _cc.enabled = false;
            _cc.transform.position = position;
            _cc.enabled = true;
            _vy = 0;
        }
    }

    internal sealed class RigidbodyMover : IPlayGoblinMover, IPlayGoblinTeleporter
    {
        readonly Rigidbody _rb;
        readonly float _speed;

        public RigidbodyMover(Rigidbody rb, float speed) { _rb = rb; _speed = speed; }

        public void MoveTowards(Vector3 destination)
        {
            var to = destination - _rb.position;
            to.y = 0;
            var v = to.sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f ? to.normalized * _speed : Vector3.zero;
#if UNITY_6000_0_OR_NEWER
            v.y = _rb.linearVelocity.y;
            _rb.linearVelocity = v;
#else
            v.y = _rb.velocity.y;
            _rb.velocity = v;
#endif
        }

        public void Jump() { _rb.AddForce(Vector3.up * 5f, ForceMode.VelocityChange); }

        public void Teleport(Vector3 position)
        {
#if UNITY_6000_0_OR_NEWER
            _rb.linearVelocity = Vector3.zero;
#else
            _rb.velocity = Vector3.zero;
#endif
            _rb.position = position;
            _rb.transform.position = position;
        }
    }

    internal sealed class TransformMover : IPlayGoblinMover, IPlayGoblinTeleporter
    {
        readonly Transform _t;
        readonly float _speed;

        public TransformMover(Transform t, float speed) { _t = t; _speed = speed; }

        public void MoveTowards(Vector3 destination)
        {
            var target = new Vector3(destination.x, _t.position.y, destination.z);
            _t.position = Vector3.MoveTowards(_t.position, target, _speed * Time.deltaTime);
        }

        public void Jump() { }

        public void Teleport(Vector3 position) { _t.position = position; }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/PlayGoblinEvents.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    public sealed class PlayGoblinEvent
    {
        public long Seq;
        public double T;              // game seconds since run start
        public string Type;
        public string Detail;
        public Vector3? Position;
        public Dictionary<string, string> Data;

        public string ToJson()
        {
            var w = new JsonWriter().BeginObject()
                .Prop("seq", Seq)
                .Prop("t", T)
                .Prop("type", Type);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Detail)) w.Prop("detail", Detail);
            if (Position.HasValue) w.Prop("pos", Position.Value);
            if (Data != null && Data.Count > 0)
            {
                w.Raw("data", new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Props(Data).EndObject().ToString());
            }
            return w.EndObject().ToString();
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Thread-safe event log for the current run. Games can call <see cref="Log"/> to add their own events
    /// (puzzle solved, boss spawned, ...). Calls outside a run are ignored, so it's safe to leave in shipping code.
    /// </summary>
    public static class PlayGoblinEvents
    {
        const int MemoryCap = 20000;

        static readonly object Gate = new object();
        static readonly List<PlayGoblinEvent> Memory = new List<PlayGoblinEvent>();
        static readonly Queue<PlayGoblinEvent> PendingWrite = new Queue<PlayGoblinEvent>();
        static long _seq;
        static long _dropped;
        static volatile bool _active;

        // Written on the main thread each frame so other threads can stamp events.
        internal static volatile float CurrentTime;
        internal static Vector3 LastPlayerPosition;
        internal static volatile bool HasPlayerPosition;

        public static bool Active => _active;
        public static long Count { get { lock (Gate) return _seq; } }

        /// <summary>Log a game-specific event. Position defaults to none.</summary>
        public static void Log(string type, string detail = null, Vector3? where = null, Dictionary<string, string> data = null)
        {
            Add(type, detail, where, data);
        }

        internal static PlayGoblinEvent Add(string type, string detail, Vector3? where, Dictionary<string, string> data)
        {
            if (!_active) return null;
            lock (Gate)
            {
                var e = new PlayGoblinEvent
                {
                    Seq = ++_seq,
                    T = CurrentTime,
                    Type = type,
                    Detail = detail,
                    Position = where,
                    Data = data
                };
                if (Memory.Count >= MemoryCap) { Memory.RemoveAt(0); _dropped++; }
                Memory.Add(e);
                PendingWrite.Enqueue(e);
                return e;
            }
        }

        internal static void Begin()
        {
            lock (Gate)
            {
                Memory.Clear();
                PendingWrite.Clear();
                _seq = 0;
                _dropped = 0;
                CurrentTime = 0;
                HasPlayerPosition = false;
                _active = true;
            }
        }

        internal static void End() { _active = false; }

        internal static List<PlayGoblinEvent> DrainPending()
        {
            lock (Gate)
            {
                var list = new List<PlayGoblinEvent>(PendingWrite);
                PendingWrite.Clear();
                return list;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Events with Seq greater than <paramref name="afterSeq"/>, oldest first.</summary>
        public static List<PlayGoblinEvent> Since(long afterSeq, int max = 1000)
        {
            lock (Gate)
            {
                var result = new List<PlayGoblinEvent>();
                foreach (var e in Memory)
                {
                    if (e.Seq <= afterSeq) continue;
                    result.Add(e);
                    if (result.Count >= max) break;
                }
                return result;
            }
        }

        public static List<PlayGoblinEvent> All()
        {
            lock (Gate) return new List<PlayGoblinEvent>(Memory);
        }
    }
}

```

`unity/Runtime/PlayGoblinHost.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Small local HTTP+JSON API the playgoblin CLI talks to. Listens on 127.0.0.1 only.
    ///   GET  /status              current or last run
    ///   POST /run/start           body: PlayGoblinRunOptions JSON (all fields optional)
    ///   POST /run/stop
    ///   GET  /events?since=N&amp;max=M
    ///   GET  /runs/latest         run.json of the last finished run
    /// Requests that touch Unity objects are queued and executed by <see cref="Pump"/> on the main thread.
    /// </summary>
    public static class PlayGoblinHost
    {
        public const int DefaultPort = 47321;

        /// <summary>Set by the editor or player bootstrap. Runs on the main thread; returns the new state ("running"/"starting").</summary>
        public static Func<PlayGoblinRunOptions, string> StartHandler;

        static readonly ConcurrentQueue<Action> MainThread = new ConcurrentQueue<Action>();
        static TcpListener _listener;
        static Thread _thread;
        static volatile bool _running;

        public static bool IsRunning => _running;
        public static int Port { get; private set; }
        public static string LastError { get; private set; }

        public static int ResolvePort()
        {
            var env = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_PORT");
            return int.TryParse(env, out var p) && p > 0 && p < 65536 ? p : DefaultPort;
        }

        public static bool Start(int port)
        {
            if (_running) return true;
            try
            {
                _listener = new TcpListener(IPAddress.Loopback, port);
                _listener.Start();
                Port = ((IPEndPoint)_listener.LocalEndpoint).Port;
                _running = true;
                LastError = null;
                _thread = new Thread(AcceptLoop) { IsBackground = true, Name = "PlayGoblin Host" };
                _thread.Start();
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                LastError = ex.Message;
                _listener = null;
                _running = false;
                return false;
            }
        }

        public static void Stop()
        {
            _running = false;
            try { _listener?.Stop(); } catch { /* already closed */ }
            _listener = null;
            _thread = null;
        }

        /// <summary>Run queued main-thread work. Call once per frame (editor update or a MonoBehaviour).</summary>
        public static void Pump()
        {
            while (MainThread.TryDequeue(out var a))
            {
                try { a(); }
                catch (Exception ex) { Debug.LogException(ex); }
            }
        }

        static void AcceptLoop()
        {
            while (_running)
            {
                TcpClient client;
                try { client = _listener.AcceptTcpClient(); }
                catch { break; }
                ThreadPool.QueueUserWorkItem(_ => Serve(client));
            }
        }

        static void Serve(TcpClient client)
        {
            using (client)
            {
                try
                {
                    client.ReceiveTimeout = 5000;
                    client.SendTimeout = 5000;
                    var stream = client.GetStream();
                    if (!TryReadRequest(stream, out var method, out var target, out var body))
                    {
                        Write(stream, 400, Error("bad_request"));
                        return;
                    }
                    var (status, json) = Handle(method, target, body);
                    Write(stream, status, json);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    LastError = ex.Message;
                }
            }
        }

        internal static (int, string) Handle(string method, string target, string body)
        {
            var q = target.IndexOf('?');
            var path = (q >= 0 ? target.Substring(0, q) : target).TrimEnd('/');
            var query = ParseQuery(q >= 0 ? target.Substring(q + 1) : "");
            if (path == "") path = "/";

            switch (method + " " + path)
            {
                case "GET /status":
                    return (200, StatusJson());

                case "POST /run/start":
                {
                    if (StartHandler == null) return (503, Error("no_start_handler"));
                    PlayGoblinRunOptions options;
                    try { options = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body) ? new PlayGoblinRunOptions() : JsonUtility.FromJson<PlayGoblinRunOptions>(body); }
                    catch (Exception ex) { return (400, Error("invalid_options: " + ex.Message)); }
                    if (options.minutes <= 0) return (400, Error("minutes must be > 0"));
                    var state = OnMainThread(() => StartHandler(options));
                    return state == null
                        ? (504, Error("main_thread_timeout"))
                        : (202, "{\"state\":" + JsonWriter.Quote(state) + "}");
                }

                case "POST /run/stop":
                {
                    var result = OnMainThread(() =>
                    {
                        if (PlayGoblinRun.Current == null) return "idle";
                        PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
                        return "stopped";
                    });
                    return result == null
                        ? (504, Error("main_thread_timeout"))
                        : (200, "{\"state\":" + JsonWriter.Quote(result) + "}");
                }

                case "GET /events":
                {
                    long since = 0;
                    int max = 1000;
                    if (query.TryGetValue("since", out var s)) long.TryParse(s, out since);
                    if (query.TryGetValue("max", out var m)) int.TryParse(m, out max);
                    max = Mathf.Clamp(max, 1, 5000);
                    var events = PlayGoblinEvents.Since(since, max);
                    var sb = new StringBuilder("{\"events\":[");
                    for (int i = 0; i < events.Count; i++)
                    {
                        if (i > 0) sb.Append(',');
                        sb.Append(events[i].ToJson());
                    }
                    var next = events.Count > 0 ? events[events.Count - 1].Seq : since;
                    sb.Append("],\"next\":").Append(next.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).Append('}');
                    return (200, sb.ToString());
                }

                case "GET /runs/latest":
                {
                    var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
                    if (snap.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended || string.IsNullOrEmpty(snap.RunDirectory))
                        return (404, Error("no_finished_run"));
                    var file = Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json");
                    return File.Exists(file) ? (200, File.ReadAllText(file)) : (404, Error("run_json_missing"));
                }

                default:
                    return (404, Error("not_found"));
            }
        }

        static string StatusJson()
        {
            var s = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var w = new JsonWriter().BeginObject()
                .Prop("playgoblin", PlayGoblinRun.Version)
                .Prop("state", s.State.ToString().ToLowerInvariant())
                .Prop("run_id", s.RunId)
                .Prop("run_dir", s.RunDirectory)
                .Prop("end_reason", s.EndReason)
                .Prop("t", s.T)
                .Prop("events", s.Events)
                .Prop("stuck", (long)s.Stucks)
                .Prop("out_of_world", (long)s.Falls)
                .Prop("errors", (long)s.Errors)
                .Prop("coverage", s.Coverage);
            return w.EndObject().ToString();
        }

        static string OnMainThread(Func<string> work, int timeoutMs = 5000)
        {
            string result = null;
            using (var done = new ManualResetEventSlim(false))
            {
                MainThread.Enqueue(() =>
                {
                    try { result = work(); }
                    catch (Exception ex) { result = "error: " + ex.Message; }
                    finally { done.Set(); }
                });
                return done.Wait(timeoutMs) ? result : null;
            }
        }

        static string Error(string message) => "{\"error\":" + JsonWriter.Quote(message) + "}";

        static Dictionary<string, string> ParseQuery(string q)
        {
            var d = new Dictionary<string, string>();
            foreach (var part in q.Split('&'))
            {
                if (part.Length == 0) continue;
                var eq = part.IndexOf('=');
                var k = Uri.UnescapeDataString(eq >= 0 ? part.Substring(0, eq) : part);
                var v = eq >= 0 ? Uri.UnescapeDataString(part.Substring(eq + 1)) : "";
                d[k] = v;
            }
            return d;
        }

        static bool TryReadRequest(NetworkStream stream, out string method, out string target, out string body)
        {
            method = target = body = null;
            var header = new List<byte>(1024);
            var complete = false;
            while (!complete && header.Count < 16 * 1024)
            {
                var b = stream.ReadByte();
                if (b < 0) return false;
                header.Add((byte)b);
                var n = header.Count;
                complete = n >= 4 && header[n - 4] == '\r' && header[n - 3] == '\n' && header[n - 2] == '\r' && header[n - 1] == '\n';
            }
            if (!complete) return false;

            var lines = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(header.ToArray()).Split(new[] { "\r\n" }, StringSplitOptions.None);
            var first = lines[0].Split(' ');
            if (first.Length < 2) return false;
            method = first[0].ToUpperInvariant();
            target = first[1];

            var length = 0;
            foreach (var l in lines)
            {
                if (l.StartsWith("Content-Length:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    int.TryParse(l.Substring(15).Trim(), out length);
            }
            if (length < 0 || length > 1024 * 1024) return false;

            var buf = new byte[length];
            var read = 0;
            while (read < length)
            {
                var n = stream.Read(buf, read, length - read);
                if (n <= 0) return false;
                read += n;
            }
            body = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buf);
            return true;
        }

        static void Write(NetworkStream stream, int status, string json)
        {
            var payload = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(json);
            var reason = status switch
            {
                200 => "OK", 202 => "Accepted", 400 => "Bad Request", 404 => "Not Found",
                503 => "Service Unavailable", 504 => "Gateway Timeout", _ => "Error"
            };
            var head = $"HTTP/1.1 {status} {reason}\r\nContent-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8\r\nContent-Length: {payload.Length}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n";
            var h = Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(head);
            stream.Write(h, 0, h.Length);
            stream.Write(payload, 0, payload.Length);
            stream.Flush();
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Player builds: pass -playgoblin-host to expose the local API, or -playgoblin-run to start a walk on launch
    /// (-playgoblin-seed N, -playgoblin-minutes M, -playgoblin-quit to exit when done). The editor uses its own bootstrap.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class PlayGoblinPlayerBootstrap
    {
        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.AfterSceneLoad)]
        static void Init()
        {
            if (Application.isEditor) return;
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            var host = Has(args, "-playgoblin-host");
            var run = Has(args, "-playgoblin-run");
            if (!host && !run) return;

            var pump = new GameObject("[PlayGoblin Host Pump]").AddComponent<HostPump>();
            UnityEngine.Object.DontDestroyOnLoad(pump.gameObject);
            PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = o => { PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(o); return "running"; };
            if (host) PlayGoblinHost.Start(PlayGoblinHost.ResolvePort());

            if (run)
            {
                var o = new PlayGoblinRunOptions();
                if (int.TryParse(Value(args, "-playgoblin-seed"), out var seed)) o.seed = seed;
                if (float.TryParse(Value(args, "-playgoblin-minutes"), NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var min) && min > 0) o.minutes = min;
                if (Has(args, "-playgoblin-quit")) PlayGoblinRun.Ended += _ => Application.Quit();
                PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(o);
            }
        }

        static bool Has(string[] args, string flag) => Array.IndexOf(args, flag) >= 0;

        static string Value(string[] args, string flag)
        {
            var i = Array.IndexOf(args, flag);
            return i >= 0 && i + 1 < args.Length ? args[i + 1] : null;
        }

        sealed class HostPump : MonoBehaviour
        {
            void Update() => PlayGoblinHost.Pump();
            void OnApplicationQuit() => PlayGoblinHost.Stop();
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/PlayGoblinPlayer.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Add this to your player object. PlayGoblin uses, in order: a component on this object that implements
    /// <see cref="IPlayGoblinMover"/>, a NavMeshAgent, a CharacterController, a Rigidbody, or plain transform movement.
    /// </summary>
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    [AddComponentMenu("PlayGoblin/PlayGoblin Player")]
    public sealed class PlayGoblinPlayer : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Tooltip("Walking speed for the CharacterController, Rigidbody and Transform movers. NavMeshAgent uses its own speed.")]
        public float moveSpeed = 3.5f;

        IPlayGoblinMover _mover;

        public IPlayGoblinMover Mover => _mover ??= CreateMover();

        public string MoverKind => Mover switch
        {
            NavMeshAgentMover _ => "NavMeshAgent",
            CharacterControllerMover _ => "CharacterController",
            RigidbodyMover _ => "Rigidbody",
            TransformMover _ => "Transform",
            _ => "Custom"
        };

        public Vector3 Position => transform.position;

        public string Diagnostics => Mover is IPlayGoblinDiagnostics d ? d.Describe() : "";

        /// <summary>World height of the bottom of the player's collider (or the pivot if it has none).</summary>
        public float FeetY => TryGetComponent(out Collider c) && c.enabled ? c.bounds.min.y : transform.position.y;

        /// <summary>Horizontal radius of the player's collider, 0.5 m when it has none.</summary>
        public float BodyRadius => TryGetComponent(out Collider c) && c.enabled ? Mathf.Max(0.1f, Mathf.Max(c.bounds.extents.x, c.bounds.extents.z)) : 0.5f;

        /// <summary>Move the player so its feet are at <paramref name="groundPosition"/>.</summary>
        public void Teleport(Vector3 groundPosition)
        {
            // NavMeshAgent.Warp already applies the agent's base offset.
            var p = Mover is NavMeshAgentMover ? groundPosition : groundPosition + Vector3.up * FootOffset();
            if (Mover is IPlayGoblinTeleporter t) t.Teleport(p);
            else transform.position = p;
        }

        /// <summary>Distance from the bottom of the player's collider up to its pivot.</summary>
        float FootOffset()
        {
            if (TryGetComponent(out Collider c) && c.enabled)
            {
                return Mathf.Max(0f, transform.position.y - c.bounds.min.y) + 0.05f;
            }
            return 0.05f;
        }

        public void StopMoving()
        {
            if (Mover is IPlayGoblinStoppable s) s.StopMoving();
        }

        IPlayGoblinMover CreateMover()
        {
            foreach (var mb in GetComponents<MonoBehaviour>())
            {
                if (mb is IPlayGoblinMover custom && !(mb is PlayGoblinPlayer)) return custom;
            }
            if (TryGetComponent(out NavMeshAgent agent)) return new NavMeshAgentMover(agent);
            if (TryGetComponent(out CharacterController cc)) return new CharacterControllerMover(cc, moveSpeed);
            if (TryGetComponent(out Rigidbody rb) && !rb.isKinematic) return new RigidbodyMover(rb, moveSpeed);
            return new TransformMover(transform, moveSpeed);
        }
    }
}

```
