`unity/Runtime/PlayGoblinRun.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    [Serializable]
    public sealed class PlayGoblinRunOptions
    {
        public int seed = 42;
        public float minutes = 10f;
        public float stuckSeconds = 5f;
        public float stuckDistance = 0.5f;
        public float positionHz = 2f;
        /// <summary>Without a NavMesh, only walk to points at most this far above or below the level the player starts on.</summary>
        public float maxClimb = 1.5f;
        public float waitForPlayerSeconds = 10f;
        public bool stopPlayModeOnEnd;
        /// <summary>Save screenshots to the run folder so reports can show what happened. Off automatically under -nographics.</summary>
        public bool recordFrames = true;
        public float frameHz = 2f;
        /// <summary>Capture rate for <see cref="burstSeconds"/> after a stuck spot or fall.</summary>
        public float burstHz = 10f;
        public float burstSeconds = 3f;
        public int frameWidth = 640;
        public int jpgQuality = 75;
        public int maxFrames = 3000;
        /// <summary>Click every uGUI button on the menu screens before a player exists, and report buttons that do nothing.</summary>
        public bool crawlMenus = true;
        public int maxMenuClicks = 200;
        public bool scanPlaceholderText = true;
        /// <summary>Report frame times above this for a full second. 0 = off. The first seconds after a load are ignored.</summary>
        public float fpsBudgetMs = 0f;
        /// <summary>Where run folders are written. Empty = &lt;project&gt;/PlayGoblinRuns in the editor, persistentDataPath in builds.</summary>
        public string outputRoot = "";
    }

    public enum PlayGoblinRunState { Idle, Starting, Running, Ended }

    /// <summary>Thread-safe view of the current or last run, for the local API and the editor window.</summary>
    public sealed class PlayGoblinRunSnapshot
    {
        public PlayGoblinRunState State;
        public string RunId;
        public string RunDirectory;
        public string EndReason;
        public double T;
        public long Events;
        public int Stucks;
        public int Falls;
        public int Errors;
        public float Coverage;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// One PlayGoblin run: finds the <see cref="PlayGoblinPlayer"/>, drives the <see cref="Explorer"/>, captures errors
    /// and writes &lt;runDir&gt;/events.jsonl plus a run.json summary when it ends.
    /// </summary>
    [AddComponentMenu("")]
    public sealed class PlayGoblinRun : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const string Version = "0.1.0-dev";

        static readonly object SnapGate = new object();
        static PlayGoblinRunSnapshot _snapshot = new PlayGoblinRunSnapshot { State = PlayGoblinRunState.Idle };

        public static PlayGoblinRun Current { get; private set; }
        public static event Action<PlayGoblinRun> Ended;

        public PlayGoblinRunOptions Options { get; private set; }
        public string RunId { get; private set; }
        public string RunDirectory { get; private set; }
        public string EndReason { get; private set; }
        public PlayGoblinRunState State { get; private set; }
        public double ElapsedGameSeconds =>
            State == PlayGoblinRunState.Idle ? 0 : State == PlayGoblinRunState.Ended ? _endT : Time.time - _startTime;

        PlayGoblinPlayer _player;
        Explorer _explorer;
        LogProbe _logs;
        Recorder _recorder;
        MenuCrawler _crawler;
        PlaceholderScanner _placeholders;
        readonly FpsWatch _fps = new FpsWatch();
        float _fpsIgnoreUntil;
        readonly FrameStats _frames = new FrameStats();
        StreamWriter _writer;
        float _startTime;
        float _startRealtime;
        float _endT;
        float _nextSnapshot;
        DateTime _startedUtc;
        string _scene;
        readonly Dictionary<string, int> _counts = new Dictionary<string, int>();

        public static PlayGoblinRunSnapshot Snapshot()
        {
            lock (SnapGate)
            {
                return new PlayGoblinRunSnapshot
                {
                    State = _snapshot.State, RunId = _snapshot.RunId, RunDirectory = _snapshot.RunDirectory,
                    EndReason = _snapshot.EndReason, T = _snapshot.T, Events = _snapshot.Events,
                    Stucks = _snapshot.Stucks, Falls = _snapshot.Falls, Errors = _snapshot.Errors, Coverage = _snapshot.Coverage
                };
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Start a run in the current play session. Stops any run already in progress.</summary>
        public static PlayGoblinRun StartRun(PlayGoblinRunOptions options)
        {
            if (!Application.isPlaying) throw new InvalidOperationException("PlayGoblin runs need Play Mode.");
            if (Current != null) Current.End("replaced");

            var go = new GameObject("[PlayGoblin Run]");
            DontDestroyOnLoad(go);
            var run = go.AddComponent<PlayGoblinRun>();
            run.Begin(options ?? new PlayGoblinRunOptions());
            return run;
        }

        public void Stop() => End("stopped");

        void Begin(PlayGoblinRunOptions options)
        {
            Options = options;
            Current = this;
            _startTime = Time.time;
            _startRealtime = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            _startedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
            _scene = SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path;
            RunId = $"{_startedUtc:yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}-s{options.seed}";

            var root = string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.outputRoot) ? DefaultOutputRoot() : options.outputRoot;
            RunDirectory = Path.Combine(root, RunId);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(RunDirectory);
            _writer = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"), false, new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(false));

            PlayGoblinEvents.Begin();
            _logs = new LogProbe();
            _recorder = new Recorder(options, RunDirectory);
            _crawler = options.crawlMenus ? new MenuCrawler(options) : null;
            _placeholders = options.scanPlaceholderText ? new PlaceholderScanner() : null;
            _fps.BudgetMs = options.fpsBudgetMs;
            _fpsIgnoreUntil = 5f;
            State = PlayGoblinRunState.Starting;
            SceneManager.activeSceneChanged += OnSceneChanged;

            PlayGoblinEvents.Add("run_start", null, null, new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                { "run_id", RunId },
                { "seed", options.seed.ToString() },
                { "minutes", options.minutes.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
                { "scene", _scene },
                { "unity", Application.unityVersion },
                { "playgoblin", Version }
            });
            UpdateSnapshot();
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (State != PlayGoblinRunState.Starting && State != PlayGoblinRunState.Running) return;

            var t = Time.time - _startTime;
            PlayGoblinEvents.CurrentTime = t;
            _frames.Add(Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
            var captured = _recorder != null && Time.frameCount - _recorder.LastCaptureFrame <= 1;
            if (_fps.Feed(Time.unscaledDeltaTime, t, t < _fpsIgnoreUntil || captured, out var drop))
            {
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("fps_drop", null, _player != null ? _player.Position : (Vector3?)null, new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    { "ms", drop.AverageMs.ToString("0.#", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
                    { "seconds", drop.Seconds.ToString("0.#", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
                    { "budget_ms", Options.fpsBudgetMs.ToString("0.#", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) }
                });
            }
            _placeholders?.Tick(Time.unscaledTime);

            if (State == PlayGoblinRunState.Starting)
            {
                _player = FindAnyObjectByType<PlayGoblinPlayer>();
                if (_player != null)
                {
                    _explorer = new Explorer(_player, Options);
                    State = PlayGoblinRunState.Running;
                    PlayGoblinEvents.Add("player_found", _player.name, _player.Position, new Dictionary<string, string>
                    {
                        { "mover", _player.MoverKind },
                        { "navmesh", _explorer.UsesNavMesh ? "true" : "false" },
                        { "reachable_cells", _explorer.Coverage.ReachableCells.ToString() }
                    });
                }
                else if (_crawler != null && _crawler.Tick(Time.unscaledTime))
                {
                    _startRealtime = Time.realtimeSinceStartup; // still working through menus: keep waiting for a player
                }
                else if (Time.realtimeSinceStartup - _startRealtime > Options.waitForPlayerSeconds)
                {
                    PlayGoblinEvents.Add("setup_error", "No PlayGoblinPlayer found. Add the PlayGoblin Player component to your player object.", null, null);
                    Flush();
                    End("no_player");
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (State == PlayGoblinRunState.Running)
            {
                if (_player == null)
                {
                    // Player destroyed (scene change, death that despawns): look for a new one next frame.
                    State = PlayGoblinRunState.Starting;
                    _startRealtime = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
                    PlayGoblinEvents.Add("player_lost", null, null, null);
                }
                else
                {
                    _explorer.Tick(t);
                }
            }

            Flush();
            _recorder?.Tick(this, t);
            if (Time.realtimeSinceStartup >= _nextSnapshot)
            {
                _nextSnapshot = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 0.25f;
                UpdateSnapshot();
            }

            if (t >= Options.minutes * 60f) End("completed");
        }

        void OnSceneChanged(Scene from, Scene to)
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.Add("scene_change", to.path, null, null);
            _scene = to.path;
            _fpsIgnoreUntil = (float)(Time.time - _startTime) + 5f;
            if (_explorer != null && _player != null) _explorer.Rebuild();
        }

        void Flush()
        {
            if (_writer == null) return;
            foreach (var e in PlayGoblinEvents.DrainPending())
            {
                _writer.WriteLine(e.ToJson());
                if (e.Type == "stuck" || e.Type == "out_of_world") _recorder?.Burst();
                _counts.TryGetValue(e.Type, out var n);
                _counts[e.Type] = n + 1;
            }
            _writer.Flush();
        }

        void End(string reason)
        {
            if (State == PlayGoblinRunState.Ended || State == PlayGoblinRunState.Idle) return;
            EndReason = reason;
            _endT = Time.time - _startTime;
            SceneManager.activeSceneChanged -= OnSceneChanged;

            var groups = _logs?.Groups() ?? new List<LogProbe.Group>();
            foreach (var g in groups)
            {
                PlayGoblinEvents.Add("error_summary", g.Message, g.FirstPosition, new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    { "hash", g.Hash },
                    { "kind", g.Kind },
                    { "count", g.Count.ToString() },
                    { "first_t", g.FirstT.ToString("0.###", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
                    { "last_t", g.LastT.ToString("0.###", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) }
                });
            }
            PlayGoblinEvents.Add("run_end", reason, null, null);
            Flush();
            _logs?.Dispose();
            _logs = null;
            _crawler?.Dispose();
            PlayGoblinEvents.End();

            WriteSummary(groups);
            _writer?.Dispose();
            _writer = null;

            State = PlayGoblinRunState.Ended;
            UpdateSnapshot();
            if (Current == this) Current = null;
            try { Ended?.Invoke(this); }
            catch (Exception ex) { Debug.LogException(ex); }

            if (Application.isPlaying) Destroy(gameObject);
        }

        void WriteSummary(List<LogProbe.Group> groups)
        {
            var w = new JsonWriter().BeginObject()
                .Prop("run_id", RunId)
                .Prop("playgoblin", Version)
                .Prop("unity", Application.unityVersion)
                .Prop("platform", Application.platform.ToString())
                .Prop("scene", _scene)
                .Prop("seed", (long)Options.seed)
                .Prop("planned_minutes", Options.minutes)
                .Prop("started_utc", _startedUtc.ToString("o"))
                .Prop("end_reason", EndReason)
                .Prop("game_seconds", (double)(Time.time - _startTime))
                .Prop("real_seconds", (double)(Time.realtimeSinceStartup - _startRealtime))
                .Prop("time_scale", Time.timeScale)
                .Prop("menu_clicks", (long)(_crawler?.Clicks ?? 0))
                .Prop("dead_buttons", (long)(_crawler?.DeadButtons ?? 0))
                .Prop("recorded_frames", (long)(_recorder?.FramesWritten ?? 0))
                .Prop("recorder_attempts", (long)(_recorder?.Attempts ?? 0))
                .Prop("recorder_problem", _recorder?.LastProblem)
                .Prop("recorder_off", _recorder != null && !_recorder.Enabled ? _recorder.DisabledReason : null)
                .Prop("mover", _player != null ? _player.MoverKind : null)
                .Prop("navmesh", _explorer != null && _explorer.UsesNavMesh)
                .Prop("grid", _explorer != null && _explorer.UsesGrid)
                .Prop("walkable_cells", (long)(_explorer?.WalkableCells ?? 0))
                .Prop("reachable_cells", (long)(_explorer?.ReachableCells ?? 0))
                .Prop("coverage", _explorer?.Coverage.Coverage ?? 0f)
                .Prop("distance_m", _explorer?.DistanceWalked ?? 0f)
                .Prop("stuck", (long)(_explorer?.Stucks ?? 0))
                .Prop("out_of_world", (long)(_explorer?.Falls ?? 0))
                .Prop("respawns", (long)(_explorer?.Respawns ?? 0))
                .Prop("waypoints", (long)(_explorer?.Waypoints ?? 0))
                .Raw("frames", _frames.ToJson());

            var counts = new JsonWriter().BeginObject();
            foreach (var kv in _counts) counts.Prop(kv.Key, (long)kv.Value);
            w.Raw("event_counts", counts.EndObject().ToString());

            w.BeginArray("errors");
            foreach (var g in groups)
            {
                var gw = new JsonWriter().BeginObject()
                    .Prop("hash", g.Hash).Prop("kind", g.Kind).Prop("message", g.Message)
                    .Prop("count", (long)g.Count).Prop("first_t", g.FirstT).Prop("last_t", g.LastT);
                if (g.FirstPosition.HasValue) gw.Prop("pos", g.FirstPosition.Value);
                w.Raw(null, gw.EndObject().ToString());
            }
            w.EndArray();

            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(RunDirectory, "run.json"), w.EndObject().ToString());
        }

        void UpdateSnapshot()
        {
            lock (SnapGate)
            {
                _snapshot = new PlayGoblinRunSnapshot
                {
                    State = State,
                    RunId = RunId,
                    RunDirectory = RunDirectory,
                    EndReason = EndReason,
                    T = ElapsedGameSeconds,
                    Events = PlayGoblinEvents.Count,
                    Stucks = _explorer?.Stucks ?? 0,
                    Falls = _explorer?.Falls ?? 0,
                    Errors = _logs?.Groups().Count ?? _snapshot.Errors,
                    Coverage = _explorer?.Coverage.Coverage ?? 0f
                };
            }
        }

        void OnApplicationQuit() => End("aborted");
        void OnDestroy() => End("aborted");

        static string DefaultOutputRoot()
        {
            return Application.isEditor
                ? Path.Combine(Directory.GetParent(Application.dataPath).FullName, "PlayGoblinRuns")
                : Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "PlayGoblinRuns");
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/Probes.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Captures errors and exceptions from any thread. Each distinct error (same message + stack) is logged
    /// once as an event; repeats are counted and reported in the run summary, so an error thrown
    /// 50 times a second doesn't flood the log.
    /// </summary>
    internal sealed class LogProbe : IDisposable
    {
        internal sealed class Group
        {
            public string Hash;
            public string Kind;      // "exception" or "error"
            public string Message;
            public int Count;
            public double FirstT;
            public double LastT;
            public Vector3? FirstPosition;
        }

        const int MaxStack = 4000;
        const int MaxMessage = 500;

        readonly object _gate = new object();
        readonly Dictionary<string, Group> _groups = new Dictionary<string, Group>();

        public LogProbe() { Application.logMessageReceivedThreaded += OnLog; }

        public void Dispose() { Application.logMessageReceivedThreaded -= OnLog; }

        void OnLog(string condition, string stackTrace, LogType type)
        {
            if (type != LogType.Exception && type != LogType.Error && type != LogType.Assert) return;
            if (!PlayGoblinEvents.Active) return;

            var kind = type == LogType.Exception ? "exception" : "error";
            var message = FirstLine(condition);
            if (message.Length > MaxMessage) message = message.Substring(0, MaxMessage);
            var hash = StableHash.Of(kind + "|" + message + "|" + stackTrace);
            var t = PlayGoblinEvents.CurrentTime;
            Vector3? pos = PlayGoblinEvents.HasPlayerPosition ? PlayGoblinEvents.LastPlayerPosition : (Vector3?)null;

            lock (_gate)
            {
                if (_groups.TryGetValue(hash, out var g))
                {
                    g.Count++;
                    g.LastT = t;
                    return;
                }
                _groups[hash] = new Group
                {
                    Hash = hash, Kind = kind, Message = message, Count = 1, FirstT = t, LastT = t, FirstPosition = pos
                };
            }

            var stack = stackTrace ?? "";
            if (stack.Length > MaxStack) stack = stack.Substring(0, MaxStack);
            PlayGoblinEvents.Add(kind, message, pos, new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                { "hash", hash },
                { "stack", stack }
            });
        }

        public List<Group> Groups()
        {
            lock (_gate) return new List<Group>(_groups.Values);
        }

        static string FirstLine(string s)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) return "";
            var i = s.IndexOf('\n');
            return (i >= 0 ? s.Substring(0, i) : s).TrimEnd('\r');
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Frame time statistics in unscaled milliseconds.</summary>
    internal sealed class FrameStats
    {
        const int Cap = 200_000;
        readonly List<float> _ms = new List<float>(4096);
        float _max;

        public void Add(float unscaledDeltaSeconds)
        {
            var ms = unscaledDeltaSeconds * 1000f;
            if (ms > _max) _max = ms;
            if (_ms.Count < Cap) _ms.Add(ms);
        }

        public int Frames => _ms.Count;

        public string ToJson()
        {
            if (_ms.Count == 0) return "{\"frames\":0}";
            var sorted = new List<float>(_ms);
            sorted.Sort();
            double sum = 0;
            foreach (var v in sorted) sum += v;
            var p95 = sorted[Mathf.Clamp((int)(sorted.Count * 0.95f), 0, sorted.Count - 1)];
            return new JsonWriter().BeginObject()
                .Prop("frames", (long)sorted.Count)
                .Prop("avg_ms", sum / sorted.Count)
                .Prop("p95_ms", p95)
                .Prop("max_ms", _max)
                .EndObject().ToString();
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/Recorder.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Saves downscaled JPEG screenshots to &lt;runDir&gt;/frames as f&lt;index&gt;_t&lt;game seconds&gt;.jpg.
    /// Captures at a low rate normally and at a high rate for a few seconds after a finding, so the report can show
    /// what happened around it. Needs a rendering device: under -batchmode -nographics it switches itself off.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class Recorder
    {
        readonly PlayGoblinRunOptions _options;
        readonly string _dir;
        float _nextCapture;
        float _burstUntil;
        int _index;
        bool _busy;
        float _busySince;
        int _generation;
        // End-of-frame capture includes UI overlays but never fires without a window (-batchmode). Then we render cameras.
        bool _useCameras;

        public bool Enabled { get; private set; }
        public string DisabledReason { get; private set; }
        public int FramesWritten => _index;
        public int Attempts { get; private set; }
        /// <summary>Time.frameCount of the latest capture, so frame-rate checks can ignore the frame it slowed down.</summary>
        public int LastCaptureFrame { get; private set; } = -100;
        public string LastProblem { get; private set; }

        public Recorder(PlayGoblinRunOptions options, string runDirectory)
        {
            _options = options;
            _dir = Path.Combine(runDirectory, "frames");
            if (!options.recordFrames) { DisabledReason = "disabled in options"; return; }
            if (SystemInfo.graphicsDeviceType == GraphicsDeviceType.Null)
            {
                DisabledReason = "no graphics device (running with -nographics)";
                return;
            }
            Enabled = true;
        }

        /// <summary>Capture at the higher rate for the next few seconds. Rates are per real second, so a sped-up game costs no more.</summary>
        public void Burst()
        {
            if (Enabled) _burstUntil = Time.unscaledTime + _options.burstSeconds;
        }

        /// <summary>Call every frame from the run's Update. Starts a capture when one is due.</summary>
        public void Tick(MonoBehaviour host, float gameTime)
        {
            if (!Enabled || _index >= _options.maxFrames) return;
            if (_busy)
            {
                if (Time.realtimeSinceStartup - _busySince < 0.5f) return;
                _generation++; // abandon the capture that never resumed
                _busy = false;
                _useCameras = true;
                LastProblem = "end-of-frame capture never fired; rendering cameras instead (UI overlays not included)";
            }
            var now = Time.unscaledTime;
            if (now < _nextCapture) return;
            LastCaptureFrame = Time.frameCount;
            var hz = now < _burstUntil ? _options.burstHz : _options.frameHz;
            _nextCapture = now + 1f / Mathf.Max(0.1f, hz);
            if (_useCameras) CaptureFromCameras(gameTime);
            else host.StartCoroutine(Capture(gameTime, _generation));
        }

        void CaptureFromCameras(float gameTime)
        {
            var cams = Camera.allCameras;
            if (cams.Length == 0) return;
            System.Array.Sort(cams, (a, b) => a.depth.CompareTo(b.depth));
            var first = cams[0];
            var aspect = first.pixelHeight > 0 ? first.pixelHeight / (float)first.pixelWidth : 9f / 16f;
            var width = _options.frameWidth;
            var height = Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.RoundToInt(width * aspect));
            var rt = RenderTexture.GetTemporary(width, height, 24, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            var prev = RenderTexture.active;
            Texture2D tex = null;
            try
            {
                foreach (var cam in cams)
                {
                    var old = cam.targetTexture;
                    cam.targetTexture = rt;
                    cam.Render();
                    cam.targetTexture = old;
                }
                RenderTexture.active = rt;
                tex = new Texture2D(width, height, TextureFormat.RGB24, false);
                tex.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0);
                tex.Apply();
                Write(tex, gameTime);
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                LastProblem = ex.GetType().Name + ": " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                RenderTexture.active = prev;
                RenderTexture.ReleaseTemporary(rt);
                if (tex != null) Object.Destroy(tex);
            }
        }

        void Write(Texture2D tex, float gameTime)
        {
            var bytes = tex.EncodeToJPG(_options.jpgQuality);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_dir);
            var name = $"f{_index:D5}_t{gameTime.ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}.jpg";
            File.WriteAllBytes(Path.Combine(_dir, name), bytes);
            _index++;
        }

        IEnumerator Capture(float gameTime, int generation)
        {
            _busy = true;
            _busySince = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            Attempts++;
            yield return new WaitForEndOfFrame();
            if (generation != _generation) yield break;
            Texture2D full = null, small = null;
            try
            {
                full = ScreenCapture.CaptureScreenshotAsTexture();
                if (full == null) throw new System.InvalidOperationException("ScreenCapture returned null");
                var tex = full;
                if (full.width > _options.frameWidth)
                {
                    small = Downscale(full, _options.frameWidth);
                    tex = small;
                }
                Write(tex, gameTime);
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                LastProblem = ex.GetType().Name + ": " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                if (full != null) Object.Destroy(full);
                if (small != null) Object.Destroy(small);
                _busy = false;
            }
        }

        static Texture2D Downscale(Texture2D src, int width)
        {
            var height = Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.RoundToInt(src.height * (width / (float)src.width)));
            var rt = RenderTexture.GetTemporary(width, height, 0, RenderTextureFormat.ARGB32);
            var prev = RenderTexture.active;
            Graphics.Blit(src, rt);
            RenderTexture.active = rt;
            var dst = new Texture2D(width, height, TextureFormat.RGB24, false);
            dst.ReadPixels(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 0, 0);
            dst.Apply();
            RenderTexture.active = prev;
            RenderTexture.ReleaseTemporary(rt);
            return dst;
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Runtime/WalkGrid.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin
{
    /// <summary>
    /// A walkability grid over the level, used when the scene has no NavMesh. Each cell is walkable when a probe
    /// finds ground there and the body fits. Gives A* paths around obstacles and the set of cells the player
    /// can really reach, so coverage and "trapped in a pocket" checks are accurate. Pure logic: ground and
    /// clearance come in as delegates, so it's unit-testable without a scene.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class WalkGrid
    {
        public const int MaxCells = 250_000;

        static readonly int[] Dx = { 1, -1, 0, 0, 1, 1, -1, -1 };
        static readonly int[] Dz = { 0, 0, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1, -1 };

        readonly int _nx, _nz;
        readonly float _minX, _minZ, _maxStep;
        readonly float[] _y;
        readonly bool[] _walk;
        bool[] _reach;
        List<Vector3> _reachList = new List<Vector3>();

        // A* scratch space, reused between calls.
        float[] _g;
        int[] _parent;
        int[] _stamp;
        int _stampNow;

        public float Cell { get; }
        public int WalkableCount { get; private set; }
        public int ReachableCount => _reachList.Count;
        public IReadOnlyList<Vector3> ReachableCells => _reachList;

        WalkGrid(Bounds b, float cell, float maxStep, int nx, int nz)
        {
            Cell = cell; _maxStep = maxStep; _nx = nx; _nz = nz;
            _minX = b.min.x; _minZ = b.min.z;
            _y = new float[nx * nz];
            _walk = new bool[nx * nz];
            _reach = new bool[nx * nz];
        }

        public static WalkGrid Build(Bounds bounds, float cell, float maxStep,
            Func<Vector3, Vector3?> ground, Func<Vector3, bool> clear)
        {
            cell = Mathf.Max(0.25f, cell);
            var sx = Mathf.Max(bounds.size.x, cell);
            var sz = Mathf.Max(bounds.size.z, cell);
            if (sx * sz / (cell * cell) > MaxCells) cell = Mathf.Sqrt(sx * sz / MaxCells);
            var nx = Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.CeilToInt(sx / cell));
            var nz = Mathf.Max(1, Mathf.CeilToInt(sz / cell));

            var g = new WalkGrid(bounds, cell, maxStep, nx, nz);
            for (int iz = 0; iz < nz; iz++)
            {
                for (int ix = 0; ix < nx; ix++)
                {
                    var from = new Vector3(g._minX + (ix + 0.5f) * cell, bounds.max.y + 1f, g._minZ + (iz + 0.5f) * cell);
                    var hit = ground(from);
                    if (!hit.HasValue || !clear(hit.Value)) continue;
                    var i = iz * nx + ix;
                    g._walk[i] = true;
                    g._y[i] = hit.Value.y;
                    g.WalkableCount++;
                }
            }
            return g;
        }

        /// <summary>Marks every cell connected to the cell nearest <paramref name="start"/>. Returns false if none is near.</summary>
        public bool FloodFrom(Vector3 start)
        {
            Array.Clear(_reach, 0, _reach.Length);
            _reachList = new List<Vector3>();
            if (!NearestWalkable(start, 4, false, out var s)) return false;

            var queue = new Queue<int>();
            _reach[s] = true;
            queue.Enqueue(s);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                var i = queue.Dequeue();
                var ix = i % _nx; var iz = i / _nx;
                for (int d = 0; d < 8; d++)
                {
                    if (!CanStep(ix, iz, d, out var j) || _reach[j]) continue;
                    _reach[j] = true;
                    queue.Enqueue(j);
                }
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < _reach.Length; i++) if (_reach[i]) _reachList.Add(CellCenter(i));
            return true;
        }

        public bool IsReachable(Vector3 p) => NearestWalkable(p, 0, true, out _);

        /// <summary>Shortest walkable path (A*), straight runs merged. Null when either end is unreachable.</summary>
        public List<Vector3> FindPath(Vector3 from, Vector3 to)
        {
            if (!NearestWalkable(from, 4, true, out var start)) return null;
            if (!NearestWalkable(to, 4, true, out var goal)) return null;
            if (start == goal) return new List<Vector3> { CellCenter(goal) };

            var n = _nx * _nz;
            if (_g == null) { _g = new float[n]; _parent = new int[n]; _stamp = new int[n]; }
            _stampNow++;
            var gx = goal % _nx; var gz = goal / _nx;

            var heap = new MinHeap();
            _g[start] = 0; _parent[start] = -1; _stamp[start] = _stampNow;
            heap.Push(Heuristic(start % _nx, start / _nx, gx, gz), start);
            var closed = new HashSet<int>();

            while (heap.Count > 0)
            {
                var cur = heap.Pop();
                if (cur == goal) return Reconstruct(cur);
                if (!closed.Add(cur)) continue;
                var ix = cur % _nx; var iz = cur / _nx;
                for (int d = 0; d < 8; d++)
                {
                    if (!CanStep(ix, iz, d, out var nb) || closed.Contains(nb)) continue;
                    var cost = _g[cur] + (d < 4 ? 1f : 1.4142135f);
                    if (_stamp[nb] == _stampNow && cost >= _g[nb]) continue;
                    _stamp[nb] = _stampNow; _g[nb] = cost; _parent[nb] = cur;
                    heap.Push(cost + Heuristic(nb % _nx, nb / _nx, gx, gz), nb);
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        List<Vector3> Reconstruct(int goal)
        {
            var cells = new List<int>();
            for (var i = goal; i >= 0; i = _parent[i]) cells.Add(i);
            cells.Reverse();

            // Drop the start cell and every node that continues in the same direction.
            var path = new List<Vector3>();
            for (int k = 1; k < cells.Count; k++)
            {
                var last = k == cells.Count - 1;
                if (!last)
                {
                    var dPrev = Dir(cells[k - 1], cells[k]);
                    var dNext = Dir(cells[k], cells[k + 1]);
                    if (dPrev == dNext) continue;
                }
                path.Add(CellCenter(cells[k]));
            }
            return path;
        }

        (int, int) Dir(int a, int b) => (b % _nx - a % _nx, b / _nx - a / _nx);

        static float Heuristic(int ax, int az, int bx, int bz)
        {
            var dx = Mathf.Abs(ax - bx); var dz = Mathf.Abs(az - bz);
            return Mathf.Max(dx, dz) + 0.4142135f * Mathf.Min(dx, dz);
        }

        bool CanStep(int ix, int iz, int d, out int j)
        {
            j = -1;
            var nx = ix + Dx[d]; var nz = iz + Dz[d];
            if (!Ok(ix, iz, nx, nz)) return false;
            if (d >= 4 && (!Ok(ix, iz, nx, iz) || !Ok(ix, iz, ix, nz))) return false;
            j = nz * _nx + nx;
            return true;
        }

        bool Ok(int ax, int az, int bx, int bz)
        {
            if (bx < 0 || bz < 0 || bx >= _nx || bz >= _nz) return false;
            var b = bz * _nx + bx;
            return _walk[b] && Mathf.Abs(_y[b] - _y[az * _nx + ax]) <= _maxStep;
        }

        bool NearestWalkable(Vector3 p, int radius, bool requireReachable, out int index)
        {
            index = -1;
            var cx = Mathf.FloorToInt((p.x - _minX) / Cell);
            var cz = Mathf.FloorToInt((p.z - _minZ) / Cell);
            var best = float.MaxValue;
            for (int dz = -radius; dz <= radius; dz++)
            {
                for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++)
                {
                    var ix = cx + dx; var iz = cz + dz;
                    if (ix < 0 || iz < 0 || ix >= _nx || iz >= _nz) continue;
                    var i = iz * _nx + ix;
                    if (!_walk[i] || (requireReachable && !_reach[i])) continue;
                    var c = CellCenter(i);
                    var dist = (c.x - p.x) * (c.x - p.x) + (c.z - p.z) * (c.z - p.z);
                    if (dist < best) { best = dist; index = i; }
                }
            }
            return index >= 0;
        }

        Vector3 CellCenter(int i) =>
            new Vector3(_minX + (i % _nx + 0.5f) * Cell, _y[i], _minZ + (i / _nx + 0.5f) * Cell);

        sealed class MinHeap
        {
            readonly List<(float key, int value)> _a = new List<(float, int)>();
            public int Count => _a.Count;

            public void Push(float key, int value)
            {
                _a.Add((key, value));
                var i = _a.Count - 1;
                while (i > 0)
                {
                    var p = (i - 1) / 2;
                    if (_a[p].key <= _a[i].key) break;
                    (_a[p], _a[i]) = (_a[i], _a[p]);
                    i = p;
                }
            }

            public int Pop()
            {
                var top = _a[0].value;
                var last = _a[_a.Count - 1];
                _a.RemoveAt(_a.Count - 1);
                if (_a.Count > 0)
                {
                    _a[0] = last;
                    var i = 0;
                    while (true)
                    {
                        var l = 2 * i + 1; var r = l + 1; var m = i;
                        if (l < _a.Count && _a[l].key < _a[m].key) m = l;
                        if (r < _a.Count && _a[r].key < _a[m].key) m = r;
                        if (m == i) break;
                        (_a[m], _a[i]) = (_a[i], _a[m]);
                        i = m;
                    }
                }
                return top;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```cs
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class StuckDetectorTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void Fires_WhenTryingToMoveButNotMoving()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 6f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, true);
            Assert.IsTrue(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DoesNotFire_WhenMoving()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 20f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, new Vector3(t * 2f, 0, 0), true);
            Assert.IsFalse(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DoesNotFire_WhenNotTryingToMove()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 20f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, false);
            Assert.IsFalse(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DoesNotFire_BeforeWindowElapses()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t < 4.5f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, true);
            Assert.IsFalse(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ResetsAfterFiring_SoItFiresAgainOnlyAfterAnotherWindow()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fires = 0;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 12f; t += 0.1f) if (d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, true)) fires++;
            Assert.AreEqual(2, fires);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SlowDrift_BelowMinDistance_StillCountsAsStuck()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 6f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, new Vector3(t * 0.02f, 0, 0), true);
            Assert.IsTrue(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void WalkingOutAndBack_ToTheSameSpot_IsNotStuck()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            // Triangle wave: 14 m out and 14 m back every 5 s, so the position after one window equals the start.
            for (float t = 0; t <= 12f; t += 0.1f)
            {
                var phase = t % 5f;
                var x = phase < 2.5f ? phase * 5.6f : (5f - phase) * 5.6f;
                fired |= d.Feed(t, new Vector3(x, 0, 0), true);
            }
            Assert.IsFalse(fired, "a round trip is progress, not a stall");
        }

        [Test]
        public void JitteringInPlace_WithinHalfAMeter_IsStuck()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t <= 6f; t += 0.1f)
                fired |= d.Feed(t, new Vector3(Mathf.Sin(t * 20f) * 0.1f, 0, Mathf.Cos(t * 20f) * 0.1f), true);
            Assert.IsTrue(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void PausingIntent_ResetsTheWindow()
        {
            var d = new StuckDetector { Window = 5f, MinDistance = 0.5f };
            var fired = false;
            for (float t = 0; t < 4f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, true);
            d.Feed(4f, Vector3.zero, false);
            for (float t = 4.1f; t < 8f; t += 0.1f) fired |= d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, true);
            Assert.IsFalse(fired, "window must restart after intent pauses");
        }
    }

    public class OutOfWorldDetectorTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void BelowKillY_Fires()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector { KillY = -10f };
            Assert.AreEqual("below_world", d.Feed(1f, new Vector3(0, -11f, 0), null));
        }

        [Test]
        public void AboveKillY_DoesNotFire()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector { KillY = -10f };
            Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(1f, new Vector3(0, -9f, 0), null));
        }

        [Test]
        public void NaN_IsInvalidPosition()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector();
            Assert.AreEqual("invalid_position", d.Feed(1f, new Vector3(float.NaN, 0, 0), null));
        }

        [Test]
        public void OffNavMesh_FiresOnlyAfterTheDelay()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector { OffNavMeshSeconds = 3f };
            Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(0f, Vector3.zero, false));
            Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(2.9f, Vector3.zero, false));
            Assert.AreEqual("off_navmesh", d.Feed(3.1f, Vector3.zero, false));
        }

        [Test]
        public void ReturningToNavMesh_ResetsTheTimer()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector { OffNavMeshSeconds = 3f };
            d.Feed(0f, Vector3.zero, false);
            d.Feed(2f, Vector3.zero, true);
            Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(4f, Vector3.zero, false));
            Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(6.5f, Vector3.zero, false));
            Assert.AreEqual("off_navmesh", d.Feed(7.1f, Vector3.zero, false));
        }

        [Test]
        public void UnknownNavMesh_NeverFiresOffNavMesh()
        {
            var d = new OutOfWorldDetector { OffNavMeshSeconds = 1f };
            for (float t = 0; t < 10f; t += 0.5f) Assert.IsNull(d.Feed(t, Vector3.zero, null));
        }
    }

    public class CoverageGridTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void Coverage_IsZero_WhenReachabilityUnknown()
        {
            var g = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            g.Visit(Vector3.zero);
            Assert.AreEqual(0f, g.Coverage);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Coverage_CountsVisitedReachableCells()
        {
            var g = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            g.MarkReachable(new Vector3(1, 0, 1));
            g.MarkReachable(new Vector3(3, 0, 1));
            g.MarkReachable(new Vector3(5, 0, 1));
            g.MarkReachable(new Vector3(7, 0, 1));
            g.Visit(new Vector3(1.5f, 0, 1));
            g.Visit(new Vector3(7.5f, 0, 1));
            Assert.AreEqual(0.5f, g.Coverage, 0.0001f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void VisitingUnreachableCells_DoesNotInflateCoverage()
        {
            var g = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            g.MarkReachable(new Vector3(1, 0, 1));
            g.Visit(new Vector3(100, 0, 100));
            Assert.AreEqual(0f, g.Coverage);
        }

        [Test]
        public void NegativeCoordinates_GetDistinctCells()
        {
            var g = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            Assert.AreNotEqual(g.Key(new Vector3(-1, 0, 1)), g.Key(new Vector3(1, 0, 1)));
            Assert.AreNotEqual(g.Key(new Vector3(1, 0, -1)), g.Key(new Vector3(1, 0, 1)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void Visits_CountsRepeats()
        {
            var g = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            g.Visit(new Vector3(1, 0, 1));
            g.Visit(new Vector3(1.2f, 0, 1.2f));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, g.Visits(new Vector3(0.5f, 0, 0.5f)));
        }
    }

    public class DestinationPickerTests
    {
        static Vector3? Flat(Vector3 from) => new Vector3(from.x, 0, from.z);

        [Test]
        public void SameSeed_GivesSamePoints()
        {
            var b = new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(100, 10, 100));
            var a = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(7, b, Flat);
            var c = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(7, b, Flat);
            for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) Assert.AreEqual(a.RandomPoint(), c.RandomPoint());
        }

        [Test]
        public void DifferentSeeds_GiveDifferentPoints()
        {
            var b = new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(100, 10, 100));
            var a = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(1, b, Flat).RandomPoint();
            var c = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(2, b, Flat).RandomPoint();
            Assert.AreNotEqual(a, c);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Points_StayInsideBounds()
        {
            var b = new Bounds(new Vector3(10, 0, -5), new Vector3(20, 4, 8));
            var p = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(3, b, Flat);
            for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++)
            {
                var pt = p.RandomPoint().Value;
                Assert.GreaterOrEqual(pt.x, b.min.x);
                Assert.LessOrEqual(pt.x, b.max.x);
                Assert.GreaterOrEqual(pt.z, b.min.z);
                Assert.LessOrEqual(pt.z, b.max.z);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void NoGround_ReturnsNull()
        {
            var b = new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(10, 10, 10));
            var p = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(3, b, _ => null);
            Assert.IsNull(p.RandomPoint());
        }

        [Test]
        public void Pick_PrefersLessVisitedCells()
        {
            var b = new Bounds(new Vector3(5, 0, 5), new Vector3(10, 4, 10));
            var grid = new CoverageGrid(2f);
            // Mark everything left of x=5 as heavily visited.
            for (float x = 0; x < 5f; x += 0.5f)
                for (float z = 0; z < 10f; z += 0.5f)
                    for (int k = 0; k < 5; k++) grid.Visit(new Vector3(x, 0, z));

            var picker = DestinationPicker.FromBounds(11, b, Flat);
            int right = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) if (picker.Pick(grid, 8).Value.x >= 5f) right++;
            Assert.Greater(right, 85, "picker should strongly favour unvisited cells");
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/EditMode/JsonAndHostTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Threading;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class JsonWriterTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void Object_WithMixedProps()
        {
            var s = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("a", "x").Prop("b", 1L).Prop("c", true).Prop("d", 1.5).EndObject().ToString();
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"a\":\"x\",\"b\":1,\"c\":true,\"d\":1.5}", s);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Strings_AreEscaped()
        {
            var s = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("m", "a\"b\\c\nd\te\u0001").EndObject().ToString();
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"m\":\"a\\\"b\\\\c\\nd\\te\\u0001\"}", s);
        }

        [Test]
        public void NullString_IsJsonNull()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"m\":null}", new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("m", (string)null).EndObject().ToString());
        }

        [Test]
        public void NaNAndInfinity_BecomeNull()
        {
            var s = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("a", double.NaN).Prop("b", double.PositiveInfinity).EndObject().ToString();
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"a\":null,\"b\":null}", s);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Vector3_IsArray()
        {
            var s = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("p", new Vector3(1, 2.5f, -3)).EndObject().ToString();
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"p\":[1,2.5,-3]}", s);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Arrays_AndNesting_UseCommasCorrectly()
        {
            var w = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("n", 1L).BeginArray("xs").Item("a").Item("b").EndArray().Prop("z", 2L).EndObject();
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"n\":1,\"xs\":[\"a\",\"b\"],\"z\":2}", w.ToString());
        }

        [Test]
        public void ArrayOfObjects_ViaRaw()
        {
            var w = new JsonWriter().BeginObject().BeginArray("xs");
            w.Raw(null, new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("i", 1L).EndObject().ToString());
            w.Raw(null, new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("i", 2L).EndObject().ToString());
            Assert.AreEqual("{\"xs\":[{\"i\":1},{\"i\":2}]}", w.EndArray().EndObject().ToString());
        }

        [Test]
        public void Numbers_UseInvariantCulture()
        {
            var old = Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture;
            try
            {
                Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = new CultureInfo("de-DE");
                Assert.AreEqual("{\"a\":1.5}", new JsonWriter().BeginObject().Prop("a", 1.5).EndObject().ToString());
            }
            finally { Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = old; }
        }

        [Test]
        public void StableHash_IsDeterministic_AndSensitive()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(StableHash.Of("abc"), StableHash.Of("abc"));
            Assert.AreNotEqual(StableHash.Of("abc"), StableHash.Of("abd"));
            Assert.AreEqual("e40c292c", StableHash.Of("a"), "FNV-1a 32-bit of \"a\"");
        }
    }

    public class EventsTests
    {
        [SetUp] public void SetUp() { PlayGoblinEvents.Begin(); }
        [TearDown] public void TearDown() { PlayGoblinEvents.End(); }

        [Test]
        public void Log_AssignsIncreasingSequenceNumbers()
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("a");
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("b");
            var all = PlayGoblinEvents.All();
            Assert.AreEqual(2, all.Count);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, all[0].Seq);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, all[1].Seq);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Log_IsIgnored_OutsideARun()
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.End();
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("x");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, PlayGoblinEvents.All().Count);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Since_ReturnsOnlyNewerEvents()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) PlayGoblinEvents.Log("e" + i);
            var newer = PlayGoblinEvents.Since(3);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, newer.Count);
            Assert.AreEqual("e3", newer[0].Type);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Since_RespectsMax()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) PlayGoblinEvents.Log("e");
            Assert.AreEqual(3, PlayGoblinEvents.Since(0, 3).Count);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DrainPending_ReturnsEachEventOnce()
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("a");
            Assert.AreEqual(1, PlayGoblinEvents.DrainPending().Count);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, PlayGoblinEvents.DrainPending().Count);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ToJson_IncludesPositionAndData()
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("stuck", "d", new Vector3(1, 2, 3), new Dictionary<string, string> { { "k", "v" } });
            var json = PlayGoblinEvents.All()[0].ToJson();
            StringAssert.Contains("\"type\":\"stuck\"", json);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"pos\":[1,2,3]", json);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"data\":{\"k\":\"v\"}", json);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Log_FromBackgroundThread_IsSafe()
        {
            var threads = new List<Thread>();
            for (int t = 0; t < 4; t++)
            {
                var th = new Thread(() => { for (int i = 0; i < 250; i++) PlayGoblinEvents.Log("bg"); });
                threads.Add(th);
                th.Start();
            }
            foreach (var th in threads) th.Join();
            var all = PlayGoblinEvents.All();
            Assert.AreEqual(1000, all.Count);
            var seen = new HashSet<long>();
            foreach (var e in all) Assert.IsTrue(seen.Add(e.Seq), "duplicate seq " + e.Seq);
        }
    }

    public class HostRouteTests
    {
        [SetUp] public void SetUp() { PlayGoblinEvents.Begin(); }
        [TearDown] public void TearDown() { PlayGoblinEvents.End(); }

        [Test]
        public void Status_IsValidJson_WithState()
        {
            var (code, json) = PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/status", "");
            Assert.AreEqual(200, code);
            var parsed = JsonUtility.FromJson<StatusDto>(json);
            Assert.IsNotNull(parsed.state);
            Assert.AreEqual(PlayGoblinRun.Version, parsed.playgoblin);
        }

        [Test]
        public void UnknownRoute_Is404()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(404, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/nope", "").Item1);
        }

        [Test]
        public void WrongMethod_Is404()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(404, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/run/start", "").Item1);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Events_SinceCursor_AndNext()
        {
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("a");
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("b");
            PlayGoblinEvents.Log("c");
            var (code, json) = PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/events?since=1&max=10", "");
            Assert.AreEqual(200, code);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"type\":\"b\"", json);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"type\":\"c\"", json);
            StringAssert.DoesNotContain("\"type\":\"a\"", json);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"next\":3", json);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Events_WithBadQuery_StillAnswers()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(200, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/events?since=abc&max=zzz", "").Item1);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Start_WithoutHandler_Is503()
        {
            var saved = PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler;
            try
            {
                PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = null;
                Assert.AreEqual(503, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("POST", "/run/start", "{}").Item1);
            }
            finally { PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = saved; }
        }

        [Test]
        public void Start_RejectsNonPositiveMinutes()
        {
            var saved = PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler;
            try
            {
                PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = _ => "running";
                Assert.AreEqual(400, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("POST", "/run/start", "{\"minutes\":0}").Item1);
            }
            finally { PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = saved; }
        }

        [Test]
        public void LatestRun_Is404_WhenNoRunFinished()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(404, PlayGoblinHost.Handle("GET", "/runs/latest", "").Item1);
        }

        [System.Serializable]
        class StatusDto { public string state; public string playgoblin; }
    }
}
```

```asmdef
{
  "name": "PlayGoblin.Tests.EditMode",
```

`unity/Tests/EditMode/WalkGridTests.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class WalkGridTests
    {
        static readonly Bounds Area = new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(20, 2, 20));

        static Vector3? Flat(Vector3 from) => new Vector3(from.x, 0, from.z);
        static bool AllClear(Vector3 p) => true;

        static WalkGrid Make(Func<Vector3, bool> clear, Func<Vector3, Vector3?> ground = null, float maxStep = 0.6f, Bounds? area = null)
            => WalkGrid.Build(area ?? Area, 1f, maxStep, ground ?? Flat, clear);

        [Test]
        public void OpenFloor_EveryWalkableCellIsReachable()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear);
            Assert.AreEqual(400, g.WalkableCount);
            Assert.IsTrue(g.FloodFrom(Vector3.zero));
            Assert.AreEqual(g.WalkableCount, g.ReachableCount);
        }

        [Test]
        public void BlockedCells_AreNotWalkable()
        {
            var g = Make(p => !(p.x > -0.6f && p.x < 0.6f));
            Assert.Less(g.WalkableCount, 400);
            Assert.AreEqual(360, g.WalkableCount, "cells at x=-0.5 and x=0.5 are both blocked");
        }

        [Test]
        public void WallWithGap_PathGoesThroughTheGap()
        {
            // Wall at x=0 from z=-10 to z=6; gap above z=6.
            bool Clear(Vector3 p) => !(Mathf.Abs(p.x) < 0.6f && p.z < 6f);
            var g = Make(Clear);
            g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0));
            var path = g.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 0, 0));
            Assert.IsNotNull(path);
            Assert.Greater(path.Count, 0);
            var maxZ = float.MinValue;
            foreach (var n in path)
            {
                Assert.IsTrue(Clear(n), "path node inside the wall: " + n);
                maxZ = Mathf.Max(maxZ, n.z);
            }
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(maxZ, 6f, "path must go up to the gap to get across");
            Assert.AreEqual(5f, path[path.Count - 1].x, 0.6f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void FullWall_IsolatesTheOtherSide()
        {
            var g = Make(p => Mathf.Abs(p.x) >= 0.6f);
            g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0));
            Assert.Less(g.ReachableCount, g.WalkableCount);
            Assert.IsNull(g.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 0, 0)));
            Assert.IsFalse(g.IsReachable(new Vector3(5, 0, 0)));
            Assert.IsTrue(g.IsReachable(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void StepsHigherThanMaxStep_BlockMovement()
        {
            Vector3? Ledge(Vector3 v) => new Vector3(v.x, v.x < 0 ? 0f : 1f, v.z);
            var tall = Make(AllClear, Ledge, 0.6f);
            tall.FloodFrom(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0));
            Assert.IsNull(tall.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 1, 0)), "1 m step is too high for 0.6 m");

            var ok = Make(AllClear, Ledge, 1.5f);
            ok.FloodFrom(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0));
            Assert.IsNotNull(ok.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 1, 0)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DiagonalMoves_CannotCutBlockedCorners()
        {
            // A checkerboard-style diagonal gap: (0,0) and (1,1) open but (1,0) and (0,1) blocked.
            bool Clear(Vector3 p)
            {
                var ix = Mathf.FloorToInt(p.x); var iz = Mathf.FloorToInt(p.z);
                return !((ix == 1 && iz == 0) || (ix == 0 && iz == 1));
            }
            var g = Make(Clear, area: new Bounds(new Vector3(1, 0, 1), new Vector3(2, 2, 2)));
            g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(0.5f, 0, 0.5f));
            Assert.AreEqual(1, g.ReachableCount, "diagonal step between two blocked orthogonals must be refused");
        }

        [Test]
        public void NoGround_MeansNothingWalkable()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear, _ => null);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, g.WalkableCount);
            Assert.IsFalse(g.FloodFrom(Vector3.zero));
        }

        [Test]
        public void StartingOutsideTheGrid_FindsNearbyCellOnlyWithinRadius()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear);
            Assert.IsTrue(g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(10.5f, 0, 0)), "just outside the edge snaps to the nearest cell");
            Assert.IsFalse(g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(100, 0, 100)), "far outside must not snap");
        }

        [Test]
        public void VeryLargeLevels_AreCoarsenedToTheCellCap()
        {
            var g = WalkGrid.Build(new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(5000, 2, 5000)), 1f, 0.6f, Flat, AllClear);
            Assert.LessOrEqual(g.WalkableCount, WalkGrid.MaxCells + 2000);
            Assert.Greater(g.Cell, 1f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void StraightRuns_AreMergedIntoOneSegment()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear);
            g.FloodFrom(Vector3.zero);
            var path = g.FindPath(new Vector3(-8, 0, 0.5f), new Vector3(8, 0, 0.5f));
            Assert.IsNotNull(path);
            Assert.LessOrEqual(path.Count, 2);
        }

        [Test]
        public void PathToTheSameCell_IsASingleNode()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear);
            g.FloodFrom(Vector3.zero);
            var path = g.FindPath(new Vector3(0.2f, 0, 0.2f), new Vector3(0.3f, 0, 0.3f));
            Assert.AreEqual(1, path.Count);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Paths_AreNearOptimal()
        {
            var g = Make(AllClear);
            g.FloodFrom(Vector3.zero);
            var from = new Vector3(-8.5f, 0, -8.5f);
            var path = g.FindPath(from, new Vector3(8.5f, 0, 8.5f));
            var len = 0f; var prev = new Vector3(-8.5f, 0, -8.5f);
            foreach (var n in path) { len += Vector3.Distance(prev, n); prev = n; }
            Assert.AreEqual(Vector3.Distance(from, new Vector3(8.5f, 0, 8.5f)), len, 0.5f, "open floor diagonal should be a straight line");
        }

        [Test]
        public void RepeatedPathQueries_GiveTheSameAnswer()
        {
            bool Clear(Vector3 p) => !(Mathf.Abs(p.x) < 0.6f && p.z < 6f);
            var g = Make(Clear);
            g.FloodFrom(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0));
            var a = g.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 0, 0));
            var b = g.FindPath(new Vector3(-5, 0, 0), new Vector3(5, 0, 0));
            Assert.AreEqual(a.Count, b.Count);
            for (int i = 0; i < a.Count; i++) Assert.AreEqual(a[i], b[i]);
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/EditMode/Week3Tests.cs`:

```cs
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class FpsWatchTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void IsOff_WithoutABudget()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 0f };
            for (float t = 0; t < 5f; t += 0.2f) Assert.IsFalse(w.Feed(0.2f, t, false, out _));
        }

        [Test]
        public void Fires_AfterASecondOfSlowFrames_WithTheAverage()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 33f };
            var fired = false;
            FpsWatch.Drop drop = default;
            for (float t = 0; t < 2f && !fired; t += 0.1f) fired = w.Feed(0.1f, t, false, out drop);
            Assert.IsTrue(fired);
            Assert.AreEqual(100f, drop.AverageMs, 0.5f);
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(drop.Seconds, 1f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SingleSpike_DoesNotFire_AndAGoodFrameResetsTheWindow()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 33f };
            Assert.IsFalse(w.Feed(0.5f, 1f, false, out _));
            Assert.IsFalse(w.Feed(0.01f, 1.5f, false, out _));
            Assert.IsFalse(w.Feed(0.6f, 2f, false, out _));
        }

        [Test]
        public void IgnoredFrames_NeverCount()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 33f };
            for (float t = 0; t < 5f; t += 0.1f) Assert.IsFalse(w.Feed(0.1f, t, true, out _));
        }

        [Test]
        public void IgnoredFramesInTheMiddle_DoNotBreakARunOfSlowFrames()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 33f };
            var fired = false;
            var i = 0;
            for (float t = 0; t < 3f && !fired; t += 0.1f, i++) fired = w.Feed(0.1f, t, ignore: i % 3 == 0, out _);
            Assert.IsTrue(fired);
        }

        [Test]
        public void OneLongStutter_IsReportedOnce()
        {
            var w = new FpsWatch { BudgetMs = 33f };
            var count = 0;
            for (float t = 0; t < 4f; t += 0.1f) if (w.Feed(0.1f, t, false, out _)) count++;
            Assert.AreEqual(1, count);
        }
    }

    public class UiRulesTests
    {
        [TestCase("TODO: real title", true)]
        [TestCase("Lorem ipsum dolor", true)]
        [TestCase("New Text", true)]
        [TestCase("Press START", false)]
        [TestCase("Octodon", false)]
        [TestCase("", false)]
        [TestCase(null, false)]
        public void Placeholder(string text, bool expected) => Assert.AreEqual(expected, PlaceholderRules.IsPlaceholder(text));

        [TestCase("Quit", true)]
        [TestCase("Exit to desktop", true)]
        [TestCase("Delete save", true)]
        [TestCase("Play", false)]
        [TestCase("Options", false)]
        public void DangerousButtons(string label, bool expected) => Assert.AreEqual(expected, MenuCrawler.IsDangerous(label));
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/PlayMode/ApiAndLongWalkTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class ApiAndLongWalkTests
    {
        readonly List<GameObject> _spawned = new List<GameObject>();
        string _outRoot;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            _outRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-tests-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            PlayGoblinHost.Stop();
            PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = null;
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            foreach (var go in _spawned) if (go != null) Object.Destroy(go);
            _spawned.Clear();
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            try { if (Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        GameObject Box(string name, Vector3 center, Vector3 size)
        {
            var b = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            b.name = name;
            b.transform.position = center;
            b.transform.localScale = size;
            _spawned.Add(b);
            return b;
        }

        GameObject Player(Vector3 at)
        {
            var p = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            p.name = "Player";
            Object.Destroy(p.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            p.AddComponent<CharacterController>();
            p.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>().moveSpeed = 6f;
            p.transform.position = at;
            _spawned.Add(p);
            return p;
        }

        static IEnumerator Await<T>(Task<T> task)
        {
            var end = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 10f;
            while (!task.IsCompleted && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < end)
            {
                PlayGoblinHost.Pump();
                yield return null;
            }
            Assert.IsTrue(task.IsCompleted, "HTTP request timed out");
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator HttpApi_StartsARun_AndReportsItsProgress()
        {
            Box("Floor", new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0), new Vector3(40, 1, 40));
            Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 4f;

            PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = o => { o.outputRoot = _outRoot; PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(o); return "running"; };
            Assert.IsTrue(PlayGoblinHost.Start(0), PlayGoblinHost.LastError);
            var baseUrl = $"http://127.0.0.1:{PlayGoblinHost.Port}";

            using (var http = new HttpClient())
            {
                var status = http.GetAsync(baseUrl + "/status");
                yield return Await(status);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.OK, status.Result.StatusCode);
                StringAssert.Contains("\"state\":\"idle\"", status.Result.Content.ReadAsStringAsync().Result);

                var missing = http.GetAsync(baseUrl + "/nope");
                yield return Await(missing);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.NotFound, missing.Result.StatusCode);

                var bad = http.PostAsync(baseUrl + "/run/start", new StringContent("{\"minutes\":-1}"));
                yield return Await(bad);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, bad.Result.StatusCode);

                var start = http.PostAsync(baseUrl + "/run/start", new StringContent("{\"seed\":5,\"minutes\":0.1}"));
                yield return Await(start);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.Accepted, start.Result.StatusCode);
                StringAssert.Contains("running", start.Result.Content.ReadAsStringAsync().Result);

                var deadline = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 30f;
                string body = "";
                while (Time.realtimeSinceStartup < deadline)
                {
                    var s = http.GetStringAsync(baseUrl + "/status");
                    yield return Await(s);
                    body = s.Result;
                    if (body.Contains("\"state\":\"ended\"")) break;
                    yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.2f);
                }
                StringAssert.Contains("\"state\":\"ended\"", body);
                StringAssert.Contains("\"end_reason\":\"completed\"", body);

                var events = http.GetStringAsync(baseUrl + "/events?since=0&max=5");
                yield return Await(events);
                StringAssert.Contains("\"type\":\"run_start\"", events.Result);
                StringAssert.Contains("\"next\":5", events.Result);

                var latest = http.GetAsync(baseUrl + "/runs/latest");
                yield return Await(latest);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.OK, latest.Result.StatusCode);
                StringAssert.Contains("\"seed\":5", latest.Result.Content.ReadAsStringAsync().Result);

                var stop = http.PostAsync(baseUrl + "/run/stop", new StringContent(""));
                yield return Await(stop);
                StringAssert.Contains("idle", stop.Result.Content.ReadAsStringAsync().Result);
            }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator HttpApi_Start_RejectsMalformedBody_WithoutCrashingTheHost()
        {
            Box("Floor", new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0), new Vector3(40, 1, 40));
            Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;
            PlayGoblinHost.StartHandler = o => "running";
            Assert.IsTrue(PlayGoblinHost.Start(0), PlayGoblinHost.LastError);
            var baseUrl = $"http://127.0.0.1:{PlayGoblinHost.Port}";

            using (var http = new HttpClient())
            {
                var bad = http.PostAsync(baseUrl + "/run/start", new StringContent("{not json"));
                yield return Await(bad);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, bad.Result.StatusCode);

                var ok = http.GetAsync(baseUrl + "/status");
                yield return Await(ok);
                Assert.AreEqual(System.Net.HttpStatusCode.OK, ok.Result.StatusCode, "host must survive a bad request");
            }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TenMinuteWalk_OnAnObstacleCourse_CompletesWithGoodCoverage()
        {
            // 60x60 floor with a wall cross, pillars and a ledge-sized step; open lanes connect every quadrant.
            Box("Floor", new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0), new Vector3(60, 1, 60));
            Box("WallN", new Vector3(0, 1.5f, 8), new Vector3(2, 3, 20));
            Box("WallS", new Vector3(0, 1.5f, -8), new Vector3(2, 3, 20));
            Box("WallE", new Vector3(8, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(20, 3, 2));
            Box("WallW", new Vector3(-8, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(20, 3, 2));
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) Box("Pillar" + i, new Vector3(-20 + i * 8, 1.5f, 18), new Vector3(1.5f, 3, 1.5f));
            Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 20f;

            var options = new PlayGoblinRunOptions { seed = 7, minutes = 10f, outputRoot = _outRoot, positionHz = 1f };
            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(options);
            var deadline = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 120f;
            while (PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < deadline) yield return null;

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            Assert.AreEqual(PlayGoblinRunState.Ended, snap.State, "10-minute walk did not finish");
            Assert.AreEqual("completed", snap.EndReason);
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(snap.T, 599f, "should cover the full 10 game minutes");

            var summaryText = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            var summary = JsonUtility.FromJson<Summary>(summaryText);
            Debug.Log("TENMIN-SUMMARY " + summaryText);
            Assert.LessOrEqual(summary.stuck, 1, "an open course should produce (almost) no false stuck reports");
            Assert.Greater(summary.coverage, 0.6f, "should visit most of the reachable floor");
            Assert.Greater(summary.distance_m, 500f, "player should actually walk a long way");
            Assert.Greater(summary.waypoints, 20);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, summary.out_of_world, "open course has no falls");

            var eventsFile = Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl");
            var size = new FileInfo(eventsFile).Length;
            Assert.Less(size, 2_000_000, "event log should stay small for a 10-minute run");
            foreach (var line in File.ReadLines(eventsFile)) Assert.IsNotNull(JsonUtility.FromJson<Line>(line), line);
        }

        [System.Serializable]
        class Summary { public float coverage; public float distance_m; public int waypoints; public int stuck; public int out_of_world; }

        [System.Serializable]
        class Line { public string type; }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/PlayMode/FpsRunTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class FpsRunTests
    {
        sealed class Stutter : MonoBehaviour
        {
            public int sleepMs = 60;
            void Update() => Thread.Sleep(sleepMs);
        }

        string _outRoot;
        GameObject _stutter;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp() => _outRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-fps-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            if (_stutter != null) Object.Destroy(_stutter);
            try { if (Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        string[] Run(float fpsBudgetMs, float seconds, out PlayGoblinRun run)
        {
            run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions
            {
                seed = 1, minutes = seconds / 60f, outputRoot = _outRoot, waitForPlayerSeconds = 60f,
                recordFrames = false, crawlMenus = false, fpsBudgetMs = fpsBudgetMs
            });
            return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator SlowFrames_AfterTheLoadGracePeriod_AreReported_WhenABudgetIsSet()
        {
            _stutter = new GameObject("Stutter", typeof(Stutter));
            Run(33f, 12f, out var run);
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended) yield return null;
            var lines = File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
            var drops = lines.Where(l => l.Contains("\"fps_drop\"")).ToArray();
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(drops.Length, 1, string.Join("\n", lines));
            Assert.IsTrue(drops.All(l => l.Contains("\"budget_ms\":\"33\"")));
            var firstT = double.Parse(System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(drops[0], "\"t\":([0-9.]+)").Groups[1].Value,
                System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(firstT, 5.0, "the first 5 s are ignored");
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NoBudget_NoFpsEvents()
        {
            _stutter = new GameObject("Stutter", typeof(Stutter));
            Run(0f, 8f, out var run);
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended) yield return null;
            var text = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
            StringAssert.DoesNotContain("fps_drop", text);
        }
    }
}
```
