`unity/Tests/PlayMode/MenuCrawlerTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class MenuCrawlerTests
    {
        string _outRoot;
        GameObject _canvas;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            _outRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-menu-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true;
            _canvas = new GameObject("Canvas", typeof(Canvas));
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            Object.Destroy(_canvas);
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = false;
            try { if (Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        Button AddButton(string label, UnityAction onClick = null, Transform parent = null)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(label, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image), typeof(Button));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent != null ? parent : _canvas.transform, false);
            var text = new GameObject("Label", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Text));
            text.transform.SetParent(go.transform, false);
            text.GetComponent<Text>().text = label;
            var b = go.GetComponent<Button>();
            if (onClick != null) b.onClick.AddListener(onClick);
            return b;
        }

        Text AddText(string content)
        {
            var go = new GameObject("Title", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Text));
            go.transform.SetParent(_canvas.transform, false);
            var t = go.GetComponent<Text>();
            t.text = content;
            return t;
        }

        IEnumerator Run(System.Action<string[]> done)
        {
            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions
            {
                seed = 1, minutes = 1f, outputRoot = _outRoot, waitForPlayerSeconds = 3f, recordFrames = false
            });
            var start = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup - start < 60f) yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual(PlayGoblinRunState.Ended, run.State, "run should end by itself (no player -> setup_error)");
            done(File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl")));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Crawler_ClicksButtons_ReportsOnlyTheDeadOne_AndNeverQuits()
        {
            var panel = new GameObject("Panel", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
            panel.transform.SetParent(_canvas.transform, false);
            panel.SetActive(false);
            var quitClicked = false;
            var playClicks = 0;
            AddButton("Play", () => { playClicks++; panel.SetActive(true); });
            AddButton("Dead");
            AddButton("Quit", () => quitClicked = true);

            string[] lines = null;
            yield return Run(l => lines = l);

            Assert.IsFalse(quitClicked, "Quit must never be clicked");
            Assert.AreEqual(1, playClicks, "a button that changes the screen is clicked once");
            var dead = lines.Where(l => l.Contains("\"dead_button\"")).ToArray();
            Assert.AreEqual(1, dead.Length, string.Join("\n", lines));
            StringAssert.Contains("Dead", dead[0]);
            Assert.IsTrue(lines.Any(l => l.Contains("\"ui_click\"") && l.Contains("Play") && l.Contains("\"changed\"")));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Crawler_FollowsAButtonThatOpensAScreen_AndFindsThePlaceholderTextThere()
        {
            var sub = new GameObject("Options", typeof(RectTransform));
            sub.transform.SetParent(_canvas.transform, false);
            sub.SetActive(false);
            var main = new GameObject("Main", typeof(RectTransform));
            main.transform.SetParent(_canvas.transform, false);
            AddButton("Options", () => { main.SetActive(false); sub.SetActive(true); }, main.transform);
            AddButton("Apply", null, sub.transform);
            var t = new GameObject("Heading", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Text));
            t.transform.SetParent(sub.transform, false);
            t.GetComponent<Text>().text = "TODO: write options";

            string[] lines = null;
            yield return Run(l => lines = l);

            Assert.IsTrue(lines.Any(l => l.Contains("\"placeholder_text\"") && l.Contains("TODO")), string.Join("\n", lines));
            Assert.IsTrue(lines.Any(l => l.Contains("\"dead_button\"") && l.Contains("Apply")), "second screen was crawled");
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Crawler_CanBeTurnedOff()
        {
            var clicked = false;
            AddButton("Play", () => clicked = true);
            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions
            {
                seed = 1, minutes = 1f, outputRoot = _outRoot, waitForPlayerSeconds = 2f, crawlMenus = false, recordFrames = false
            });
            var start = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup - start < 30f) yield return null;
            Assert.IsFalse(clicked);
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/PlayMode/NavMeshTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class NavMeshTests
    {
        readonly List<GameObject> _spawned = new List<GameObject>();
        NavMeshDataInstance _navInstance;
        string _outRoot;
        string _keep;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            // PLAYGOBLIN_KEEP_RUNS=<dir> keeps the run folders so the CLI can be tried on real output.
            _keep = System.Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_KEEP_RUNS");
            _outRoot = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_keep)
                ? _keep
                : Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-tests-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true;
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            foreach (var go in _spawned) if (go != null) Object.Destroy(go);
            _spawned.Clear();
            if (_navInstance.valid) NavMesh.RemoveNavMeshData(_navInstance);
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = false;
            try { if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_keep) && Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        GameObject Box(string name, Vector3 center, Vector3 size)
        {
            var b = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            b.name = name;
            b.transform.position = center;
            b.transform.localScale = size;
            _spawned.Add(b);
            return b;
        }

        /// <summary>Bakes a NavMesh from every collider in the scene, the same way a level's baked NavMesh would exist at runtime.</summary>
        void BakeNavMesh(Bounds bounds)
        {
            var sources = new List<NavMeshBuildSource>();
            NavMeshBuilder.CollectSources(bounds, ~0, NavMeshCollectGeometry.PhysicsColliders, 0,
                new List<NavMeshBuildMarkup>(), sources);
            var settings = NavMesh.GetSettingsByID(0);
            var data = NavMeshBuilder.BuildNavMeshData(settings, sources, bounds, Vector3.zero, Quaternion.identity);
            Assert.IsNotNull(data, "NavMesh bake failed");
            _navInstance = NavMesh.AddNavMeshData(data);
        }

        GameObject AgentPlayer(Vector3 at, float speed = 6f)
        {
            var p = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            p.name = "Player";
            Object.Destroy(p.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            p.transform.position = at;
            var agent = p.AddComponent<NavMeshAgent>();
            agent.speed = speed;
            agent.acceleration = 40f;
            p.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>();
            _spawned.Add(p);
            return p;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NavMeshAgent_WalksABakedLevel_WithGoodCoverageAndNoFalseReports()
        {
            Box("Floor", new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0), new Vector3(60, 1, 60));
            Box("WallN", new Vector3(0, 1.5f, 8), new Vector3(2, 3, 20));
            Box("WallS", new Vector3(0, 1.5f, -8), new Vector3(2, 3, 20));
            Box("WallE", new Vector3(8, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(20, 3, 2));
            Box("WallW", new Vector3(-8, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(20, 3, 2));
            yield return null;
            Physics.SyncTransforms();
            BakeNavMesh(new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(80, 20, 80)));

            var player = AgentPlayer(new Vector3(-20, 0, -20));
            yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 10f;

            var options = new PlayGoblinRunOptions { seed = 3, minutes = 5f, outputRoot = _outRoot, positionHz = 1f };
            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(options);
            var deadline = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 120f;
            while (PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < deadline) yield return null;

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            Assert.AreEqual(PlayGoblinRunState.Ended, snap.State, "NavMesh walk did not finish");
            Assert.AreEqual("completed", snap.EndReason);

            var text = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            Debug.Log("NAVMESH-SUMMARY " + text);
            var s = JsonUtility.FromJson<Summary>(text);
            Assert.IsTrue(text.Contains("\"navmesh\":true"), "run should have used the NavMesh code path");
            Assert.LessOrEqual(s.stuck, 1, "open NavMesh level should produce (almost) no stuck reports");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, s.out_of_world, "agent on a NavMesh should never fall or leave it");
            Assert.Greater(s.distance_m, 150f, "agent should really walk");
            Assert.Greater(s.waypoints, 8);
            Assert.Greater(s.coverage, 0.4f);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NavMeshAgent_PlacedOffTheNavMesh_IsReportedAndRespawned()
        {
            Box("Floor", new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0), new Vector3(40, 1, 40));
            yield return null;
            Physics.SyncTransforms();
            BakeNavMesh(new Bounds(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(60, 20, 60)));

            var player = AgentPlayer(Vector3.zero);
            yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 5f;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions { seed = 1, minutes = 3f, outputRoot = _outRoot, positionHz = 1f });
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(2f);

            // Pull the agent off the mesh the way a glitch would: disable it and drop it far outside.
            player.GetComponent<NavMeshAgent>().enabled = false;
            player.transform.position = new Vector3(200, 0, 200);

            var deadline = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 60f;
            while (PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < deadline) yield return null;

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var text = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            Debug.Log("NAVMESH-OFF-SUMMARY " + text);
            var s = JsonUtility.FromJson<Summary>(text);
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(s.out_of_world, 1, "leaving the NavMesh should be reported");
            var events = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
            StringAssert.Contains("\"type\":\"out_of_world\"", events);
        }

        [System.Serializable]
        class Summary { public float coverage; public float distance_m; public int waypoints; public int stuck; public int out_of_world; }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/PlayMode/RecorderTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class RecorderTests
    {
        string _outRoot;
        GameObject _floor, _player, _cam;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            _outRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-rec-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            _floor = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            _floor.transform.position = new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0);
            _floor.transform.localScale = new Vector3(30, 1, 30);
            _player = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            Object.Destroy(_player.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            _player.AddComponent<CharacterController>();
            _player.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>().moveSpeed = 5f;
            _player.transform.position = new Vector3(0, 1, 0);
            _cam = new GameObject("Cam", typeof(Camera));
            _cam.transform.position = new Vector3(0, 12, -14);
            _cam.transform.LookAt(Vector3.zero);
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            Object.Destroy(_floor); Object.Destroy(_player); Object.Destroy(_cam);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(System.Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_KEEP_RUNS"))) return;
            try { if (Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Recorder_WritesNamedJpegFrames_AndReportsTheCount()
        {
            if (SystemInfo.graphicsDeviceType == GraphicsDeviceType.Null)
                Assert.Ignore("needs a rendering device (run without -nographics)");

            var keep = System.Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PLAYGOBLIN_KEEP_RUNS");
            var root = string.IsNullOrEmpty(keep) ? _outRoot : keep;
            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions { seed = 3, minutes = 6f / 60f, outputRoot = root, frameHz = 4f });
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended) yield return null;

            var frames = Directory.GetFiles(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "frames"), "*.jpg").Select(Path.GetFileName).OrderBy(n => n).ToArray();
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(frames.Length, 12, "6 s at 4 fps");
            StringAssert.IsMatch(@"^f00000_t\d+\.\d\d\.jpg$", frames[0]);
            var tex = new Texture2D(2, 2);
            Assert.IsTrue(tex.LoadImage(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "frames", frames[0]))));
            Assert.LessOrEqual(tex.width, 640);
            Assert.Greater(tex.width, 100);
            var summary = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            StringAssert.Contains("\"recorded_frames\":" + frames.Length, summary.Replace(" ", ""));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Recorder_CanBeTurnedOff()
        {
            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(new PlayGoblinRunOptions { seed = 3, minutes = 2f / 60f, outputRoot = _outRoot, recordFrames = false });
            while (run.State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended) yield return null;
            Assert.IsFalse(Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "frames")));
            StringAssert.Contains("disabled in options", File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(run.RunDirectory, "run.json")));
        }
    }
}
```

`unity/Tests/PlayMode/WalkTests.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace PlayGoblin.Tests
{
    public class WalkTests
    {
        readonly List<GameObject> _spawned = new List<GameObject>();
        string _outRoot;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            _outRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "playgoblin-tests-" + System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            Time.timeScale = 4f;
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true; // tests deliberately log errors
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            if (PlayGoblinRun.Current != null) PlayGoblinRun.Current.Stop();
            foreach (var go in _spawned) if (go != null) Object.Destroy(go);
            _spawned.Clear();
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = false;
            try { if (Directory.Exists(_outRoot)) Directory.Delete(_outRoot, true); } catch { /* best effort */ }
        }

        GameObject Floor(float size = 40f)
        {
            var f = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            f.name = "Floor";
            f.transform.position = new Vector3(0, -0.5f, 0);
            f.transform.localScale = new Vector3(size, 1f, size);
            _spawned.Add(f);
            return f;
        }

        GameObject Player(Vector3 at)
        {
            var p = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            p.name = "Player";
            Object.Destroy(p.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            p.AddComponent<CharacterController>();
            p.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>().moveSpeed = 6f;
            p.transform.position = at;
            _spawned.Add(p);
            return p;
        }

        GameObject Wall(Vector3 center, Vector3 size)
        {
            var w = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            w.name = "Wall";
            w.transform.position = center;
            w.transform.localScale = size;
            _spawned.Add(w);
            return w;
        }

        PlayGoblinRunOptions Options(float gameSeconds, int seed = 1) => new PlayGoblinRunOptions
        {
            seed = seed, minutes = gameSeconds / 60f, stuckSeconds = 2f, outputRoot = _outRoot, waitForPlayerSeconds = 2f
        };

        IEnumerator WaitForEnd(float realSeconds = 60f)
        {
            var end = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + realSeconds;
            while (PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().State != PlayGoblinRunState.Ended && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < end) yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual(PlayGoblinRunState.Ended, PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().State, "run did not finish in time");
        }

        static List<EventLine> ReadEvents(string runDir)
        {
            var list = new List<EventLine>();
            foreach (var line in File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(runDir, "events.jsonl")))
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line)) continue;
                var e = JsonUtility.FromJson<EventLine>(line);
                Assert.IsNotNull(e, "line is not valid JSON: " + line);
                Assert.IsNotEmpty(e.type, "line has no type: " + line);
                list.Add(e);
            }
            return list;
        }

        [System.Serializable]
        class EventLine { public long seq; public double t; public string type; public string detail; }

        static int Count(List<EventLine> events, string type) => events.FindAll(e => e.type == type).Count;

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator WalksAFlatFloor_AndWritesAValidEventLog()
        {
            Floor(); Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(12f));
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            Assert.AreEqual("completed", snap.EndReason);
            var events = ReadEvents(snap.RunDirectory);
            Assert.AreEqual("run_start", events[0].type);
            Assert.AreEqual("run_end", events[events.Count - 1].type);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, Count(events, "player_found"));
            Assert.Greater(Count(events, "waypoint"), 1, "should have chosen several waypoints");
            Assert.Greater(Count(events, "pos"), 5, "should sample positions");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, Count(events, "stuck"), "open floor must not report stuck");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, Count(events, "out_of_world"));

            for (int i = 1; i < events.Count; i++) Assert.Greater(events[i].seq, events[i - 1].seq, "seq must increase");

            var summary = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            StringAssert.Contains("\"end_reason\":\"completed\"", summary);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"mover\":\"CharacterController\"", summary);
            Assert.IsNotNull(JsonUtility.FromJson<SummaryLine>(summary));
            Assert.Greater(JsonUtility.FromJson<SummaryLine>(summary).distance_m, 5f, "player should have actually walked");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, JsonUtility.FromJson<SummaryLine>(summary).stuck);
        }

        [System.Serializable]
        class SummaryLine { public string run_id; public float distance_m; public int stuck; public int out_of_world; public int respawns; public int walkable_cells; public int reachable_cells; }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator PlayerWalledIn_IsReportedTrapped_WithoutSpammingEvents()
        {
            Floor(60f);
            Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            // Closed 3x3 box around the player; the rest of the floor is unreachable from inside.
            foreach (var (c, s) in new[] {
                (new Vector3(2, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(0.5f, 3, 4.5f)), (new Vector3(-2, 1.5f, 0), new Vector3(0.5f, 3, 4.5f)),
                (new Vector3(0, 1.5f, 2), new Vector3(4.5f, 3, 0.5f)), (new Vector3(0, 1.5f, -2), new Vector3(4.5f, 3, 0.5f)) })
                Wall(c, s);
            yield return null;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(20f));
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var events = ReadEvents(snap.RunDirectory);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, Count(events, "trapped"), "a walled-in player must be reported as trapped, once");
            Assert.Less(Count(events, "waypoint"), 120, "tiny reachable area must not flood the log");
            var summary = JsonUtility.FromJson<SummaryLine>(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json")));
            Assert.Less(summary.reachable_cells, summary.walkable_cells / 5);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator WallAppearingMidRun_IsReportedStuck()
        {
            Floor(40f);
            var player = Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(40f, 3));
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
            // Not known to the planner (built at run start), so paths through it get blocked.
            Wall(new Vector3(0, 1.5f, 5), new Vector3(60, 3, 1));
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var events = ReadEvents(snap.RunDirectory);
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(Count(events, "stuck"), 1, "walking into an unmapped wall must be reported as stuck");
            Assert.AreEqual(snap.Stucks, Count(events, "stuck"));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator FallingBelowTheWorld_IsReported_AndPlayerIsRespawned()
        {
            Floor(); var p = Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(15f));
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(1.5f);
            var cc = p.GetComponent<CharacterController>();
            cc.enabled = false; p.transform.position = new Vector3(0, -500f, 0); cc.enabled = true;
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var events = ReadEvents(snap.RunDirectory);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, Count(events, "out_of_world"));
            Assert.GreaterOrEqual(Count(events, "respawn"), 1);
            Assert.Greater(p.transform.position.y, -5f, "player must be back above the world");
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator RepeatedError_IsLoggedOnce_AndCountedInSummary()
        {
            Floor(); Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true;
            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(6f));
            yield return null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) Debug.LogError("boom: same every time");
            Debug.LogError("a different error");
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            var events = ReadEvents(snap.RunDirectory);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, Count(events, "error"), "one event per distinct error");
            var summary = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "run.json"));
            StringAssert.Contains("\"count\":50", summary);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Exception_IsCaptured_WithStack()
        {
            Floor(); Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            LogAssert.ignoreFailingMessages = true;
            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(5f));
            yield return null;
            Debug.LogException(new System.InvalidOperationException("kaboom"));
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var events = ReadEvents(PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().RunDirectory);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, Count(events, "exception"));
            var raw = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
            StringAssert.Contains("kaboom", raw);
            StringAssert.Contains("\"stack\":", raw);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NoPlayer_EndsWithClearSetupError()
        {
            Floor();
            yield return null;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(30f));
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            var snap = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot();
            Assert.AreEqual("no_player", snap.EndReason);
            var raw = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(snap.RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
            StringAssert.Contains("setup_error", raw);
            StringAssert.Contains("PlayGoblin Player", raw);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Stop_EndsTheRunEarly()
        {
            Floor(); Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            var run = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(600f));
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(1f);
            run.Stop();
            yield return WaitForEnd(5f);

            Assert.AreEqual("stopped", PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().EndReason);
            Assert.IsNull(PlayGoblinRun.Current);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator StartingASecondRun_ReplacesTheFirst()
        {
            Floor(); Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(600f, 1));
            var firstDir = PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().RunDirectory;
            yield return null;
            var second = PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(3f, 2));
            Assert.AreSame(second, PlayGoblinRun.Current);
            yield return WaitForEnd();

            StringAssert.Contains("\"end_reason\":\"replaced\"", File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(firstDir, "run.json")));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator SameSeed_PicksTheSameFirstWaypoint()
        {
            Floor(); var p = Player(new Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            yield return null;

            var firsts = new List<string>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                var cc = p.GetComponent<CharacterController>();
                cc.enabled = false; p.transform.position = new Vector3(0, 1, 0); cc.enabled = true;
                yield return null;
                PlayGoblinRun.StartRun(Options(2f, 99));
                yield return WaitForEnd();
                var raw = File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(PlayGoblinRun.Snapshot().RunDirectory, "events.jsonl"));
                firsts.Add(System.Array.Find(raw, l => l.Contains("\"type\":\"waypoint\"")));
                yield return null;
            }
            Assert.IsNotNull(firsts[0]);
            var strip = new System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex("\"seq\":\\d+,\"t\":[\\d.]+,");
            Assert.AreEqual(strip.Replace(firsts[0], ""), strip.Replace(firsts[1], ""));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Mover_IsChosenFromTheComponentsOnThePlayer()
        {
            Floor();
            var p = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Capsule);
            Object.Destroy(p.GetComponent<CapsuleCollider>());
            var rb = p.AddComponent<Rigidbody>();
            rb.constraints = RigidbodyConstraints.FreezeRotation;
            p.AddComponent<CapsuleCollider>();
            var bw = p.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>();
            p.transform.position = new Vector3(0, 1, 0);
            _spawned.Add(p);
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual("Rigidbody", bw.MoverKind);

            Object.Destroy(rb);
            yield return null;
            var fresh = p.AddComponent<PlayGoblinPlayer>();
            Assert.IsNull(fresh, "DisallowMultipleComponent must reject a second PlayGoblinPlayer");
        }
    }
}
```
