`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinEvents.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"

DECLARE_LOG_CATEGORY_EXTERN(LogPlayGoblin, Log, All);

namespace PlayGoblin
{
	inline FString JsonEscape(const FString& In)
	{
		FString Out;
		Out.Reserve(In.Len() + 8);
		for (TCHAR C : In)
		{
			switch (C)
			{
			case TEXT('"'): Out += TEXT("\\\""); break;
			case TEXT('\\'): Out += TEXT("\\\\"); break;
			case TEXT('\n'): Out += TEXT("\\n"); break;
			case TEXT('\r'): Out += TEXT("\\r"); break;
			case TEXT('\t'): Out += TEXT("\\t"); break;
			default:
				if (C < 0x20) Out += FString::Printf(TEXT("\\u%04x"), static_cast<int32>(C));
				else Out.AppendChar(C);
			}
		}
		return Out;
	}

	inline FString Num(double V) { return FString::SanitizeFloat(V); }

	/** Unreal is Z-up in centimetres. Run folders use metres and Y-up (X, height, Z) so the CLI reads them like Unity runs. */
	inline FString PosJson(const FVector& Cm)
	{
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("[%s,%s,%s]"), *Num(FMath::RoundToDouble(Cm.X) / 100.0),
			*Num(FMath::RoundToDouble(Cm.Z) / 100.0), *Num(FMath::RoundToDouble(Cm.Y) / 100.0));
	}

	inline FString MetresCsv(const FVector& Cm)
	{
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("%.2f,%.2f,%.2f"), Cm.X / 100.0, Cm.Z / 100.0, Cm.Y / 100.0);
	}

	/** FNV-1a, 8 hex digits: stable across runs, used to group identical errors. */
	inline FString StableHash(const FString& S)
	{
		uint32 H = 2166136261u;
		for (TCHAR C : S) { H ^= static_cast<uint32>(C); H *= 16777619u; }
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("%08x"), H);
	}
}

struct FPGEvent
{
	int64 Seq = 0;
	double T = 0.0;
	FString Type;
	FString Detail;
	bool bHasPos = false;
	FVector Pos = FVector::ZeroVector;
	TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Data;

	FString ToJson() const
	{
		FString J = FString::Printf(TEXT("{\"seq\":%lld,\"t\":%s,\"type\":\"%s\""), Seq, *PlayGoblin::Num(FMath::RoundToDouble(T * 1000.0) / 1000.0),
			*PlayGoblin::JsonEscape(Type));
		if (!Detail.IsEmpty()) J += FString::Printf(TEXT(",\"detail\":\"%s\""), *PlayGoblin::JsonEscape(Detail));
		if (bHasPos) J += TEXT(",\"pos\":") + PlayGoblin::PosJson(Pos);
		if (Data.Num() > 0)
		{
			J += TEXT(",\"data\":{");
			for (int32 i = 0; i < Data.Num(); ++i)
			{
				if (i) J += TEXT(",");
				J += FString::Printf(TEXT("\"%s\":\"%s\""), *PlayGoblin::JsonEscape(Data[i].Key), *PlayGoblin::JsonEscape(Data[i].Value));
			}
			J += TEXT("}");
		}
		return J + TEXT("}");
	}
};

```

`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinMenus.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "PlayGoblinMenus.h"

#include "Blueprint/UserWidget.h"
#include "Blueprint/WidgetTree.h"
#include "Components/Button.h"
#include "Components/PanelWidget.h"
#include "Components/TextBlock.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameViewportClient.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerController.h"
#include "HAL/PlatformTime.h"
#include "Internationalization/Regex.h"
#include "UObject/UObjectIterator.h"

namespace
{
	bool ChainVisible(const UWidget* W)
	{
		for (const UWidget* C = W; C; C = C->GetParent())
			if (!C->IsVisible()) return false;
		return true;
	}

	/** Text of the first UTextBlock under a widget (depth first). */
	bool FirstText(UWidget* W, FString& Out)
	{
		if (UTextBlock* T = Cast<UTextBlock>(W)) { Out = T->GetText().ToString(); return true; }
		if (UPanelWidget* P = Cast<UPanelWidget>(W))
			for (int32 i = 0; i < P->GetChildrenCount(); ++i)
				if (FirstText(P->GetChildAt(i), Out)) return true;
		return false;
	}

	void Collect(UUserWidget* Root, TArray<UWidget*>& Out, int32 Depth = 0)
	{
		if (!Root || !Root->WidgetTree || Depth > 8) return;
		TArray<UWidget*> All;
		Root->WidgetTree->GetAllWidgets(All);
		for (UWidget* W : All)
		{
			Out.Add(W);
			if (UUserWidget* Nested = Cast<UUserWidget>(W)) Collect(Nested, Out, Depth + 1);
		}
	}

	/** Visible widgets in the viewport owned by this world, sorted by name so the crawl order is deterministic. */
	TArray<UUserWidget*> ViewportRoots(UWorld* World)
	{
		TArray<UUserWidget*> Roots;
		for (TObjectIterator<UUserWidget> It; It; ++It)
		{
			UUserWidget* W = *It;
			if (!W || W->IsTemplate() || !IsValid(W) || W->GetWorld() != World || !W->IsInViewport() || !W->IsVisible()) continue;
			Roots.Add(W);
		}
		Roots.Sort([](const UUserWidget& A, const UUserWidget& B) { return A.GetName() < B.GetName(); });
		return Roots;
	}

	FString OwnerClass(const UWidget* W)
	{
		const UObject* Outer = W->GetOuter();
		while (Outer && !Outer->IsA<UUserWidget>()) Outer = Outer->GetOuter();
		return Outer ? Outer->GetClass()->GetName() : TEXT("Widget");
	}

	bool IsPlaceholder(const FString& Text)
	{
		static const FRegexPattern Words(TEXT("(?i)\\b(todo|tbd|lorem|placeholder|fixme)\\b"));
		static const FRegexPattern Defaults(TEXT("(?i)^\\s*(text block|button)\\s*$"));
		FRegexMatcher A(Words, Text);
		if (A.FindNext()) return true;
		FRegexMatcher B(Defaults, Text);
		return B.FindNext();
	}

	uint64 Mix(uint64 H, uint64 V) { return (H ^ V) * 1099511628211ull; }
}

uint64 FPGMenuCrawler::Signature(UWorld* World) const
{
	uint64 H = 1469598103934665603ull;
	H = Mix(H, reinterpret_cast<uint64>(World));
	H = Mix(H, GetTypeHash(World->GetMapName()));
	H = Mix(H, World->IsPaused() ? 1 : 0);
	if (APlayerController* PC = World->GetFirstPlayerController()) H = Mix(H, PC->bShowMouseCursor ? 1 : 0);
	if (UGameViewportClient* VC = World->GetGameViewport()) H = Mix(H, (VC->IgnoreInput() ? 2 : 0) | (static_cast<uint64>(VC->GetMouseCaptureMode()) << 4));
	for (UUserWidget* Root : ViewportRoots(World))
	{
		H = Mix(H, reinterpret_cast<uint64>(Root));
		TArray<UWidget*> All;
		Collect(Root, All);
		for (UWidget* W : All)
			H = Mix(Mix(H, reinterpret_cast<uint64>(W)), (W->IsVisible() ? 1 : 0) | (W->GetIsEnabled() ? 2 : 0) | (static_cast<uint64>(W->GetVisibility()) << 4));
	}
	return H;
}

void FPGMenuCrawler::Scan(UWorld* World)
{
	for (UUserWidget* Root : ViewportRoots(World))
	{
		TArray<UWidget*> All;
		Collect(Root, All);
		for (UWidget* W : All)
		{
			if (!ChainVisible(W)) continue;
			const FString Path = OwnerClass(W) + TEXT("/") + W->GetName();
			if (UTextBlock* T = Cast<UTextBlock>(W))
			{
				const FString Text = T->GetText().ToString();
				if (IsPlaceholder(Text) && !PlaceholderSeen.Contains(Path + TEXT("|") + Text))
				{
					PlaceholderSeen.Add(Path + TEXT("|") + Text);
					Emit(TEXT("placeholder_text"), Text, { { TEXT("path"), Path } });
				}
			}
			else if (UButton* B = Cast<UButton>(W))
			{
				if (Clicked.Contains(Path) || Skipped.Contains(Path) || !B->GetIsEnabled()) continue;
				if (B->GetVisibility() == ESlateVisibility::HitTestInvisible || B->GetVisibility() == ESlateVisibility::SelfHitTestInvisible) continue;
				FString Label;
				FirstText(B, Label);
				const FString Lower = Label.ToLower();
				if (Lower.Contains(TEXT("quit")) || Lower.Contains(TEXT("exit")))
				{
					Skipped.Add(Path);
					Emit(TEXT("menu_skip"), Label, { { TEXT("path"), Path }, { TEXT("reason"), TEXT("quit_or_exit") } });
					continue;
				}
				bool bQueued = false;
				for (const FQueued& Q : Queue) bQueued |= Q.Path == Path;
				if (!bQueued) Queue.Add({ B, Path, Label });
			}
		}
	}
}

void FPGMenuCrawler::Tick(UWorld* World)
{
	if (!World) return;
	const double Now = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
	if (bWaiting)
	{
		if (Signature(World) != BaseSig) { bWaiting = false; NextScan = Now + 0.5; }
		else if (Now >= Deadline)
		{
			bWaiting = false;
			Emit(TEXT("dead_button"), WaitLabel, { { TEXT("path"), WaitPath }, { TEXT("label"), WaitLabel }, { TEXT("scene"), WaitScene } });
		}
		return;
	}
	if (Queue.Num() == 0)
	{
		if (Now < NextScan) return;
		NextScan = Now + 2.0;
		Scan(World);
	}
	while (Queue.Num() > 0)
	{
		if (ClickCount >= MaxClicks)
		{
			if (!bLimitReported) { bLimitReported = true; Emit(TEXT("menu_skip"), TEXT("click limit"), { { TEXT("reason"), TEXT("max_clicks") } }); }
			Queue.Reset();
			return;
		}
		FQueued Q = Queue[0];
		Queue.RemoveAt(0);
		UButton* B = Q.Button.Get();
		if (!B || !IsValid(B) || !ChainVisible(B) || !B->GetIsEnabled()) continue; // gone since the scan (e.g. an earlier click closed the menu)
		BaseSig = Signature(World);
		WaitPath = Q.Path; WaitLabel = Q.Label; WaitScene = World->GetMapName();
		Clicked.Add(Q.Path);
		ClickCount++;
		B->OnClicked.Broadcast();
		Deadline = Now + 1.0;
		bWaiting = true;
		return;
	}
}

```

`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinMenus.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"

class UWorld;
class UButton;

/**
 * Crawls visible UMG menus like the Unity MenuCrawler: clicks each enabled button once (max 30 per run, never Quit/Exit),
 * reports buttons that change nothing within 1 s as dead_button, and visible placeholder text as placeholder_text.
 * Clicks are simulated with UButton::OnClicked.Broadcast(): the game's click handler runs, but Slate hit testing,
 * hover/press styles and the input stack are not exercised.
 */
class FPGMenuCrawler
{
public:
	using FEmit = TFunction<void(const FString& Type, const FString& Detail, TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Data)>;

	explicit FPGMenuCrawler(FEmit InEmit, int32 InMaxClicks = 30) : Emit(MoveTemp(InEmit)), MaxClicks(InMaxClicks) {}

	void Tick(UWorld* World);
	int32 Clicks() const { return ClickCount; }

private:
	void Scan(UWorld* World);
	uint64 Signature(UWorld* World) const;

	FEmit Emit;
	int32 MaxClicks;
	int32 ClickCount = 0;
	double NextScan = 0;

	bool bWaiting = false;
	uint64 BaseSig = 0;
	double Deadline = 0;
	FString WaitPath, WaitLabel, WaitScene;

	struct FQueued { TWeakObjectPtr<UButton> Button; FString Path, Label; };
	TArray<FQueued> Queue;
	TSet<FString> Clicked, Skipped, PlaceholderSeen;
	bool bLimitReported = false;
};

```

`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinModule.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "Containers/Ticker.h"
#include "Dom/JsonObject.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "HttpPath.h"
#include "HttpServerModule.h"
#include "HttpServerResponse.h"
#include "IHttpRouter.h"
#include "Misc/CommandLine.h"
#include "Misc/Parse.h"
#include "Misc/Paths.h"
#include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"
#include "PlayGoblinRun.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonReader.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonSerializer.h"

/**
 * Nothing runs unless the game is started with -playgoblin, so shipping a build with the plugin enabled is harmless.
 *
 *   -playgoblin                 start the local API on 127.0.0.1:47321 (override with -PlayGoblinPort=)
 *   -PlayGoblinSeconds=120      also start a run by itself once a game world exists
 *   -PlayGoblinSeed= -PlayGoblinOut=<folder> -PlayGoblinTimeScale= -PlayGoblinFpsBudgetMs= -PlayGoblinExit
 */
class FPlayGoblinModule : public IModuleInterface
{
public:
	virtual void StartupModule() override
	{
		if (!FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("playgoblin"))) return;
		int32 Port = 47321;
		FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinPort="), Port);
		StartServer(Port);

		float Seconds = 0.f;
		if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinSeconds="), Seconds) && Seconds > 0.f)
		{
			Auto.Minutes = Seconds / 60.f;
			FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinSeed="), Auto.Seed);
			FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinOut="), Auto.OutputRoot);
			FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinTimeScale="), Auto.TimeScale);
			FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinFpsBudgetMs="), Auto.FpsBudgetMs);
			Auto.bExitWhenDone = FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinExit"));
			Auto.bUseKeys = FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinKeys"));
			FParse::Bool(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("PlayGoblinMenus="), Auto.bMenus);
			if (FParse::Param(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("NoPlayGoblinMenus"))) Auto.bMenus = false;
			bAutoPending = true;
		}
		Ticker = FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().AddTicker(FTickerDelegate::CreateRaw(this, &FPlayGoblinModule::Tick));
	}

	virtual void ShutdownModule() override
	{
		if (Ticker.IsValid()) FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().RemoveTicker(Ticker);
		Current.Reset();
	}

private:
	FTSTicker::FDelegateHandle Ticker;
	TUniquePtr<FPlayGoblinRun> Current;
	FPGSnapshot LastSnapshot;
	FString LastSummaryPath;
	FPlayGoblinOptions Auto;
	bool bAutoPending = false;

	static UWorld* FindGameWorld()
	{
		if (!GEngine) return nullptr;
		for (const FWorldContext& C : GEngine->GetWorldContexts())
			if ((C.WorldType == EWorldType::Game || C.WorldType == EWorldType::PIE) && C.World() && C.World()->HasBegunPlay())
				return C.World();
		return nullptr;
	}

	bool Tick(float)
	{
		UWorld* World = FindGameWorld();
		if (!World) return true;
		if (bAutoPending)
		{
			bAutoPending = false;
			StartRun(Auto);
		}
		if (Current)
		{
			Current->Tick(World);
			if (Current->GetState() == EPGState::Ended)
			{
				LastSnapshot = Current->Snapshot();
				LastSummaryPath = FPaths::Combine(Current->RunDirectory, TEXT("run.json"));
				const bool bExit = Current->bExitWhenDone();
				Current.Reset();
				if (bExit) FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	void StartRun(const FPlayGoblinOptions& Options)
	{
		if (Current) Current->Stop();
		Current = MakeUnique<FPlayGoblinRun>(Options);
		Current->Begin();
	}

	static FString StateName(EPGState S)
	{
		switch (S)
		{
		case EPGState::Starting: return TEXT("starting");
		case EPGState::Running: return TEXT("running");
		case EPGState::Ended: return TEXT("ended");
		default: return TEXT("idle");
		}
	}

	FString StatusJson()
	{
		const FPGSnapshot S = Current ? Current->Snapshot() : LastSnapshot;
		using namespace PlayGoblin;
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("{\"state\":\"%s\",\"run_id\":\"%s\",\"run_dir\":\"%s\",\"end_reason\":\"%s\",\"t\":%s,\"events\":%lld,\"stuck\":%d,\"out_of_world\":%d,\"errors\":%d,\"coverage\":%s}"),
			*StateName(S.State), *JsonEscape(S.RunId), *JsonEscape(S.RunDir), *JsonEscape(S.EndReason), *Num(S.T), S.Events, S.Stucks, S.Falls, S.Errors, *Num(S.Coverage));
	}

	static TUniquePtr<FHttpServerResponse> Json(const FString& Body, EHttpServerResponseCodes Code = EHttpServerResponseCodes::Ok)
	{
		TUniquePtr<FHttpServerResponse> R = FHttpServerResponse::Create(Body, TEXT("application/json"));
		R->Code = Code;
		return R;
	}

	void StartServer(int32 Port)
	{
		TSharedPtr<IHttpRouter> Router = FHttpServerModule::Get().GetHttpRouter(Port, true);
		if (!Router.IsValid())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogPlayGoblin, Error, TEXT("Could not open port %d for the PlayGoblin API."), Port);
			return;
		}
		auto Bind = [&](const TCHAR* Path, EHttpServerRequestVerbs Verb, TFunction<TUniquePtr<FHttpServerResponse>(const FHttpServerRequest&)> Fn)
		{
			Router->BindRoute(FHttpPath(Path), Verb, FHttpRequestHandler::CreateLambda(
				[Fn](const FHttpServerRequest& Req, const FHttpResultCallback& Done) { Done(Fn(Req)); return true; }));
		};
		Bind(TEXT("/status"), EHttpServerRequestVerbs::VERB_GET, [this](const FHttpServerRequest&) { return Json(StatusJson()); });
		Bind(TEXT("/events"), EHttpServerRequestVerbs::VERB_GET, [this](const FHttpServerRequest& Req)
		{
			const FString* SinceS = Req.QueryParams.Find(TEXT("since"));
			const FString* MaxS = Req.QueryParams.Find(TEXT("max"));
			const int64 Since = SinceS ? FCString::Atoi64(**SinceS) : 0;
			const int32 Max = MaxS ? FMath::Clamp(FCString::Atoi(**MaxS), 1, 5000) : 1000;
			return Json(Current ? Current->EventsJson(Since, Max) : TEXT("{\"events\":[],\"next\":0}"));
		});
		Bind(TEXT("/runs/latest"), EHttpServerRequestVerbs::VERB_GET, [this](const FHttpServerRequest&)
		{
			FString Text;
			if (LastSummaryPath.IsEmpty() || !FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *LastSummaryPath))
				return Json(TEXT("{\"error\":\"no_run\"}"), EHttpServerResponseCodes::NotFound);
			return Json(Text);
		});
		Bind(TEXT("/run/start"), EHttpServerRequestVerbs::VERB_POST, [this](const FHttpServerRequest& Req)
		{
			FPlayGoblinOptions O;
			FString Body = FString(UTF8_TO_TCHAR(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(Req.Body.GetData()))).Left(Req.Body.Num());
			TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Obj;
			if (!Body.IsEmpty() && FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Body), Obj) && Obj.IsValid())
			{
				Obj->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("seed"), O.Seed);
				double Minutes = O.Minutes;
				Obj->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("minutes"), Minutes);
				O.Minutes = static_cast<float>(Minutes);
			}
			if (O.Minutes <= 0.f) return Json(TEXT("{\"error\":\"minutes must be > 0\"}"), EHttpServerResponseCodes::BadRequest);
			StartRun(O);
			return Json(TEXT("{\"state\":\"starting\"}"), EHttpServerResponseCodes::Accepted);
		});
		Bind(TEXT("/run/stop"), EHttpServerRequestVerbs::VERB_POST, [this](const FHttpServerRequest&)
		{
			if (Current) Current->Stop();
			return Json(StatusJson());
		});
		FHttpServerModule::Get().StartAllListeners();
		UE_LOG(LogPlayGoblin, Display, TEXT("PlayGoblin API listening on port %d"), Port);
	}
};

IMPLEMENT_MODULE(FPlayGoblinModule, PlayGoblin)

```

`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinRun.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "PlayGoblinRun.h"
#include "PlayGoblinMenus.h"

#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Engine/StaticMesh.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Components/PrimitiveComponent.h"
#include "Materials/Material.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInterface.h"
#include "EngineUtils.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/Character.h"
#include "GameFramework/CharacterMovementComponent.h"
#include "Components/CapsuleComponent.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerController.h"
#include "GameFramework/WorldSettings.h"
#include "HAL/FileManager.h"
#include "HAL/PlatformMisc.h"
#include "HAL/PlatformTime.h"
#include "Misc/App.h"
#include "Misc/EngineVersion.h"
#include "HAL/PlatformProperties.h"
#include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
#include "Misc/Paths.h"
#include "NavigationPath.h"
#include "NavigationSystem.h"
#include "UnrealClient.h"
#include "InputKeyEventArgs.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"
#include "GameFramework/PlayerInput.h"
#include "Components/InputComponent.h"

DEFINE_LOG_CATEGORY(LogPlayGoblin);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- log capture

void FPGLogCapture::Serialize(const TCHAR* V, ELogVerbosity::Type Verbosity, const FName& Category)
{
	if (Category == TEXT("LogPlayGoblin")) return;
	const bool bScript = Category == TEXT("LogScript") || Category == TEXT("LogBlueprintUserMessages");
	const bool bError = Verbosity == ELogVerbosity::Error || Verbosity == ELogVerbosity::Fatal;
	if (!bError && !(bScript && Verbosity == ELogVerbosity::Warning)) return;

	FString Line(V);
	int32 NewLine;
	if (Line.FindChar(TEXT('\n'), NewLine)) Line.LeftInline(NewLine);
	Line.TrimEndInline();

	FString Normalized;
	for (TCHAR C : Line) if (!FChar::IsDigit(C)) Normalized.AppendChar(C);
	const FString Kind = bScript ? TEXT("exception") : TEXT("error");
	const FString Hash = PlayGoblin::StableHash(Kind + TEXT("|") + Category.ToString() + TEXT("|") + Normalized);

	FScopeLock Lock(&Gate);
	if (FGroup* G = Map.Find(Hash))
	{
		G->Count++;
		G->LastT = CurrentT;
		return;
	}
	FGroup N;
	N.Hash = Hash; N.Kind = Kind; N.Message = Category.ToString() + TEXT(": ") + Line;
	N.Count = 1; N.FirstT = N.LastT = CurrentT; N.bHasPos = bHasPos; N.Pos = LastPos;
	Map.Add(Hash, N);
	Order.Add(Hash);
}

void FPGLogCapture::SetContext(double InT, bool bInHasPos, const FVector& InPos)
{
	FScopeLock Lock(&Gate);
	CurrentT = InT; bHasPos = bInHasPos; LastPos = InPos;
}

TArray<FPGLogCapture::FGroup> FPGLogCapture::Groups() const
{
	FScopeLock Lock(&Gate);
	TArray<FGroup> Out;
	for (const FString& H : Order) Out.Add(Map[H]);
	return Out;
}

int32 FPGLogCapture::GroupCount() const
{
	FScopeLock Lock(&Gate);
	return Map.Num();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- run

FPlayGoblinRun::FPlayGoblinRun(const FPlayGoblinOptions& InOptions) : Options(InOptions), Rng(InOptions.Seed) {}

FPlayGoblinRun::~FPlayGoblinRun()
{
	if (State != EPGState::Ended && State != EPGState::Idle) End(TEXT("aborted"));
	if (Writer) { Writer->Close(); delete Writer; Writer = nullptr; }
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::Begin()
{
	StartedUtc = FDateTime::UtcNow();
	StartReal = WaitStartReal = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
	RunId = StartedUtc.ToString(TEXT("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")) + FString::Printf(TEXT("-s%d"), Options.Seed);
	const FString Root = Options.OutputRoot.IsEmpty() ? FPaths::Combine(FPaths::ProjectSavedDir(), TEXT("PlayGoblinRuns")) : Options.OutputRoot;
	RunDirectory = FPaths::ConvertRelativePathToFull(FPaths::Combine(Root, RunId));
	IFileManager::Get().MakeDirectory(*RunDirectory, true);
	EventsPath = FPaths::Combine(RunDirectory, TEXT("events.jsonl"));
	Writer = IFileManager::Get().CreateFileWriter(*EventsPath, FILEWRITE_AllowRead);

	Logs = MakeUnique<FPGLogCapture>();
	GLog->AddOutputDevice(Logs.Get());

	if (Options.bRecordFrames)
	{
		if (!FApp::CanEverRender()) RecorderOff = TEXT("no rendering device");
		else { bRecording = true; FramesDir = FPaths::Combine(RunDirectory, TEXT("frames")); }
	}
	else RecorderOff = TEXT("disabled in options");

	State = EPGState::Starting;
	Add(TEXT("run_start"), TEXT(""), nullptr, {
		{ TEXT("run_id"), RunId }, { TEXT("seed"), FString::FromInt(Options.Seed) },
		{ TEXT("minutes"), FString::SanitizeFloat(Options.Minutes) },
		{ TEXT("engine"), TEXT("unreal") }, { TEXT("unity"), TEXT("Unreal ") + FEngineVersion::Current().ToString(EVersionComponent::Patch) },
		{ TEXT("playgoblin"), TEXT("0.1.0-dev") } });
	Flush();
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::Add(const FString& Type, const FString& Detail, const FVector* Pos, TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Data)
{
	FScopeLock Lock(&EventGate);
	FPGEvent E;
	E.Seq = ++SeqCounter; E.T = CurrentT; E.Type = Type; E.Detail = Detail;
	if (Pos) { E.bHasPos = true; E.Pos = *Pos; }
	E.Data = MoveTemp(Data);
	if (Memory.Num() >= 20000) Memory.RemoveAt(0);
	Memory.Add(E);
	Pending.Add(E);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::Flush()
{
	if (!Writer) return;
	TArray<FPGEvent> Local;
	{
		FScopeLock Lock(&EventGate);
		Local = MoveTemp(Pending);
		Pending.Reset();
	}
	for (const FPGEvent& E : Local)
	{
		const FString Line = E.ToJson() + TEXT("\n");
		FTCHARToUTF8 Utf8(*Line);
		Writer->Serialize(const_cast<char*>(Utf8.Get()), Utf8.Length());
		Counts.FindOrAdd(E.Type)++;
		if (bRecording && (E.Type == TEXT("stuck") || E.Type == TEXT("out_of_world")))
			BurstUntilReal = FPlatformTime::Seconds() + Options.BurstSeconds;
	}
	Writer->Flush();
}

FPGSnapshot FPlayGoblinRun::Snapshot() const
{
	FPGSnapshot S;
	S.State = State; S.RunId = RunId; S.RunDir = RunDirectory; S.EndReason = EndReason;
	S.T = State == EPGState::Ended ? EndT : CurrentT;
	{
		FScopeLock Lock(&EventGate);
		S.Events = SeqCounter;
	}
	S.Stucks = Stucks; S.Falls = Falls;
	S.Errors = Logs ? Logs->GroupCount() : 0;
	S.Coverage = WalkableCell.Num() > 0 ? static_cast<float>(Visited.Num()) / WalkableCell.Num() : 0.f;
	return S;
}

FString FPlayGoblinRun::EventsJson(int64 Since, int32 Max) const
{
	FScopeLock Lock(&EventGate);
	FString Out = TEXT("{\"events\":[");
	int64 Next = Since;
	int32 N = 0;
	for (const FPGEvent& E : Memory)
	{
		if (E.Seq <= Since) continue;
		if (N >= Max) break;
		if (N++) Out += TEXT(",");
		Out += E.ToJson();
		Next = E.Seq;
	}
	return Out + FString::Printf(TEXT("],\"next\":%lld}"), Next);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::Tick(UWorld* World)
{
	if (State == EPGState::Ended || State == EPGState::Idle || !World) return;

	const double Now = World->GetTimeSeconds();
	if (!bTimeInit)
	{
		bTimeInit = true;
		StartWorldTime = Now;
		if (!FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Options.TimeScale, 1.f))
			if (AWorldSettings* WS = World->GetWorldSettings()) WS->SetTimeDilation(Options.TimeScale);
		MapName = World->GetMapName();
	}
	CurrentT = Now - StartWorldTime;
	const double Real = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
	const float FrameMs = static_cast<float>(FApp::GetDeltaTime() * 1000.0);
	MaxFrameMs = FMath::Max(MaxFrameMs, FrameMs); FrameMsSum += FrameMs; FrameCount++;

	APawn* Pawn = PawnPtr.Get();
	if (State == EPGState::Starting)
	{
		APlayerController* PC = World->GetFirstPlayerController();
		Pawn = PC ? PC->GetPawn() : nullptr;
		if (Pawn) OnPlayerFound(World, Pawn);
		else if (Real - WaitStartReal > Options.WaitForPlayerSeconds)
		{
			Add(TEXT("setup_error"), TEXT("No player pawn found. PlayGoblin needs a possessed Pawn (ideally an ACharacter) in the level."), nullptr);
			Flush();
			End(TEXT("no_player"));
			return;
		}
	}
	else if (!Pawn)
	{
		State = EPGState::Starting;
		WaitStartReal = Real;
		bHasDest = false;
		Add(TEXT("player_lost"), TEXT(""), nullptr);
	}

	// Frame-rate watch: skip the first seconds and the frame a screenshot was requested in.
	if (Options.FpsBudgetMs > 0.f && CurrentT >= IgnoreFpsUntil && GFrameCounter - LastCaptureFrameNo > 1)
	{
		if (FrameMs <= Options.FpsBudgetMs) { SlowSeconds = SlowMsSum = 0; SlowFrames = 0; }
		else
		{
			SlowSeconds += FrameMs / 1000.0; SlowMsSum += FrameMs; SlowFrames++;
			if (SlowSeconds >= 1.0 && CurrentT >= CooldownUntil)
			{
				const FVector P = Pawn ? Pawn->GetActorLocation() : FVector::ZeroVector;
				Add(TEXT("fps_drop"), TEXT(""), Pawn ? &P : nullptr, {
					{ TEXT("ms"), FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1f"), SlowMsSum / SlowFrames) },
					{ TEXT("seconds"), FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1f"), SlowSeconds) },
					{ TEXT("budget_ms"), FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1f"), Options.FpsBudgetMs) } });
				CooldownUntil = CurrentT + 5.0; SlowSeconds = SlowMsSum = 0; SlowFrames = 0; FpsEvents++;
			}
		}
	}
	LastRealNow = Real;

	if (State == EPGState::Running && Pawn)
	{
		Walk(World, Pawn, CurrentT);
		if (bRecording) CaptureFrame(CurrentT);
		Logs->SetContext(CurrentT, true, Pawn->GetActorLocation());
	}
	else if (Logs) Logs->SetContext(CurrentT, false, FVector::ZeroVector);

	if (Options.bMenus)
	{
		if (!Menus) Menus = MakeUnique<FPGMenuCrawler>([this](const FString& Type, const FString& Detail, TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Data) { Add(Type, Detail, nullptr, MoveTemp(Data)); });
		Menus->Tick(World);
	}

	Flush();
	if (CurrentT >= Options.Minutes * 60.0) End(TEXT("completed"));
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::OnPlayerFound(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn)
{
	PawnPtr = Pawn;
	if (const ACharacter* Ch = Cast<ACharacter>(Pawn))
	{
		if (const UCapsuleComponent* Cap = Ch->GetCapsuleComponent()) { HalfHeight = Cap->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight(); Radius = Cap->GetScaledCapsuleRadius(); }
		MoverKind = TEXT("CharacterMovement");
	}
	else MoverKind = TEXT("Pawn");

	UNavigationSystemV1* Nav = FNavigationSystem::GetCurrent<UNavigationSystemV1>(World);
	bUsesNav = Nav && Nav->GetDefaultNavDataInstance(FNavigationSystem::DontCreate) != nullptr;
	LastGood = Pawn->GetActorLocation();
	LastPos = LastGood; bHasLast = true;
	BuildGrid(World, Pawn);
	State = EPGState::Running;
	const FVector P = Pawn->GetActorLocation();
	Add(TEXT("player_found"), Pawn->GetName(), &P, {
		{ TEXT("mover"), MoverKind }, { TEXT("navmesh"), bUsesNav ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") },
		{ TEXT("reachable_cells"), FString::FromInt(Walkable.Num()) } });
	ScanPrototypeLook(World, Pawn);
	DiscoverInputs(Pawn);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::ScanPrototypeLook(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn)
{
	constexpr int32 MaxReported = 50;
	const UMaterial* DefaultMat = UMaterial::GetDefaultMaterial(MD_Surface);
	auto IsPlaceholderMaterial = [DefaultMat](const UMaterialInterface* M)
	{
		if (!M) return false;
		if (M == DefaultMat) return true;
		if (!M->GetPathName().StartsWith(TEXT("/Engine/"))) return false;
		const FString Name = M->GetName();
		return Name.Contains(TEXT("WorldGridMaterial")) || Name.Contains(TEXT("DefaultMaterial"))
			|| Name.Contains(TEXT("BasicShapeMaterial")) || Name.Contains(TEXT("Checker"));
	};

	struct FHit { FString Actor, Reason, Mesh, Material, Component; FVector Loc; };
	TArray<FHit> Hits;
	for (TActorIterator<AActor> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		AActor* A = *It;
		if (A == Pawn || Cast<APawn>(A) || A->IsHidden() || A->IsEditorOnly()) continue;
		TInlineComponentArray<UPrimitiveComponent*> Prims;
		A->GetComponents(Prims);
		for (UPrimitiveComponent* C : Prims)
		{
			if (!C || !C->IsVisible() || C->IsEditorOnly()) continue;
			const UStaticMeshComponent* SMC = Cast<UStaticMeshComponent>(C);
			const UStaticMesh* Mesh = SMC ? SMC->GetStaticMesh() : nullptr;
			const FString MeshPath = Mesh ? Mesh->GetPathName() : FString();
			const bool bBasic = MeshPath.StartsWith(TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/"));
			FString BadMat;
			for (int32 i = 0, N = C->GetNumMaterials(); i < N; ++i)
			{
				const UMaterialInterface* M = C->GetMaterial(i);
				if (IsPlaceholderMaterial(M)) { BadMat = M->GetPathName(); break; }
			}
			if (!bBasic && BadMat.IsEmpty()) continue;
			FHit H;
#if WITH_EDITOR
			H.Actor = A->GetActorLabel();
#endif
			if (H.Actor.IsEmpty()) H.Actor = A->GetName();
			H.Reason = bBasic ? TEXT("basic_shape") : TEXT("default_material");
			H.Mesh = MeshPath; H.Material = BadMat; H.Component = C->GetName(); H.Loc = A->GetActorLocation();
			Hits.Add(MoveTemp(H));
			break; // one event per actor
		}
	}
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Hits.Num() && i < MaxReported; ++i)
	{
		const FHit& H = Hits[i];
		Add(TEXT("prototype_look"), H.Actor, &H.Loc, { { TEXT("reason"), H.Reason }, { TEXT("mesh"), H.Mesh }, { TEXT("material"), H.Material },
			{ TEXT("component"), H.Component }, { TEXT("total"), FString::FromInt(Hits.Num()) } });
	}
	UE_LOG(LogPlayGoblin, Log, TEXT("Prototype look: %d actor(s) with placeholder meshes/materials (%d reported)"), Hits.Num(), FMath::Min(Hits.Num(), MaxReported));
}

int32 FPlayGoblinRun::CellIndex(const FVector& P) const
{
	const int32 X = FMath::FloorToInt((P.X - GridMin.X) / CellCm);
	const int32 Y = FMath::FloorToInt((P.Y - GridMin.Y) / CellCm);
	return (X < 0 || Y < 0 || X >= GridNx || Y >= GridNz) ? -1 : Y * GridNx + X;
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::BuildGrid(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn)
{
	FBox Bounds(ForceInit);
	for (TActorIterator<AActor> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		AActor* A = *It;
		if (A == Pawn || Cast<APawn>(A) || Cast<AWorldSettings>(A) || A->IsHidden()) continue;
		const FBox B = A->GetComponentsBoundingBox(false);
		if (B.IsValid && B.GetSize().GetMax() < 500000.f) Bounds += B;
	}
	const float LevelZ = Pawn->GetActorLocation().Z - HalfHeight;
	FallZ = LevelZ - 5000.f;
	if (!Bounds.IsValid) return;

	GridMin = Bounds.Min;
	GridNx = FMath::Max(1, FMath::CeilToInt(Bounds.GetSize().X / CellCm));
	GridNz = FMath::Max(1, FMath::CeilToInt(Bounds.GetSize().Y / CellCm));
	FCollisionQueryParams Q(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(PlayGoblinGrid), false, Pawn);
	const FCollisionShape Capsule = FCollisionShape::MakeCapsule(Radius * 0.9f, HalfHeight);
	float LowestGround = LevelZ;
	int32 RejNoHit = 0, RejSteep = 0, RejOverlap = 0;
	TArray<FString> IgnoredVolumes;
	for (int32 Y = 0; Y < GridNz; ++Y)
		for (int32 X = 0; X < GridNx; ++X)
		{
			const FVector C(GridMin.X + (X + 0.5f) * CellCm, GridMin.Y + (Y + 0.5f) * CellCm, 0.f);
			FHitResult Hit;
			// Start just above head height at the player's level, not above the whole level: a trace from the top of the
			// bounds lands on roofs, sky domes and ceilings and rejects every cell (found on the first real Unreal run).
			const FVector From(C.X, C.Y, LevelZ + Options.MaxClimbCm + 2.f * HalfHeight), To(C.X, C.Y, Bounds.Min.Z - 5000.f);
			// Trace with the channel the pawn's own movement collides with. ECC_Visibility also hits invisible helper volumes
			// and meshes that only block the camera, which the player walks straight through.
			bool bHit = false;
			// A trace that starts inside a blocking volume reports a hit at its own start point. A huge volume around the
			// player (found in the first real game: one actor spanning the whole level) would hide the ground in every cell,
			// so ignore actors the trace starts inside and trace again.
			for (int32 Try = 0; Try < 4; ++Try)
			{
				bHit = World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, From, To, ECC_Pawn, Q);
				if (!bHit || !Hit.bStartPenetrating || !Hit.GetActor()) break;
				IgnoredVolumes.AddUnique(Hit.GetActor()->GetName());
				Q.AddIgnoredActor(Hit.GetActor());
				bHit = false;
			}
			if (!bHit) { ++RejNoHit; continue; }
			if (Hit.ImpactNormal.Z < 0.7f || FMath::Abs(Hit.ImpactPoint.Z - LevelZ) > Options.MaxClimbCm) { ++RejSteep; continue; }
			const FVector Stand = Hit.ImpactPoint + FVector(0, 0, HalfHeight + 5.f);
			if (World->OverlapBlockingTestByChannel(Stand, FQuat::Identity, ECC_Pawn, Capsule, Q)) { ++RejOverlap; continue; }
			Walkable.Add(Hit.ImpactPoint);
			WalkableCell.Add(Y * GridNx + X);
			LowestGround = FMath::Min(LowestGround, Hit.ImpactPoint.Z);
		}
	FallZ = LowestGround - 1000.f;
	for (const FString& N : IgnoredVolumes) UE_LOG(LogTemp, Display, TEXT("PlayGoblin grid: ignored actor that encloses the player's level: %s"), *N);
	UE_LOG(LogTemp, Display, TEXT("PlayGoblin grid: %dx%d cells, walkable=%d, rejected: no-hit=%d steep-or-far=%d blocked=%d, LevelZ=%.0f, bounds=%s"),
		GridNx, GridNz, Walkable.Num(), RejNoHit, RejSteep, RejOverlap, LevelZ, *Bounds.ToString());
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::PickDestination(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn)
{
	Path.Reset(); PathIndex = 0; bHasDest = false;
	const FVector From = Pawn->GetActorLocation();
	FVector Best = FVector::ZeroVector;
	bool bFound = false;

	if (bUsesNav)
	{
		if (UNavigationSystemV1* Nav = FNavigationSystem::GetCurrent<UNavigationSystemV1>(World))
		{
			for (int32 Try = 0; Try < 8 && !bFound; ++Try)
			{
				FNavLocation L;
				if (!Nav->GetRandomReachablePointInRadius(From, 6000.f, L)) continue;
				if (FVector::Dist2D(L.Location, From) < 400.f) continue;
				if (UNavigationPath* P = UNavigationSystemV1::FindPathToLocationSynchronously(World, From, L.Location, Pawn))
					if (P->IsValid() && !P->IsPartial() && P->PathPoints.Num() > 1) { Path = P->PathPoints; Best = L.Location; bFound = true; }
			}
		}
	}
	if (!bFound && Walkable.Num() > 0)
	{
		// Prefer cells not visited yet.
		for (int32 Try = 0; Try < 8; ++Try)
		{
			const int32 I = Rng.RandRange(0, Walkable.Num() - 1);
			if (FVector::Dist2D(Walkable[I], From) < 400.f) continue;
			Best = Walkable[I]; bFound = true;
			if (!Visited.Contains(WalkableCell[I])) break;
		}
	}
	if (!bFound) return;
	Dest = Best; bHasDest = true; DestSince = CurrentT;
	if (Path.Num() == 0) { Path.Add(Dest); }
	PathIndex = bUsesNav && Path.Num() > 1 ? 1 : 0;
}

bool FPlayGoblinRun::FeedStuck(double T, const FVector& P, bool bTrying)
{
	if (!bTrying) { StuckSamples.Reset(); return false; }
	StuckSamples.Add({ T, P });
	while (StuckSamples.Num() > 0 && T - StuckSamples[0].Key > Options.StuckSeconds) StuckSamples.RemoveAt(0);
	if (StuckSamples.Num() < 2 || T - StuckSamples[0].Key < Options.StuckSeconds * 0.9) return false;
	FVector2D Min(MAX_flt, MAX_flt), Max(-MAX_flt, -MAX_flt);
	for (const auto& S : StuckSamples)
	{
		Min.X = FMath::Min(Min.X, S.Value.X); Min.Y = FMath::Min(Min.Y, S.Value.Y);
		Max.X = FMath::Max(Max.X, S.Value.X); Max.Y = FMath::Max(Max.Y, S.Value.Y);
	}
	if (FMath::Max(Max.X - Min.X, Max.Y - Min.Y) < Options.StuckDistanceCm) { StuckSamples.Reset(); return true; }
	return false;
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::Walk(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn, double T)
{
	const FVector Loc = Pawn->GetActorLocation();
	if (bHasLast) DistanceCm += FVector::Dist2D(Loc, LastPos);
	LastPos = Loc; bHasLast = true;
	const int32 Cell = CellIndex(Loc);
	if (Cell >= 0) Visited.Add(Cell);

	const UCharacterMovementComponent* CMC = Pawn->FindComponentByClass<UCharacterMovementComponent>();
	const bool bGrounded = !CMC || CMC->IsMovingOnGround();

	if (T >= NextPos)
	{
		NextPos = T + 1.0 / FMath::Max(0.1f, Options.PositionHz);
		Add(TEXT("pos"), TEXT(""), &Loc);
	}
	if (bGrounded && T >= NextGood) { NextGood = T + 1.0; LastGood = Loc; }

	if (Loc.Z < FallZ)
	{
		Falls++;
		Add(TEXT("out_of_world"), TEXT("below_world"), &Loc, { { TEXT("reason"), TEXT("below_world") }, { TEXT("fell_to"), PlayGoblin::MetresCsv(Loc) } });
		const FVector To = LastGood + FVector(0, 0, HalfHeight + 20.f);
		Pawn->SetActorLocation(To, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);
		if (UCharacterMovementComponent* M = Pawn->FindComponentByClass<UCharacterMovementComponent>()) { M->StopMovementImmediately(); M->SetMovementMode(MOVE_Falling); }
		Respawns++;
		Add(TEXT("respawn"), TEXT(""), &To, { { TEXT("from"), PlayGoblin::MetresCsv(Loc) } });
		StuckSamples.Reset(); bHasDest = false; bHasLast = false;
		return;
	}

	if (!bHasDest || T - DestSince > 40.0)
	{
		if (bHasDest)
		{
			APlayerController* PCDiag = Cast<APlayerController>(Pawn->GetController());
			Add(TEXT("waypoint_timeout"), TEXT(""), &Loc, {
				{ TEXT("mode"), CMC ? StaticEnum<EMovementMode>()->GetNameStringByValue(CMC->MovementMode) : TEXT("none") },
				{ TEXT("move_ignored"), PCDiag && PCDiag->IsMoveInputIgnored() ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") },
				{ TEXT("has_controller"), Pawn->GetController() ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") },
				{ TEXT("max_speed"), FString::SanitizeFloat(CMC ? CMC->MaxWalkSpeed : 0.f) },
				{ TEXT("velocity_cm_s"), FString::SanitizeFloat(Pawn->GetVelocity().Size()) },
				{ TEXT("floor_hit"), CMC && CMC->CurrentFloor.bBlockingHit ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") },
				{ TEXT("floor_actor"), CMC && CMC->CurrentFloor.HitResult.GetActor() ? CMC->CurrentFloor.HitResult.GetActor()->GetName() : TEXT("") },
				{ TEXT("gravity_scale"), FString::SanitizeFloat(CMC ? CMC->GravityScale : 0.f) },
				{ TEXT("custom_time_dilation"), FString::SanitizeFloat(Pawn->CustomTimeDilation) },
				{ TEXT("dest"), PlayGoblin::MetresCsv(Dest) } });
		}
		PickDestination(World, Pawn);
		if (!bHasDest) return;
	}

	const FVector Target = Path.IsValidIndex(PathIndex) ? Path[PathIndex] : Dest;
	FVector Dir = Target - Loc; Dir.Z = 0;
	const bool bFinal = PathIndex >= Path.Num() - 1;
	if (Dir.Size() < (bFinal ? 150.f : 120.f))
	{
		if (bKeyDown)
		{
			if (APlayerController* KeyPC = Cast<APlayerController>(Pawn->GetController()))
				KeyPC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(ForwardKeys.Num() > 0 ? ForwardKeys[0] : EKeys::W, IE_Released, 0.f, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
			bKeyDown = false;
		}
		if (bFinal)
		{
			Waypoints++;
			Add(TEXT("waypoint"), TEXT(""), &Dest);
			bHasDest = false;
		}
		else PathIndex++;
		return;
	}
	Dir.Normalize();
	if (Options.bUseKeys)
	{
		if (APlayerController* KeyPC = Cast<APlayerController>(Pawn->GetController()))
		{
			HumanLook(KeyPC, Dir.Rotation().Yaw);
			// Walk only roughly facing the target, like a person who turns, then goes.
			const float Err = FMath::Abs(FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Dir.Rotation().Yaw - KeyPC->GetControlRotation().Yaw));
			const FKey Fwd = ForwardKeys.Num() > 0 ? ForwardKeys[0] : EKeys::W;
			if (Err < 50.f)
			{
				KeyPC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(Fwd, bKeyDown ? IE_Repeat : IE_Pressed, 1.f, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
				bKeyDown = true;
			}
			else if (bKeyDown)
			{
				KeyPC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(Fwd, IE_Released, 0.f, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
				bKeyDown = false;
			}
			UpdateKeyPresses(KeyPC, T);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Pawn->AddMovementInput(Dir, 1.f);
	}

	if (bGrounded && FeedStuck(T, Loc, true))
	{
		Stucks++;
		FString Blocked;
		FHitResult Hit;
		FCollisionQueryParams Q(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(PlayGoblinStuck), false, Pawn);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Loc, Loc + Dir * 200.f, ECC_Visibility, Q) && Hit.GetActor())
			Blocked = Hit.GetActor()->GetName();
		if (Options.bUseKeys && JumpKey.IsValid())
			if (APlayerController* HopPC = Cast<APlayerController>(Pawn->GetController())) PressKey(HopPC, JumpKey, T); // a person tries hopping over it
		Add(TEXT("stuck"), FString::Printf(TEXT("no progress for %.0fs"), Options.StuckSeconds), &Loc, {
			{ TEXT("blocked_by"), Blocked },
			{ TEXT("controller"), CMC ? StaticEnum<EMovementMode>()->GetNameStringByValue(CMC->MovementMode) : TEXT("") } });
		bHasDest = false;
	}
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::CaptureFrame(double T)
{
	const double Real = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
	if (Real < NextFrameReal) return;
	const float Hz = Real < BurstUntilReal ? Options.BurstHz : Options.FrameHz;
	NextFrameReal = Real + 1.0 / FMath::Max(0.1f, Hz);
	if (FrameIndex >= 3000) return;
	IFileManager::Get().MakeDirectory(*FramesDir, true);
	const FString Name = FString::Printf(TEXT("f%05d_t%.2f.png"), FrameIndex++, T);
	LastCaptureFrameNo = GFrameCounter;
	FScreenshotRequest::RequestScreenshot(FPaths::Combine(FramesDir, Name), false, false);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::End(const FString& Reason)
{
	if (State == EPGState::Ended || State == EPGState::Idle) return;
	EndReason = Reason;
	EndT = CurrentT;
	TArray<FPGLogCapture::FGroup> Groups;
	if (Logs)
	{
		Groups = Logs->Groups();
		for (const FPGLogCapture::FGroup& G : Groups)
		{
			Add(TEXT("error_summary"), G.Message, G.bHasPos ? &G.Pos : nullptr, {
				{ TEXT("hash"), G.Hash }, { TEXT("kind"), G.Kind }, { TEXT("count"), FString::FromInt(G.Count) },
				{ TEXT("first_t"), FString::Printf(TEXT("%.3f"), G.FirstT) }, { TEXT("last_t"), FString::Printf(TEXT("%.3f"), G.LastT) } });
		}
	}
	Add(TEXT("run_end"), Reason, nullptr);
	Flush();
	if (Logs) { GLog->RemoveOutputDevice(Logs.Get()); }
	WriteSummary(Groups);
	if (Writer) { Writer->Close(); delete Writer; Writer = nullptr; }
	State = EPGState::Ended;
	UE_LOG(LogPlayGoblin, Display, TEXT("Run %s ended (%s). Folder: %s"), *RunId, *Reason, *RunDirectory);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::WriteSummary(const TArray<FPGLogCapture::FGroup>& Groups)
{
	using namespace PlayGoblin;
	const float Cov = WalkableCell.Num() > 0 ? static_cast<float>(Visited.Num()) / WalkableCell.Num() : 0.f;
	FString J = TEXT("{");
	auto S = [&](const TCHAR* K, const FString& V) { J += FString::Printf(TEXT("\"%s\":\"%s\","), K, *JsonEscape(V)); };
	auto N = [&](const TCHAR* K, double V) { J += FString::Printf(TEXT("\"%s\":%s,"), K, *Num(V)); };
	S(TEXT("run_id"), RunId); S(TEXT("playgoblin"), TEXT("0.1.0-dev")); S(TEXT("engine"), TEXT("unreal"));
	S(TEXT("engine_version"), TEXT("Unreal ") + FEngineVersion::Current().ToString(EVersionComponent::Patch));
	S(TEXT("platform"), FPlatformProperties::IniPlatformName()); S(TEXT("scene"), MapName);
	N(TEXT("seed"), Options.Seed); N(TEXT("planned_minutes"), Options.Minutes);
	S(TEXT("started_utc"), StartedUtc.ToIso8601()); S(TEXT("end_reason"), EndReason);
	N(TEXT("game_seconds"), EndT); N(TEXT("real_seconds"), FPlatformTime::Seconds() - StartReal);
	N(TEXT("time_scale"), Options.TimeScale);
	S(TEXT("mover"), MoverKind);
	J += FString::Printf(TEXT("\"navmesh\":%s,\"grid\":%s,"), bUsesNav ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false"), Walkable.Num() > 0 ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false"));
	N(TEXT("walkable_cells"), WalkableCell.Num()); N(TEXT("reachable_cells"), Walkable.Num());
	N(TEXT("coverage"), Cov); N(TEXT("distance_m"), DistanceCm / 100.0);
	N(TEXT("stuck"), Stucks); N(TEXT("out_of_world"), Falls); N(TEXT("respawns"), Respawns); N(TEXT("waypoints"), Waypoints);
	N(TEXT("recorded_frames"), FrameIndex);
	if (!RecorderOff.IsEmpty()) S(TEXT("recorder_off"), RecorderOff);
	J += FString::Printf(TEXT("\"frames\":{\"frames\":%lld,\"avg_ms\":%s,\"max_ms\":%s},"), FrameCount,
		*Num(FrameCount ? FrameMsSum / FrameCount : 0.0), *Num(MaxFrameMs));
	J += TEXT("\"event_counts\":{");
	bool bFirst = true;
	for (const auto& KV : Counts) { J += FString::Printf(TEXT("%s\"%s\":%d"), bFirst ? TEXT("") : TEXT(","), *JsonEscape(KV.Key), KV.Value); bFirst = false; }
	J += TEXT("},\"errors\":[");
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Groups.Num(); ++i)
	{
		const FPGLogCapture::FGroup& G = Groups[i];
		J += FString::Printf(TEXT("%s{\"hash\":\"%s\",\"kind\":\"%s\",\"message\":\"%s\",\"count\":%d,\"first_t\":%s,\"last_t\":%s}"),
			i ? TEXT(",") : TEXT(""), *G.Hash, *G.Kind, *JsonEscape(G.Message), G.Count, *Num(G.FirstT), *Num(G.LastT));
	}
	J += TEXT("]}");
	FFileHelper::SaveStringToFile(J, *FPaths::Combine(RunDirectory, TEXT("run.json")), FFileHelper::EEncodingOptions::ForceUTF8WithoutBOM);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::DiscoverInputs(APawn* Pawn)
{
	APlayerController* PC = Cast<APlayerController>(Pawn->GetController());
	UPlayerInput* PlayerIn = PC ? PC->PlayerInput.Get() : nullptr;
	TArray<UInputComponent*> Comps;
	if (Pawn->InputComponent) Comps.Add(Pawn->InputComponent);
	if (PC && PC->InputComponent) Comps.Add(PC->InputComponent);

	TArray<FString> Axes;
	bool bEnhanced = false;
	for (UInputComponent* C : Comps)
	{
		if (C->GetClass()->GetName().Contains(TEXT("Enhanced"))) bEnhanced = true;
		for (const FInputKeyBinding& KB : C->KeyBindings)
		{
			const FKey K = KB.Chord.Key;
			if (KB.KeyEvent != IE_Pressed || !K.IsValid() || K == EKeys::Escape) continue;
			if (K == EKeys::SpaceBar || (!JumpKey.IsValid() && K == EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom)) JumpKey = K;
			if (!K.IsGamepadKey()) ActionKeys.AddUnique(K);
		}
		for (const FInputAxisBinding& AB : C->AxisBindings)
		{
			Axes.AddUnique(AB.AxisName.ToString());
			if (!PlayerIn) continue;
			const FString Name = AB.AxisName.ToString();
			const bool bForwardAxis = Name.Contains(TEXT("Forward")) || Name.Contains(TEXT("Vertical")) || Name.Contains(TEXT("MoveY"));
			for (const FInputAxisKeyMapping& M : PlayerIn->GetKeysForAxis(AB.AxisName))
			{
				if (M.Key == EKeys::MouseX) bMouseLookBound = true;
				if (bForwardAxis && M.Scale > 0.f && !M.Key.IsAnalog() && !M.Key.IsGamepadKey()) ForwardKeys.AddUnique(M.Key);
			}
		}
		for (const FInputAxisKeyBinding& AKB : C->AxisKeyBindings)
			if (AKB.AxisKey == EKeys::MouseX) bMouseLookBound = true;
	}
	ForwardKeys.Sort([](const FKey& A, const FKey& B) { return A == EKeys::W; }); // prefer W when several keys move forward

	auto Join = [](const TArray<FKey>& Ks) { TArray<FString> S; for (const FKey& K : Ks) S.Add(K.ToString()); return FString::Join(S, TEXT(",")); };
	const FVector P = Pawn->GetActorLocation();
	Add(TEXT("input_map"), TEXT(""), &P, {
		{ TEXT("forward_keys"), Join(ForwardKeys) }, { TEXT("action_keys"), Join(ActionKeys) },
		{ TEXT("jump_key"), JumpKey.IsValid() ? JumpKey.ToString() : TEXT("") },
		{ TEXT("mouse_look"), bMouseLookBound ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") },
		{ TEXT("axes"), FString::Join(Axes, TEXT(",")) }, { TEXT("enhanced_input"), bEnhanced ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false") } });
	if (!bEnhanced)
	{
		if (ForwardKeys.Num() == 0)
			Add(TEXT("dead_input"), TEXT("No keyboard key moves the player forward"), &P, { { TEXT("input"), TEXT("move") }, { TEXT("axes"), FString::Join(Axes, TEXT(",")) } });
		if (!bMouseLookBound)
			Add(TEXT("dead_input"), TEXT("Moving the mouse does not turn the camera (no MouseX binding)"), &P, { { TEXT("input"), TEXT("look") } });
	}
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::HumanLook(APlayerController* PC, float DesiredYaw)
{
	const float Yaw = PC->GetControlRotation().Yaw;
	const float Err = FRotator::NormalizeAxis(DesiredYaw - Yaw);
	if (!bMouseLookBound || !bMouseLookWorks)
	{
		FRotator R = PC->GetControlRotation(); R.Yaw = Yaw + FMath::Clamp(Err, -12.f, 12.f); // turn smoothly, not in one snap
		PC->SetControlRotation(R);
		return;
	}
	if (FMath::Abs(Err) < 2.f) return;
	// Mouse counts: proportional, clamped like a wrist flick. The game's own sensitivity decides how far that turns.
	const float MouseCounts = FMath::Clamp(Err * 1.5f, -40.f, 40.f);
	if (!bMouseLookChecked)
	{
		if (!bMouseProbeStarted) { bMouseProbeStarted = true; MouseProbeYaw = Yaw; }
		MouseProbeSent += FMath::Abs(MouseCounts);
		if (MouseProbeSent > 400.f)
		{
			bMouseLookChecked = true;
			if (FMath::Abs(FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Yaw - MouseProbeYaw)) < 0.5f)
			{
				bMouseLookWorks = false;
				const FVector P = PC->GetPawn() ? PC->GetPawn()->GetActorLocation() : FVector::ZeroVector;
				Add(TEXT("dead_input"), TEXT("Moving the mouse does not turn the camera"), &P, { { TEXT("input"), TEXT("look") }, { TEXT("mouse_counts_sent"), FString::SanitizeFloat(MouseProbeSent) } });
				return;
			}
		}
	}
	PC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(EKeys::MouseX, IE_Axis, MouseCounts, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::PressKey(APlayerController* PC, const FKey& Key, double T)
{
	if (ReleaseAt >= 0.0 || !Key.IsValid()) return; // one tap at a time
	PC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(Key, IE_Pressed, 1.f, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
	PressedKey = Key; ReleaseAt = T + 0.15; KeyPresses++;
	const FVector P = PC->GetPawn() ? PC->GetPawn()->GetActorLocation() : FVector::ZeroVector;
	Add(TEXT("input_press"), Key.ToString(), &P);
}

void FPlayGoblinRun::UpdateKeyPresses(APlayerController* PC, double T)
{
	if (ReleaseAt >= 0.0 && T >= ReleaseAt)
	{
		PC->InputKey(FInputKeyEventArgs::CreateSimulated(PressedKey, IE_Released, 0.f, 1, FInputDeviceId::CreateFromInternalId(0)));
		ReleaseAt = -1.0;
	}
	if (T >= NextActionAt && ActionKeys.Num() > 0)
	{
		PressKey(PC, ActionKeys[Rng.RandRange(0, ActionKeys.Num() - 1)], T);
		NextActionAt = T + Rng.FRandRange(2.5f, 6.0f);
	}
}

```

`unreal/PlayGoblin/Source/PlayGoblin/Private/PlayGoblinRun.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "HAL/CriticalSection.h"
#include "Misc/OutputDevice.h"
#include "Math/RandomStream.h"
#include "PlayGoblinEvents.h"

class APawn;
class UWorld;

struct FPlayGoblinOptions
{
	int32 Seed = 42;
	float Minutes = 10.f;
	/** Seconds without progress before the player counts as stuck. */
	float StuckSeconds = 5.f;
	/** Horizontal movement (cm) below which, over StuckSeconds, the player counts as stuck. */
	float StuckDistanceCm = 50.f;
	float PositionHz = 2.f;
	float WaitForPlayerSeconds = 10.f;
	/** Only walk to ground at most this far (cm) above or below the level the player starts on. */
	float MaxClimbCm = 150.f;
	FString OutputRoot;
	bool bRecordFrames = true;
	float FrameHz = 2.f;
	float BurstHz = 10.f;
	float BurstSeconds = 3.f;
	float FpsBudgetMs = 0.f;
	float TimeScale = 1.f;
	bool bExitWhenDone = false;
	/** Drive the pawn with simulated key presses (W + control yaw) instead of AddMovementInput, so a missing or broken input binding shows up as a bot that cannot move. */
	bool bUseKeys = false;
	/** Crawl visible UMG menus (dead buttons, placeholder text). On by default; -PlayGoblinMenus=0 turns it off. */
	bool bMenus = true;
};

enum class EPGState : uint8 { Idle, Starting, Running, Ended };

struct FPGSnapshot
{
	EPGState State = EPGState::Idle;
	FString RunId;
	FString RunDir;
	FString EndReason;
	double T = 0.0;
	int64 Events = 0;
	int32 Stucks = 0;
	int32 Falls = 0;
	int32 Errors = 0;
	float Coverage = 0.f;
};

/** Groups identical error logs (by message, ignoring digits) like the Unity LogProbe does. */
class FPGLogCapture : public FOutputDevice
{
public:
	struct FGroup
	{
		FString Hash, Kind, Message;
		int32 Count = 0;
		double FirstT = 0, LastT = 0;
		bool bHasPos = false;
		FVector Pos = FVector::ZeroVector;
	};

	virtual void Serialize(const TCHAR* V, ELogVerbosity::Type Verbosity, const FName& Category) override;
	virtual bool CanBeUsedOnMultipleThreads() const override { return true; }

	void SetContext(double InT, bool bInHasPos, const FVector& InPos);
	TArray<FGroup> Groups() const;
	int32 GroupCount() const;

private:
	mutable FCriticalSection Gate;
	TMap<FString, FGroup> Map;
	TArray<FString> Order;
	double CurrentT = 0;
	bool bHasPos = false;
	FVector LastPos = FVector::ZeroVector;
};

/** One run: finds the player pawn, walks, detects problems and writes events.jsonl plus run.json. */
class FPlayGoblinRun
{
public:
	explicit FPlayGoblinRun(const FPlayGoblinOptions& InOptions);
	~FPlayGoblinRun();

	void Begin();
	void Tick(UWorld* World);
	void Stop() { End(TEXT("stopped")); }

	EPGState GetState() const { return State; }
	FPGSnapshot Snapshot() const;
	FString EventsJson(int64 Since, int32 Max) const;
	FString RunId, RunDirectory, EndReason;
	bool bExitWhenDone() const { return Options.bExitWhenDone; }

private:
	void Add(const FString& Type, const FString& Detail, const FVector* Pos, TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Data = {});
	void Flush();
	void End(const FString& Reason);
	void WriteSummary(const TArray<FPGLogCapture::FGroup>& Groups);

	void OnPlayerFound(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn);
	void BuildGrid(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn);
	/** Reports visible actors that still look like a prototype (engine default/grid material, /Engine/BasicShapes mesh) as prototype_look events. */
	void ScanPrototypeLook(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn);
	void PickDestination(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn);
	void Walk(UWorld* World, APawn* Pawn, double T);
	/** Reads the game's real bindings (legacy key/axis bindings on the pawn and controller input components). */
	void DiscoverInputs(APawn* Pawn);
	/** Turns toward a yaw with simulated mouse movement, like a person would. Falls back to SetControlRotation if the mouse does nothing. */
	void HumanLook(APlayerController* PC, float DesiredYaw);
	void PressKey(APlayerController* PC, const FKey& Key, double T);
	void UpdateKeyPresses(APlayerController* PC, double T);
	bool FeedStuck(double T, const FVector& P, bool bTrying);
	void CaptureFrame(double T);
	int32 CellIndex(const FVector& P) const;

	FPlayGoblinOptions Options;
	TUniquePtr<class FPGMenuCrawler> Menus;
	EPGState State = EPGState::Idle;
	FRandomStream Rng;
	FString EventsPath;
	FArchive* Writer = nullptr;
	TUniquePtr<FPGLogCapture> Logs;

	mutable FCriticalSection EventGate;
	TArray<FPGEvent> Memory;
	TArray<FPGEvent> Pending;
	int64 SeqCounter = 0;
	TMap<FString, int32> Counts;

	bool bTimeInit = false;
	double StartWorldTime = 0, CurrentT = 0, EndT = 0, NextPos = 0, NextGood = 0;
	double StartReal = 0, WaitStartReal = 0, LastRealNow = 0;
	FDateTime StartedUtc;
	FString MapName, MoverKind;
	TWeakObjectPtr<APawn> PawnPtr;

	// Walking
	bool bUsesNav = false;
	TArray<FVector> Path;
	int32 PathIndex = 0;
	FVector Dest = FVector::ZeroVector;
	bool bKeyDown = false;
	TArray<FKey> ForwardKeys;
	TArray<FKey> ActionKeys;
	FKey JumpKey;
	bool bMouseLookBound = false;
	bool bMouseLookWorks = true;
	bool bMouseLookChecked = false;
	float MouseProbeSent = 0.f;
	float MouseProbeYaw = 0.f;
	bool bMouseProbeStarted = false;
	FKey PressedKey;
	double ReleaseAt = -1.0;
	double NextActionAt = 3.0;
	int32 KeyPresses = 0;
	bool bHasDest = false;
	double DestSince = 0;
	int32 Waypoints = 0, Stucks = 0, Falls = 0, Respawns = 0;
	double DistanceCm = 0;
	FVector LastPos = FVector::ZeroVector, LastGood = FVector::ZeroVector;
	bool bHasLast = false;
	float FallZ = 0.f, HalfHeight = 88.f, Radius = 34.f;

	// Stuck window
	TArray<TPair<double, FVector>> StuckSamples;

	// Coverage grid
	TArray<FVector> Walkable;
	TArray<int32> WalkableCell;
	TSet<int32> Visited;
	FVector GridMin = FVector::ZeroVector;
	int32 GridNx = 0, GridNz = 0;
	static constexpr float CellCm = 300.f;

	// Frames
	FString FramesDir;
	bool bRecording = false;
	FString RecorderOff;
	int32 FrameIndex = 0;
	double NextFrameReal = 0, BurstUntilReal = 0;
	uint64 LastCaptureFrameNo = 0;

	// Frame-rate watch
	double SlowSeconds = 0, SlowMsSum = 0, CooldownUntil = 0;
	int32 SlowFrames = 0;
	double IgnoreFpsUntil = 5.0;
	int32 FpsEvents = 0;
	float MaxFrameMs = 0, FrameMsSum = 0;
	int64 FrameCount = 0;
};

```

`unreal/README.md`:

```md
# PlayGoblin for Unreal Engine (experimental)

> **Status: compiles (0 errors, 0 warnings; UE 5.8.3, Win64 Development Editor) and is verified on its own sample (2026-11-02).** `scripts/verify_unreal.py` (UnrealEditor-Cmd, seed 1, 150 s, `-nullrhi`) finds all five planted bugs in `samples/unreal-sandbox` (trapdoor fall, 70 cm step stuck, error after first fall, dead UMG "Options" button, "TODO: title" menu text) and reports 0 findings on the `-PGClean` level; the same result with `--game-arg=-PlayGoblinKeys` (real input). One seed and one machine only: not yet verified on a real game, and frame capture (rendered mode) was not re-run. Please open an issue with what happened if you try it.

A C++ runtime plugin that walks a game and writes the same run folder as the Unity package ([docs/PROTOCOL.md](../docs/PROTOCOL.md)),
so `playgoblin report`, `playgoblin file-issues` and the MCP server work unchanged.

## What it does

- Finds the possessed pawn of player 0 (best with an `ACharacter`), picks random walkable destinations and moves with `AddMovementInput`.
- Uses the NavMesh when the level has one (`UNavigationSystemV1`), otherwise a floor grid sampled with line traces.
- Detects stuck spots (no horizontal progress for 5 s), falls below the level, and respawns the player at the last safe spot.
- Prototype look: at run start it lists visible actors that use the engine default/grid material (`WorldGridMaterial`, `DefaultMaterial`, `BasicShapeMaterial`, `Checker` under `/Engine/`) or a mesh from `/Engine/BasicShapes/`, as `prototype_look` events (max 50 actors, one event each; `data.total` has the full count). The CLI turns each into a medium finding. It checks only static assigned materials and meshes, so art that is a placeholder in other ways (grey custom materials, UI) is not seen. The generated sandbox is all engine cubes, so it reports 6 on both levels; `verify_unreal.py` prints that count and does not count it as a miss or false positive.
- Captures `Error` logs (and `LogScript` warnings, as exceptions), grouped by message.
- Optional frame capture through `FScreenshotRequest` (not with `-nullrhi`), and frame-rate drops with a budget.
- Local HTTP API (same routes as Unity) on 127.0.0.1:47321 for `playgoblin run`.

- Menu crawling (UMG, `-PlayGoblinMenus=0` turns it off): about every 2 s it finds visible `UUserWidget`s in the viewport, reports visible `UTextBlock`s with TODO/TBD/lorem/placeholder/FIXME (whole word) or the default text "Text Block"/"Button" as `placeholder_text`, and clicks each enabled `UButton` once (max 30 per run, top to bottom by widget name). A button is `dead_button` if, within 1 s, the viewport widget set, any widget visibility/enabled state, the world/map, pause state and cursor/input mode all stayed the same. Buttons labelled Quit/Exit are never clicked (`menu_skip`). Limits: clicks are simulated with `UButton::OnClicked.Broadcast()`, so Slate hit testing, hover/press styles and the input stack are not tested; only UMG (not raw Slate, CommonUI-only or Enhanced-Input-driven menus); a button that only changes text, plays a sound or sets game state is reported dead; a click that closes a menu hides the buttons queued behind it that scan (they are picked up only if the menu comes back); buttons are clicked once each by widget class and name.

Not implemented yet: blank/pink frame checks on the engine side (the CLI does the frame checks), a hook for custom start flows.
Positions are written as metres with Y up (`[x, z, y]` of Unreal's cm values) so the CLI reads them like Unity runs.

## Use it

1. Copy `PlayGoblin/` into your project's `Plugins/` folder (or add `unreal/` to `AdditionalPluginDirectories` in the `.uproject`) and enable the plugin.
2. Nothing runs unless you launch with `-playgoblin`, so shipping with the plugin enabled is harmless.

```bash
# the plugin starts a run by itself and quits when it is done
UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe MyGame.uproject -game -playgoblin -PlayGoblinSeconds=300 -PlayGoblinSeed=1 -PlayGoblinOut=D:/runs -PlayGoblinTimeScale=3 -PlayGoblinExit
playgoblin report D:/runs/<run id>

# or leave the game open and drive it from the CLI / an AI agent
UnrealEditor.exe MyGame.uproject -game -playgoblin
playgoblin run --minutes 10
```

| Flag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `-playgoblin` | Enable the plugin and open the local API (`-PlayGoblinPort=` to change the port) |
| `-PlayGoblinSeconds=N` | Start a run by itself, N game seconds long |
| `-PlayGoblinMenus=0` | Do not crawl UMG menus (on by default) |
| `-PlayGoblinSeed=`, `-PlayGoblinOut=`, `-PlayGoblinTimeScale=`, `-PlayGoblinFpsBudgetMs=`, `-PlayGoblinExit` | Seed, output folder, game speed, frame budget, quit afterwards |

**Security note:** the API is control-only (start/stop a run, read events) but Unreal's HTTP server may listen on all interfaces. Only use `-playgoblin` on a trusted network.

## Build notes (Windows)

- Close Unreal Editor first. UnrealBuildTool refuses to build while an editor with Live Coding is open.
- Keep the project in a **short path** (for example `C:\pg`). Unreal fails on paths over 260 characters.
- `samples/unreal-sandbox` is a minimal C++ project with a generated level (a trapdoor, a 70 cm step the character cannot climb, an error after the first fall) and a `-PGClean` mode with none of those:

```bash
Build.bat PlayGoblinSandboxEditor Win64 Development -Project=C:/pg/samples/unreal-sandbox/PlayGoblinSandbox.uproject -WaitMutex
python scripts/verify_unreal.py --editor "C:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8/Engine/Binaries/Win64/UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe"
```

## Known issues (first real run, 2026-11-02)
- The floor grid used to trace from the top of the level on `ECC_Visibility` and rejected every cell in a game with roof/sky meshes. It now traces on `ECC_Pawn` from head height and skips actors the trace starts inside.
- In the first game tested the pawn stayed in `MOVE_Falling` with zero velocity and no floor at spawn, so the bot never moved. `waypoint_timeout` events now carry movement diagnostics. Whether this is a game bug (player embedded in collision) or a plugin issue is not yet resolved.
- Launching with `UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe` while the project enables Epic's experimental toolset plugins logs ~47 Python `Error` lines at t=0; they are reported as findings. These are false positives and need a filter (planned).


```
